remember that through them you were born, and what will you repay them as they gave to you?

**Sirach 7:29**

With all your soul fear the Lord, and marvel at his priests.

**Sirach 7:30**

With all strength love the one who made you, and do not abandon his ministers.

**Sirach 7:31**

Fear the Lord and glorify a priest, and give him the portion, as it has been commanded to you, firstfruit and for trespass and gift of shoulders and sacrifice of sanctification and firstfruit of holy things.

**Sirach 7:32**

And stretch out your hand to a poor man, so that your blessing may be completed.

**Sirach 7:33**

Grace of gift is before every living one, and over a dead man do not hinder grace.

**Sirach 7:34**

Do not be lacking from weeping ones, and mourn with mourning ones.

**Sirach 7:35**

Do not hesitate to visit a sick man, for from such things you will be loved.

**Sirach 7:36**

In all your words remember your last things, and forever you will not sin.

### Chapter 8

**Sirach 8:1**

Do not contend with a powerful man, lest you fall into his hands.

**Sirach 8:2**

Do not strive with a rich man, lest he set your weight against you; for gold destroyed many and turned aside hearts of kings.

**Sirach 8:3**

Do not contend with a talkative man, and do not pile wood upon his fire.

**Sirach 8:4**

Do not jest with an undisciplined man, lest your ancestors be dishonored.

**Sirach 8:5**

Do not reproach a man turning away from sin; remember that we are all under penalties.

**Sirach 8:6**

Do not dishonor a man in his old age, for some of us also grow old.

**Sirach 8:7**

Do not rejoice over a dead man; remember that we all die.

**Sirach 8:8**

Do not neglect a narrative of wise men, and live among their proverbs; because from them you will learn discipline and to minister to great men.

**Sirach 8:9**

Do not miss a narrative of elders, for they also learned from their fathers; because from them you will learn understanding and to give answer in time of need.

**Sirach 8:10**

Do not kindle coals of a sinner, lest you be burned in the fire of his flame.

**Sirach 8:11**

Do not rise up from the face of an insolent man, lest he sit as an ambush for your mouth.

**Sirach 8:12**

Do not lend to a man stronger than you; and if you lend, become as one having lost it.

**Sirach 8:13**

Do not give surety beyond your power; and if you give surety, think as one who will pay.

**Sirach 8:14**

Do not litigate with a judge, for according to his glory they will judge for him.

**Sirach 8:15**

Do not travel on a road with a daring man, lest he be burdensome against you; for he will act according to his will, and by his folly you will perish together.

**Sirach 8:16**

Do not make a fight with a wrathful man, and do not journey through the wilderness with him; because blood is as nothing in his eyes, and where there is no help, he will throw you down.

**Sirach 8:17**

Do not take counsel with a foolish man, for he will not be able to keep a word.

**Sirach 8:18**

Before a stranger do not do a hidden thing, for you do not know what it will bear.

**Sirach 8:19**

Do not reveal your heart to every man, and let him not bring back favor to you.

### Chapter 9

**Sirach 9:1**

Do not be jealous of the wife of your bosom, nor teach evil discipline against yourself.

**Sirach 9:2**

Do not give your soul to a woman, to let her step upon your strength.

**Sirach 9:3**

Do not meet a woman acting as companion, lest you fall into her snares.

**Sirach 9:4**

With a singing woman do not spend time, lest you be captured in her attempts.

**Sirach 9:5**

Do not gaze closely at a virgin, lest you be scandalized in her penalties.

**Sirach 9:6**

Do not give your soul to prostitutes, lest you destroy your inheritance.

**Sirach 9:7**

Do not look around in streets of a city, and in her deserted places do not wander.

**Sirach 9:8**

Turn your eye from a beautiful woman, and do not gaze closely at another's beauty; by beauty of a woman many were deceived, and from this friendship is kindled like fire.

**Sirach 9:9**

With a married woman do not sit together at all, and do not feast with her over wine, lest your soul turn aside toward her and by your spirit you slip into destruction.

**Sirach 9:10**

Do not abandon an ancient friend, for a recent one is not equal to him. New wine, new friend; if it grows old, with gladness you will drink it.

**Sirach 9:11**

Do not be jealous of the glory of a sinner, for you do not know what his overthrow will be.

**Sirach 9:12**

Do not take pleasure in the good pleasure of ungodly ones; remember that until Hades they shall surely not be justified.

**Sirach 9:13**

Keep far from a man who has authority to kill, and you shall surely not suspect fear of death; and if you approach, do not offend, lest he take away your life. Know that you pass through the midst of snares and walk upon battlements of cities.

**Sirach 9:14**

According to your strength, aim at your neighbors, and take counsel with wise men.

**Sirach 9:15**

And let your reasoning be with understanding ones, and all your narrative in the law of the Most High.

**Sirach 9:16**

Let righteous men be your dinner companions, and let your boast be in fear of the Lord.

**Sirach 9:17**

In the hand of craftsmen a work will be praised, and the leader of a people is wise in his word.

**Sirach 9:18**

Fearsome in his city is a talkative man, and one rash in his word will be hated.

### Chapter 10

**Sirach 10:1**

A wise judge will discipline his people, and leadership of an understanding one will be ordered.

**Sirach 10:2**

According to the judge of his people, so also are his ministers, and according to the leader of the city, all those inhabiting it.

**Sirach 10:3**

An undisciplined king will destroy his people, and a city will be inhabited through the understanding of rulers.

**Sirach 10:4**

In the hand of the Lord is authority of the earth, and the useful man he will raise up for a time upon it.

**Sirach 10:5**

In the hand of the Lord is good success of a man, and upon the face of a scribe he will put his glory.

**Sirach 10:6**

For every wrong, do not be angry at your neighbor, and do nothing in works of arrogance.

**Sirach 10:7**

Pride is hateful before the Lord and men, and against both it will commit injustice.

**Sirach 10:8**

A kingdom is transferred from nation to nation through injustices and arrogant acts and money.

**Sirach 10:9**

Why is earth and ash proud? Because in life I cast out his entrails.

**Sirach 10:10**

A long sickness mocks a doctor; and a king today, and tomorrow he will die.

**Sirach 10:11**

For when a man dies, he will inherit reptiles and beasts and worms.

**Sirach 10:12**

Beginning of pride of a man is departing from the Lord, and his heart departed from the one who made him.

**Sirach 10:13**

Because beginning of pride is sin, and the one holding it will rain down abomination; because of this the Lord made calamities wondrous and overthrew them to the end.

**Sirach 10:14**

The Lord pulled down thrones of rulers and seated meek ones instead of them.

**Sirach 10:15**

The Lord plucked out roots of nations and planted humble ones instead of them.

**Sirach 10:16**

The Lord overthrew lands of nations and destroyed them to foundations of earth.

**Sirach 10:17**

He dried up from them and destroyed them and made their memorial cease from earth.

**Sirach 10:18**

Pride was not created for men, nor wrath of anger for offspring of women.

**Sirach 10:19**

Honored seed, what kind? Seed of man. Honored seed, what kind? Those fearing the Lord. Dishonored seed, what kind? Seed of man. Dishonored seed, what kind? Those transgressing commandments.

**Sirach 10:20**

Among brothers their leader is honored, and those fearing the Lord are in his eyes.

**Sirach 10:22**

Rich and glorious and poor, their boast is fear of the Lord.

**Sirach 10:23**

It is not righteous to dishonor an understanding poor man, and it is not fitting to glorify a sinful man.

**Sirach 10:24**

A great man and judge and ruler will be glorified, and none of them is greater than the one fearing the Lord.

**Sirach 10:25**

Free men will minister to a wise servant, and a knowledgeable man will not grumble.

**Sirach 10:26**

Do not play the wise man to do your work, and do not glorify yourself in time of your distress.

**Sirach 10:27**

Better is one working and abounding in all things than one walking about glorifying himself and lacking bread.

**Sirach 10:28**

Child, in meekness glorify your soul and give it honor according to its worth.

**Sirach 10:29**

The one sinning against his soul, who will justify? And who will glorify the one dishonoring his life?

**Sirach 10:30**

A poor man is glorified through his knowledge, and a rich man is glorified through his wealth.

**Sirach 10:31**

The one glorified in poverty, how much more in wealth? And the one without glory in wealth, how much more in poverty?

### Chapter 11

**Sirach 11:1**

Wisdom of a humble man will exalt his head and seat him in the midst of great men.

**Sirach 11:2**

Do not praise a man in his beauty, and do not abhor a man in his appearance.

**Sirach 11:3**

Small among winged creatures is a bee, and beginning of sweet things is her fruit.

**Sirach 11:4**

Do not boast in clothing of garments, and do not exalt yourself in a day of glory; because the works of the Lord are wonderful, and his works are hidden among men.

**Sirach 11:5**

Many rulers sat upon the ground, but the unsuspected one wore a diadem.

**Sirach 11:6**

Many mighty ones were greatly dishonored, and glorious ones were handed over into hands of others.

**Sirach 11:7**

Before you examine, do not blame; understand first and then rebuke.

**Sirach 11:8**

Before you hear, do not answer, and among words do not interrupt.

**Sirach 11:9**

Concerning a matter for which you have no need, do not argue, and in judgment of sinners do not sit together.

**Sirach 11:10**

Child, do not let your actions be about many things; if you multiply, you will not be innocent; and if you pursue, you shall surely not overtake, and you shall surely not escape by running away.

**Sirach 11:11**

There is one laboring and toiling and hastening, and so much more he is lacking.

**Sirach 11:12**

There is a sluggish one needing help, lacking in strength and abounding in poverty; and the eyes of the Lord looked upon him for good things, and he raised him up from his humiliation,

**Sirach 11:13**

and exalted his head, and many marveled at him.

**Sirach 11:14**

Good things and bad things, life and death, poverty and wealth are from the Lord.

**Sirach 11:17**

The gift of the Lord remains with godly ones, and his good pleasure will prosper forever.

**Sirach 11:18**

There is one enriched from his attention and tightness, and this is the portion of his wage.

**Sirach 11:19**

When he says, I found rest and now I will eat from my good things, he does not know what time will pass; and he will leave them to others and die.

**Sirach 11:20**

Stand in your covenant and converse in it, and grow old in your work.

**Sirach 11:21**

Do not marvel at works of a sinner; trust the Lord and remain in your labor, because it is easy in the eyes of the Lord to make a poor man rich swiftly, suddenly.

**Sirach 11:22**

The blessing of the Lord is in the wage of a godly man, and in a swift hour his blessing sprouts.

**Sirach 11:23**

Do not say, What is my need, and what good things will be mine from now?

**Sirach 11:24**

Do not say, They are sufficient for me, and from now what will harm me?

**Sirach 11:25**

In a day of good things there is forgetfulness of bad things, and in a day of bad things, good things will not be remembered;

**Sirach 11:26**

because it is easy before the Lord in the day of death to repay a man according to his ways.

**Sirach 11:27**

Affliction of an hour makes forgetfulness of luxury, and at the completion of a man there is revelation of his works.

**Sirach 11:28**

Before death call no one blessed, and in his children a man will be known.

**Sirach 11:29**

Do not bring every man into your house, for many are the snares of the deceitful one.

**Sirach 11:30**

A hunting partridge in a basket, so is the heart of a proud man, and like a spy he watches a fall;

**Sirach 11:31**

for turning good things into bad, he lies in wait, and among chosen things he will set a blemish.

**Sirach 11:32**

From a spark of fire a coal heap increases, and a sinful man lies in wait for blood.

**Sirach 11:33**

Take heed from an evildoer, for he fabricates wicked things, lest he give you a blemish forever.

**Sirach 11:34**

Lodge a stranger, and he will twist you in troubles and estrange you from your own things.

### Chapter 12

**Sirach 12:1**

If you do good, know to whom you do it, and there will be favor for your good things.

**Sirach 12:2**

Do good to a godly man, and you will find repayment; and if not from him, then from the Most High.

**Sirach 12:3**

There are no good things for one continuing in evils and for one not granting mercy.

**Sirach 12:4**

Give to the godly man and do not help the sinner.

**Sirach 12:5**

Do good to the humble man and do not give to the ungodly; hinder his breads and do not give to him, lest by them he dominate you; for you will find double evils in all good things that you do to him.

**Sirach 12:6**

Because also the Most High hated sinners and will repay vengeance to ungodly ones.

**Sirach 12:7**

Give to the good man and do not help the sinner.

**Sirach 12:8**

A friend will not be recognized in good things, and an enemy will not be hidden in bad things.

**Sirach 12:9**

In good things of a man, his enemies are in grief, and in his bad things even a friend will separate.

**Sirach 12:10**

Never trust your enemy; for as bronze rusts, so is his wickedness.

**Sirach 12:11**

And even if he is humbled and goes bent down, set your soul and guard from him, and you will be to him as one who has wiped a mirror, and you will know that it has not rusted to the end.

**Sirach 12:12**

Do not set him beside yourself, lest, overthrowing you, he stand upon your place; do not seat him at your right, lest he seek your seat, and at last you recognize my words and be pierced by my sayings.

**Sirach 12:13**

Who will pity a snake-charmer bitten by a snake, and all those who approach beasts?

**Sirach 12:14**

So is the one going toward a sinful man and being mixed in his sins.

**Sirach 12:15**

For an hour he will remain with you, and if you turn aside, he will surely not endure.

**Sirach 12:16**

And with his lips an enemy will sweeten, and in his heart he will plan to overthrow you into a pit; in his eyes an enemy will weep, and if he finds time, he will not be satisfied with blood.

**Sirach 12:17**

If evils meet you, you will find him there before you, and as if helping, he will cut under your heel;

**Sirach 12:18**

he will shake his head and clap his hands and whisper much and alter his face.

### Chapter 13

**Sirach 13:1**

The one touching pitch will be defiled, and the one sharing with a proud man will become like him.

**Sirach 13:2**

Do not lift a weight above you, and do not associate with one stronger and richer than you. What will a pot share with a kettle? This one will strike, and that one will be shattered.

**Sirach 13:3**

A rich man did wrong, and he himself added rage; a poor man has been wronged, and he himself will make petition.

**Sirach 13:4**

If you are useful, he works in you; and if you are lacking, he will leave you.

**Sirach 13:5**

If you have, he will live with you and empty you, and he himself will not suffer.

**Sirach 13:6**

He has had need of you, and he will deceive you and smile at you and give you hope; he will speak good things to you and say, What is your need?

**Sirach 13:7**

And he will shame you in his foods until he empties you twice or three times, and at last he will mock you; after these things he will see you and leave you and shake his head at you.

**Sirach 13:8**

Take heed, lest you be deceived and humbled in your folly.

**Sirach 13:9**

When a ruler invites you, become one withdrawing, and so much more he will invite you.

**Sirach 13:10**

Do not fall in, lest you be pushed away, and do not stand far off, lest you be forgotten.

**Sirach 13:11**

Do not set your heart to speak as an equal with him, and do not trust his many words; for from much speech he will test you, and as if smiling he will examine you.

**Sirach 13:12**

Merciless is the one not keeping words, and he shall surely not spare concerning harm and bonds.

**Sirach 13:13**

Keep and take heed greatly, because you walk with your fall. [14] Hearing these in your sleep, wake up; all your life love the Lord, and call upon him for your salvation.

**Sirach 13:15**

Every living creature loves what is like itself, and every man loves his neighbor;

**Sirach 13:16**

all flesh is gathered according to kind, and a man will cling to one like him.

**Sirach 13:17**

What will a wolf share with a lamb? So is a sinner toward a godly man.

**Sirach 13:18**

What peace has a hyena with a dog? And what peace has a rich man with a poor man?

**Sirach 13:19**

Wild asses in the wilderness are prey of lions; so poor men are pastures of rich men.

**Sirach 13:20**

Humility is an abomination to a proud man; so a poor man is an abomination to a rich man.

**Sirach 13:21**

A rich man being shaken is supported by friends, but a humble man having fallen is pushed farther away by friends.

**Sirach 13:22**

When a rich man stumbled, many were helpers; he spoke unspeakable things, and they justified him. A humble man stumbled, and they rebuked him further; he uttered understanding, and no place was given him.

**Sirach 13:23**

A rich man spoke, and all were silent, and they raised his word to the clouds. A poor man spoke, and they said, Who is this? And if he stumbles, they will overthrow him further.

**Sirach 13:24**

Wealth is good when there is no sin, and poverty is evil in the mouth of an ungodly man.

**Sirach 13:25**

The heart of a man alters his face, whether toward good things or toward bad things.

**Sirach 13:26**

A trace of a heart in good things is a cheerful face, and finding parables is reasonings with labor.

### Chapter 14

**Sirach 14:1**

Blessed is a man who did not slip in his mouth and was not pierced in grief of sin.

**Sirach 14:2**

Blessed is he whose soul did not condemn him and who did not fall from his hope.

**Sirach 14:3**

For a stingy man wealth is not good, and for a grudging man why possessions?

**Sirach 14:4**

The one gathering from his soul gathers for others, and in his good things others will live luxuriously.

**Sirach 14:5**

The one evil to himself, to whom will he be good? And he shall surely not be gladdened in his possessions.

**Sirach 14:6**

There is none worse than the one who grudges himself, and this is repayment of his evil;

**Sirach 14:7**

even if he does good, he does it in forgetfulness, and at last he reveals his evil.

**Sirach 14:8**

Evil is the one grudging with an eye, turning away his face and overlooking souls.

**Sirach 14:9**

The eye of a greedy one is not filled with a portion, and evil injustice dries up a soul.

**Sirach 14:10**

An evil eye is envious over bread and lacking at his table.

**Sirach 14:11**

Child, as you have, do good to yourself and bring offerings worthily to the Lord.

**Sirach 14:12**

Remember that death will not delay, and the covenant of Hades has not been shown to you;

**Sirach 14:13**

before you die, do good to a friend, and according to your strength stretch out and give to him.

**Sirach 14:14**

Do not be deprived of a good day, and let no portion of good desire pass you by.

**Sirach 14:15**

Will you not leave your labors to another and your toils for division of a lot?

**Sirach 14:16**

Give and take and indulge your soul, because in Hades there is no seeking luxury.

**Sirach 14:17**

All flesh grows old like a garment, for the covenant from eternity is: by death you will die.

**Sirach 14:18**

Like a flourishing leaf upon a dense tree, some it casts down and others it makes grow, so is a generation of flesh and blood; one dies, and another is born.

**Sirach 14:19**

Every decaying work fails, and the one working it will go away with it.

**Sirach 14:20**

Blessed is a man who will end in wisdom and who will converse in his understanding,

**Sirach 14:21**

the one thinking on her ways in his heart, and he will understand in her hidden things.

**Sirach 14:22**

Go out behind her like a tracker, and lie in wait in her entrances.

**Sirach 14:23**

The one peering through her windows and at her doorways will listen.

**Sirach 14:24**

The one lodging near her house will also fasten a peg in her walls;

**Sirach 14:25**

he will set his tent by her hands and lodge in a lodging of good things;

**Sirach 14:26**

he will set his children in her shelter and lodge under her branches;

**Sirach 14:27**

he will be sheltered by her from burning heat and lodge in her glory.

### Chapter 15

**Sirach 15:1**

The one fearing the Lord will do this, and the one mastering the law will take hold of her;

**Sirach 15:2**

and she will meet him as a mother and receive him as a wife of virginity.

**Sirach 15:3**

She will feed him bread of understanding and give him water of wisdom to drink.

**Sirach 15:4**

He will be supported upon her and shall surely not be bent, and upon her he will hold fast and shall surely not be ashamed;

**Sirach 15:5**

and she will exalt him beyond his neighbors and in the midst of an assembly open his mouth.

**Sirach 15:6**

Gladness and a crown of rejoicing and an eternal name he will inherit.

**Sirach 15:7**

Men without understanding shall surely not take hold of her, and sinful men shall surely not see her;

**Sirach 15:8**

she is far from pride, and lying men shall surely not remember her.

**Sirach 15:9**

Praise is not beautiful in the mouth of a sinner, because it was not sent from the Lord;

**Sirach 15:10**

for in wisdom praise will be spoken, and the Lord will prosper it.

**Sirach 15:11**

Do not say that because of the Lord I departed; for what he hated, you shall not do.

**Sirach 15:12**

Do not say that he deceived me; for he has no need of a sinful man.

**Sirach 15:13**

Every abomination the Lord hated, and it is not beloved to those fearing him.

**Sirach 15:14**

He from the beginning made man and left him in the hand of his deliberation.

**Sirach 15:15**

If you want, you will keep commandments, and faith to do good pleasure.

**Sirach 15:16**

He placed before you fire and water; whichever you want, you will stretch out your hand.

**Sirach 15:17**

Before men are life and death, and whichever he approves will be given to him.

**Sirach 15:18**

For much is the wisdom of the Lord; strong in dominion and seeing all things,

**Sirach 15:19**

and his eyes are upon those fearing him, and he will recognize every work of man.

**Sirach 15:20**

And he did not command anyone to be ungodly, and he did not give anyone leave to sin.

### Chapter 16

**Sirach 16:1**

Do not desire a multitude of useless children; do not rejoice over ungodly sons.

**Sirach 16:2**

If they multiply, do not rejoice over them, unless fear of the Lord is with them.

**Sirach 16:3**

Do not trust their life, and do not set your heart upon their multitude; for better is one than a thousand, and to die childless than to have ungodly children.

**Sirach 16:4**

For from one understanding man a city will be populated, but a tribe of lawless ones will be made desolate.

**Sirach 16:5**

Many such things my eyes have seen, and stronger things than these my ear has heard.

**Sirach 16:6**

In a congregation of sinners fire will be kindled, and in a disobedient nation wrath was burned.

**Sirach 16:7**

He did not make atonement concerning the ancient giants, who revolted by their strength;

**Sirach 16:8**

he did not spare concerning the sojourn of Lot, whom he abhorred because of their pride;

**Sirach 16:9**

he did not show mercy to a nation of destruction, those lifted up in their sins;

**Sirach 16:10**

and thus six hundred thousands of footmen, those gathered together in their hardheartedness.

**Sirach 16:11**

And even if there is one stiff-necked man, it is a wonder if he will be innocent; for mercy and wrath are with him, ruler of atonements and pouring out wrath.

**Sirach 16:12**

According to his much mercy, so also is his much reproof; he judges a man according to his works.

**Sirach 16:13**

A sinner will not escape with plunder, and he shall surely not delay the endurance of a godly man.

**Sirach 16:14**

He will make a place for every mercy; each will find according to his works. [15 The Lord hardened Pharaoh not to know him, so that his works might be known under heaven.

**Sirach 16:16**

To all creation his mercy is manifest, and his light and the darkness he divided by adamant.]

**Sirach 16:17**

Do not say, I will be hidden from the Lord, and who from the height will remember me? Among a greater people I shall surely not be known, for what is my soul in immeasurable creation?

**Sirach 16:18**

Behold, heaven and the heaven of heaven, abyss and earth will be shaken at his visitation.

**Sirach 16:19**

Together the mountains and the foundations of the earth, when he looks upon them, are shaken with trembling,

**Sirach 16:20**

and concerning them no heart will think; and his ways, who will consider?

**Sirach 16:21**

And a storm, which man will not see, and most of his works are in hidden places.

**Sirach 16:22**

Works of righteousness, who will announce, or who will endure? For the covenant is far away.

**Sirach 16:23**

One lessened in heart thinks these things, and a foolish and wandering man thinks foolish things.

**Sirach 16:24**

Listen to me, child, and learn science, and attend to my words with your heart.

**Sirach 16:25**

I will disclose discipline by weight and announce science with precision.

**Sirach 16:26**

In the judgment of the Lord are his works from the beginning, and from their making he separated their portions.

**Sirach 16:27**

He adorned his works forever and their beginnings for their generations; they neither hungered nor labored, and they did not fail from their works;

**Sirach 16:28**

each did not crowd his neighbor, and forever they will not disobey his word.

**Sirach 16:29**

And after these things the Lord looked upon the earth and filled it with his good things;

**Sirach 16:30**

the soul of every living creature covered its face, and to it is their return.

### Chapter 17

**Sirach 17:1**

The Lord created man from earth and again turned him back into it.

**Sirach 17:2**

He gave them days of number and a time, and gave them authority over the things upon it.

**Sirach 17:3**

According to themselves he clothed them with strength and made them according to his image,

**Sirach 17:4**

and he put his fear upon all flesh and to master beasts and winged creatures. [5 They received use of the five operations of the Lord, and as sixth he bestowed mind on them, apportioning also the seventh, speech, interpreter of his operations.]

**Sirach 17:6**

Counsel and tongue and eyes, ears and heart he gave them to think.

**Sirach 17:7**

He filled them with science of understanding and showed them good things and bad things.

**Sirach 17:8**

He put his eye upon their hearts to show them the majesty of his works,

**Sirach 17:9**

and they will praise a name of sanctification,

**Sirach 17:10**

so that they may tell the majesties of his works.

**Sirach 17:11**

He added science to them and gave them a law of life as inheritance.

**Sirach 17:12**

He established a covenant of an age with them and showed them his judgments.

**Sirach 17:13**

Their eyes saw majesty of glory, and their ear heard glory of his voice.

**Sirach 17:14**

And he said to them, Take heed from every unrighteous thing, and he commanded each of them concerning the neighbor.

**Sirach 17:15**

Their ways are always before him; they will not be hidden from his eyes.

**Sirach 17:17**

For each nation he appointed a leader, and Israel is the portion of the Lord.

**Sirach 17:19**

All their works are like the sun before him, and his eyes are continually upon their ways.

**Sirach 17:20**

Their unrighteous deeds were not hidden from him, and all their sins are before the Lord.

**Sirach 17:22**

Mercy of a man is like a seal with him, and grace of a man he will keep like a pupil.

**Sirach 17:23**

After these things he will rise up and repay them, and he will repay their repayment upon their head;

**Sirach 17:24**

nevertheless, to those repenting he gave a return and comforted those failing in endurance.

**Sirach 17:25**

Turn to the Lord and leave sins; make petition before his face and lessen offense.

**Sirach 17:26**

Return to the Most High and turn away from unrighteousness, and greatly hate abomination.

**Sirach 17:27**

Who will praise the Most High in Hades instead of the living and those giving thanks?

**Sirach 17:28**

From a dead man, as from one not existing, confession perishes; a living and healthy man will praise the Lord.

**Sirach 17:29**

How great is the mercy of the Lord and atonement for those returning to him.

**Sirach 17:30**

For all things cannot be in men, because a son of man is not immortal.

**Sirach 17:31**

What is brighter than the sun? Yet this fails; and an evil man will consider flesh and blood.

**Sirach 17:32**

He visits the power of the height of heaven, and all men are earth and ash.

### Chapter 18

**Sirach 18:1**

The one living forever created all things in common;

**Sirach 18:2**

the Lord alone will be justified. [And there is no other besides him,

**Sirach 18:3**

steering the world in the span of his hand, and all things obey his will; for he is king of all by his might, separating among them holy things from profane.]

**Sirach 18:4**

He did not enable anyone to declare his works; and who will trace out his majestic things?

**Sirach 18:5**

The might of his majesty, who will count out? And who will add to tell out his mercies?

**Sirach 18:6**

There is no diminishing nor adding, and there is no tracing out the wonders of the Lord;

**Sirach 18:7**

when a man finishes, then he begins, and when he stops, then he will be perplexed.

**Sirach 18:8**

What is man, and what is his use? What is his good, and what is his evil?

**Sirach 18:9**

The number of man's days is many years, a hundred.

**Sirach 18:10**

As a drop of water from a sea and a pebble of sand, so are few years in a day of an age.

**Sirach 18:11**

Because of this the Lord was long-suffering upon them and poured out his mercy upon them.

**Sirach 18:12**

He saw and recognized their overthrow, that it is evil; because of this he multiplied his atonement.

**Sirach 18:13**

Mercy of a man is upon his neighbor, but mercy of the Lord is upon all flesh, reproving and disciplining and teaching and returning, as a shepherd his flock.

**Sirach 18:14**

He shows mercy to those accepting discipline and those hastening toward his judgments.

**Sirach 18:15**

Child, in good things do not give a blemish, and in every gift do not give grief of words.

**Sirach 18:16**

Will not dew give rest to burning heat? So a word is better than a gift.

**Sirach 18:17**

Behold, is not a word above a good gift? And both are with a gracious man.

**Sirach 18:18**

A fool will reproach ungraciously, and the gift of a grudging man melts eyes.

**Sirach 18:19**

Before you speak, learn, and before sickness, treat yourself.

**Sirach 18:20**

Before judgment examine yourself, and in an hour of visitation you will find atonement.

**Sirach 18:21**

Before you become sick, humble yourself, and in a time of sins show turning.

**Sirach 18:22**

Do not be hindered from repaying a vow in due time, and do not wait until death to be justified.

**Sirach 18:23**

Before making a vow, prepare yourself, and do not become as a man testing the Lord.

**Sirach 18:24**

Remember wrath in days of death and a time of vengeance in the turning away of face.

**Sirach 18:25**

Remember a time of famine in a time of fullness, poverty and lack in days of wealth.

**Sirach 18:26**

From morning until evening time changes, and all things are swift before the Lord.

**Sirach 18:27**

A wise man will be cautious in everything, and in days of sins he will take heed from trespass.

**Sirach 18:28**

Every understanding one knew wisdom and will give confession to the one who found her.

**Sirach 18:29**

Understanding ones in words also became wise and rained down precise proverbs. Self-Control of Soul.

**Sirach 18:30**

Do not go after your desires, and restrain yourself from your appetites.

**Sirach 18:31**

If you supply your soul with good pleasure of desire, it will make you an object of joy to your enemies.

**Sirach 18:32**

Do not rejoice over much luxury, nor be bound to its expense.

**Sirach 18:33**

Do not become poor by banquet-making from borrowing when there is nothing in your purse.

### Chapter 19

**Sirach 19:1**

A drunken worker will not become rich, and the one despising small things will fall little by little.

**Sirach 19:2**

Wine and women will make understanding men fall away, and the one clinging to prostitutes will become more daring;

**Sirach 19:3**

moths and worms will inherit him, and a daring soul will be taken away.

**Sirach 19:4**

The one quickly trusting is light in heart, and the one sinning against his soul will trespass.

**Sirach 19:5**

The one rejoicing in heart will be condemned,

**Sirach 19:6**

and the one hating chatter is lessened in evil.

**Sirach 19:7**

Never repeat a word, and nothing shall surely be lessened for you.

**Sirach 19:8**

Do not report it among friend or enemy, and unless it is sin to you, do not reveal it;

**Sirach 19:9**

for he heard you and kept watch over you, and in time he will hate you.

**Sirach 19:10**

You heard a word; let it die with you. Take courage; it shall surely not burst you.

**Sirach 19:11**

Because of a word a fool will labor, like the one giving birth because of a child.

**Sirach 19:12**

An arrow fixed in a thigh of flesh, so is a word in the belly of a fool.

**Sirach 19:13**

Reprove a friend, lest perhaps he did not do it, and if he did anything, lest perhaps he add to it.

**Sirach 19:14**

Reprove your neighbor, lest perhaps he did not say it, and if he has spoken, so that he may not repeat it.

**Sirach 19:15**

Reprove a friend, for often slander happens, and do not trust every word.

**Sirach 19:16**

There is one slipping, and not from the soul; and who has not sinned with his tongue?

**Sirach 19:17**

Reprove your neighbor before threatening, and give place to the law of the Most High. [Becoming without wrath,

**Sirach 19:18**

fear of the Lord is beginning of acceptance, and wisdom from him gains love.

**Sirach 19:19**

Knowledge of the commandments of the Lord is discipline of life, and those doing the things pleasing to him bear fruit from a tree of immortality.]

**Sirach 19:20**

All wisdom is fear of the Lord, and in all wisdom is doing of law; [and knowledge of his almighty power.

**Sirach 19:21**

A servant saying to the master, I will not do as it pleases, if after these things he does it, provokes the one feeding him.]

**Sirach 19:22**

And science of wickedness is not wisdom, and there is no prudence where counsel of sinners is.

**Sirach 19:23**

There is wickedness, and this is abomination; and there is a fool lacking wisdom.

**Sirach 19:24**

Better is one defeated in understanding and fearing than one abounding in prudence and transgressing law.

**Sirach 19:25**

There is exact craftiness, and this is unrighteous; and there is one twisting favor to make judgment appear.

**Sirach 19:26**

There is one acting wickedly, bent down in blackness, and his inward parts are full of deceit;

**Sirach 19:27**

bending his face and pretending deafness, where he was not recognized, he will get ahead of you;

**Sirach 19:28**

and if by lack of strength he is hindered from sinning, if he finds a time, he will do evil.

**Sirach 19:29**

From appearance a man will be recognized, and from encounter of face an intelligent man will be recognized.

**Sirach 19:30**

A man's clothing and laughter of teeth and steps of a man announce the things concerning him.

### Chapter 20

**Sirach 20:1**

There is a reproof that is not beautiful, and there is one keeping silent, and he himself is prudent.

**Sirach 20:2**

How good to reprove rather than be angry, and the one confessing back will be hindered from loss.

**Sirach 20:4**

Desire of a eunuch to deflower a young woman, so is one doing judgments by force.

**Sirach 20:5**

There is one keeping silent found wise, and there is one hated because of much chatter.

**Sirach 20:6**

There is one keeping silent, for he has no answer, and there is one keeping silent knowing the time.

**Sirach 20:7**

A wise man will be silent until a time, but the chatterer and fool will overstep a time.

**Sirach 20:8**

The one abounding in speech will be abhorred, and the one taking authority for himself will be hated.

**Sirach 20:9**

There is good success in bad things for a man, and there is a find that leads to loss.

**Sirach 20:10**

There is a gift that will not profit you, and there is a gift whose repayment is double.

**Sirach 20:11**

There is loss for the sake of glory, and there is one who lifted up his head from humiliation.

**Sirach 20:12**

There is one buying many things for little and paying for them sevenfold.

**Sirach 20:13**

The wise one in speech will make himself beloved, but favors of fools will be poured out.

**Sirach 20:14**

The gift of a foolish man will not profit you, for his eyes are many instead of one;

**Sirach 20:15**

he will give few things and reproach many things and open his mouth like a herald; today he will lend and tomorrow he will demand back; such a man is hated.

**Sirach 20:16**

A fool will say, I have no friend, and there is no favor for my good things; those eating my bread are worthless in tongue;

**Sirach 20:17**

how often and how many will laugh him down?

**Sirach 20:18**

A slip from ground rather than from tongue; so a fall of evil men will come quickly.

**Sirach 20:19**

A graceless man is an untimely tale; it will continue in the mouth of undisciplined ones.

**Sirach 20:20**

A parable from the mouth of a fool will be rejected, for he shall surely not speak it in its time.

**Sirach 20:21**

There is one hindered from sinning by lack, and in his rest he will not be pierced.

**Sirach 20:22**

There is one destroying his soul through shame, and from a foolish face he will destroy it.

**Sirach 20:23**

There is one promising to a friend because of shame, and he acquired him as an enemy for nothing.

**Sirach 20:24**

An evil blemish in a man is a lie; it will continue in the mouth of undisciplined ones.

**Sirach 20:25**

Preferable is a thief rather than one continuing in a lie, but both will inherit destruction.

**Sirach 20:26**

The character of a lying man is dishonor, and his shame is with him continually. Words of Parables.

**Sirach 20:27**

The wise one in words will advance himself, and a prudent man will please great men.

**Sirach 20:28**

The one working the ground will lift up his heap, and the one pleasing great men will atone for unrighteousness.

**Sirach 20:29**

Guest-gifts and presents blind eyes of wise men and like a muzzle in a mouth turn away reproofs.

**Sirach 20:30**

Hidden wisdom and unseen treasure, what profit is in both?

**Sirach 20:31**

Better is a man hiding his foolishness than a man hiding his wisdom.

### Chapter 21

**Sirach 21:1**

Child, have you sinned? Add no longer, and make petition concerning your former sins.

**Sirach 21:2**

Flee from sin as from the face of a serpent, for if you approach, it will bite you; its teeth are lion's teeth, taking away souls of men.

**Sirach 21:3**

All lawlessness is like a two-edged sword; for its wound there is no healing.

**Sirach 21:4**

Terror and arrogance will make wealth desolate; so a house of a proud man will be made desolate.

**Sirach 21:5**

Petition of a poor man is from mouth to his ears, and his judgment comes quickly.

**Sirach 21:6**

One hating reproof is in the track of a sinner, and the one fearing the Lord will turn in heart.

**Sirach 21:7**

Known from far away is the one powerful in tongue, but the intelligent one knows when he slips.

**Sirach 21:8**

The one building his house with another's possessions is like one gathering his stones for winter.

**Sirach 21:9**

A gathered tow is a congregation of lawless ones, and their end is flame of fire.

**Sirach 21:10**

A way of sinners is smoothed from stones, and at its end is pit of Hades.

**Sirach 21:11**

The one keeping law masters his thought, and completion of fear of the Lord is wisdom.

**Sirach 21:12**

One who is not skillful will not be disciplined; there is a craftiness multiplying bitterness.

**Sirach 21:13**

Knowledge of a wise man will be multiplied like a flood, and his counsel like a spring of life.

**Sirach 21:14**

Inward parts of a fool are like a broken vessel, and it will not hold any knowledge.

**Sirach 21:15**

If a knowledgeable man hears a wise word, he will praise it and add to it; the wasteful man heard, and it displeased him, and he turned it behind his back.

**Sirach 21:16**

Explanation of a fool is like a burden on a road, but grace will be found upon lips of an understanding one.

**Sirach 21:17**

Mouth of a prudent one will be sought in assembly, and they will think on his words in heart.

**Sirach 21:18**

Wisdom is to a fool like a vanished house, and knowledge of one without understanding is unexamined words.

**Sirach 21:19**

Discipline is fetters on feet to senseless ones and like handcuffs on a right hand.

**Sirach 21:20**

A fool in laughter lifts up his voice, but a skillful man will scarcely smile quietly.

**Sirach 21:21**

Discipline is like a gold ornament to a prudent one and like a bracelet upon right arm.

**Sirach 21:22**

Foot of a fool is quick into a house, but a man of much experience will be ashamed before a face.

**Sirach 21:23**

A fool peeps from the door into a house, but a disciplined man will stand outside.

**Sirach 21:24**

It is lack of discipline for a man to listen beside a door, but the prudent one will be weighed down with dishonor.

**Sirach 21:25**

Lips of strangers will be weighed down in these things, but words of prudent ones will be weighed in a balance.

**Sirach 21:26**

In the mouth of fools is their heart, but the mouth of wise ones is in their heart.

**Sirach 21:27**

When an ungodly man curses Satan, he curses his own soul.

**Sirach 21:28**

The whisperer defiles his own soul and will be hated in a sojourning.

### Chapter 22

**Sirach 22:1**

A lazy man was compared to a dirty stone, and everyone will hiss at his dishonor.

**Sirach 22:2**

A lazy man was compared to a lump of dung; everyone who picks it up will shake off his hand.

**Sirach 22:3**

Shame of a father is in begetting an undisciplined child, and a daughter is born for loss.

**Sirach 22:4**

A prudent daughter will inherit her husband, and the one putting to shame is for grief of the one who begot her;

**Sirach 22:5**

the bold woman shames father and husband, and by both she will be dishonored.

**Sirach 22:6**

Music in mourning is an untimely tale, but whips and discipline are wisdom in every time.

**Sirach 22:7**

One teaching a fool is gluing a potsherd, waking one sleeping from deep sleep.

**Sirach 22:8**

One telling a tale to one nodding is one telling a tale to a fool, and at the end he will say, what is it?

**Sirach 22:11**

Weep over a dead man, for light has failed, and weep over a fool, for understanding has failed. Weep more sweetly over a dead man, because he has rested, but the life of the fool is evil beyond death.

**Sirach 22:12**

Mourning for a dead man is seven days, but for a fool and ungodly man, all the days of his life.

**Sirach 22:13**

Do not multiply speech with a foolish man, and do not go to one without understanding. Guard yourself from him, so that you may not have trouble, and you shall surely not be defiled in his shaking off. Turn aside from him and you will find rest, and you shall surely not be weary in his senselessness.

**Sirach 22:14**

What will be made heavy beyond lead? And what name does he have except fool?

**Sirach 22:15**

Sand and salt and a lump of iron are easier to bear than a man without understanding.

**Sirach 22:16**

Wooden bracing bound into a building will not be loosened in a shaking; so a heart established on a thought of counsel will not be cowardly in a time.

**Sirach 22:17**

A heart firmly fixed on a thought of understanding is like a sanded ornament of a polished wall.

**Sirach 22:18**

Posts set on a height against wind shall surely not endure; so a cowardly heart on a fool's thought shall surely not endure against any fear.

**Sirach 22:19**

The one piercing an eye will bring down tears, and the one piercing a heart makes feeling appear.

**Sirach 22:20**

One throwing a stone at birds will scare them away, and the one reproaching a friend will dissolve friendship.

**Sirach 22:21**

If you draw a sword against a friend, do not despair, for there is a return.

**Sirach 22:22**

If you open a mouth against a friend, do not be cautious, for there is reconciliation, except reproach and pride and revealing of a mystery and a deceitful blow; in these things every friend will flee.

**Sirach 22:23**

Acquire faithfulness in poverty with your neighbor, so that you may be filled together in his good things. Remain with him in time of affliction, so that you may inherit together in his inheritance.

**Sirach 22:24**

Before fire are vapor of furnace and smoke; so before bloodshed are revilings.

**Sirach 22:25**

I will not be ashamed to cover a friend, and from his face I shall surely not hide.

**Sirach 22:26**

And if evils happen to me because of him, everyone hearing will guard himself from him.

**Sirach 22:27**

Who will give me a guard over my mouth and a skillful seal upon my lips, so that I may not fall because of it and my tongue destroy me?

### Chapter 23

**Sirach 23:1**

Lord, Father and Master of my life, do not abandon me in their counsel; do not let me fall among them.

**Sirach 23:2**

Who will set whips over my thought and discipline of wisdom over my heart, so that they may not spare my ignorances and my sins may surely not pass by,

**Sirach 23:3**

so that my ignorances may not multiply and my sins abound, and I fall before adversaries and my enemy rejoice over me?

**Sirach 23:4**

Lord, Father and God of my life, do not give me loftiness of eyes,

**Sirach 23:5**

and turn desire away from me.

**Sirach 23:6**

Do not let appetite of belly and intercourse overtake me, and do not hand me over to a shameless soul. Discipline of Mouth.

**Sirach 23:7**

Hear discipline of mouth, children, and the one keeping it shall surely not be caught.

**Sirach 23:8**

A sinner will be seized by his lips, and a reviler and proud man will be stumbled by them.

**Sirach 23:9**

Do not accustom your mouth to an oath, and do not become accustomed to naming the Holy One;

**Sirach 23:10**

for just as a servant examined continually will not lack a bruise, so also the one swearing and naming continually shall surely not be cleansed from sin.

**Sirach 23:11**

A man of many oaths will be filled with lawlessness, and a whip will not depart from his house. If he trespasses, his sin is upon him, and if he overlooks, he has sinned double; and if he swore in vain, he will not be justified, for his house will be filled with calamities.

**Sirach 23:12**

There is a word clothed around with death; let it not be found in the inheritance of Jacob, for all these things will depart from the devout, and they will not roll themselves in sins.

**Sirach 23:13**

Do not accustom your mouth to shameful lack of discipline, for a word of sin is in it.

**Sirach 23:14**

Remember your father and mother, for you sit among great men; lest you forget before them and by your habit be made foolish, and you wish you had not been born and curse the day of your birth.

**Sirach 23:15**

A man accustomed to words of reproach shall surely not be disciplined in all his days.

**Sirach 23:16**

Two kinds multiply sins, and the third will bring on wrath:

**Sirach 23:17**

a hot soul like burning fire shall surely not be quenched until it is consumed; a fornicator man in the body of his flesh shall surely not stop until fire burns out; to a fornicator man every bread is sweet; he shall surely not cease until he dies.

**Sirach 23:18**

A man transgressing from his bed, saying in his soul, Who sees me? Darkness is around me, and the walls cover me, and no one sees me; why should I be cautious? The Most High shall surely not remember my sins.

**Sirach 23:19**

And eyes of men are his fear, and he did not know that the eyes of the Lord are brighter than the sun ten-thousandfold, looking upon all ways of men and observing hidden parts.

**Sirach 23:20**

Before all things were created, it was known to him, and so also after they are completed.

**Sirach 23:21**

This one will be punished in the streets of a city, and where he did not suspect, he will be caught.

**Sirach 23:22**

So also a woman leaving her husband and presenting an heir from another;

**Sirach 23:23**

for first she disobeyed in the law of the Most High, and second she trespassed against her own husband, and third she committed adultery in fornication and presented children from another man.

**Sirach 23:24**

This woman will be brought out into an assembly, and there will be visitation upon her children.

**Sirach 23:25**

Her children will not spread into root, and her branches will not give fruit.

**Sirach 23:26**

She will leave her memorial for a curse, and her reproach will not be wiped out.

**Sirach 23:27**

And those left behind will recognize that nothing is better than fear of the Lord, and nothing sweeter than paying attention to commandments of the Lord.

### Chapter 24

**Sirach 24:1**

Praise of Wisdom. Wisdom will praise her own soul and boast in the midst of her people.

**Sirach 24:2**

In the assembly of the Most High she will open her mouth and boast before his power:

**Sirach 24:3**

I came out from the mouth of the Most High, and like mist I covered earth.

**Sirach 24:4**

I tabernacled in the heights, and my throne was in a pillar of cloud.

**Sirach 24:5**

I alone circled the vault of heaven and walked in the depth of abysses.

**Sirach 24:6**

In waves of sea and in all the earth and in every people and nation I gained possession.

**Sirach 24:7**

Among all these I sought rest, and in whose inheritance shall I lodge?

**Sirach 24:8**

Then the creator of all commanded me, and the one who created me made my tent rest and said, Tabernacle in Jacob and be inherited in Israel.

**Sirach 24:9**

Before the age, from the beginning, he created me, and until age I shall surely not fail.

**Sirach 24:10**

In a holy tent before him I ministered, and so I was established in Zion.

**Sirach 24:11**

In the beloved city likewise he gave me rest, and in Jerusalem is my authority.

**Sirach 24:12**

And I took root in a glorified people, in the portion of the Lord, of his inheritance.

**Sirach 24:13**

Like a cedar I was lifted up in Lebanon and like a cypress in the mountains of Hermon;

**Sirach 24:14**

like a palm I was lifted up on shores and like rose plants in Jericho, like a comely olive tree in a plain, and I was lifted up like a plane tree.

**Sirach 24:15**

Like cinnamon and aspalathus of spices I gave fragrance, and like chosen myrrh I spread sweet odor, like galbanum and onycha and stacte and like vapor of frankincense in a tent.

**Sirach 24:16**

I, like a terebinth, stretched out my branches, and my branches are branches of glory and grace.

**Sirach 24:17**

I, like a vine, sprouted grace, and my flowers are fruit of glory and wealth. [18 I am mother of beautiful love, and of fear and knowledge and holy hope; therefore I am given to all my children, ever-born to those named by him.]

**Sirach 24:19**

Come near to me, you who desire me, and be filled from my products;

**Sirach 24:20**

for my memorial is sweeter than honey, and my inheritance more than a honeycomb.

**Sirach 24:21**

Those eating me will still hunger, and those drinking me will still thirst.

**Sirach 24:22**

The one obeying me will not be ashamed, and those working in me will not sin.

**Sirach 24:23**

All these things are book of covenant of God Most High, law which Moses commanded us, an inheritance for congregations of Jacob. [24 Do not grow weak in strength in the Lord, but cling to him so that he may strengthen you; Lord Almighty is God alone, and there is no savior yet besides him.]

**Sirach 24:25**

The one filling wisdom like Pison and like Tigris in days of new fruits,

**Sirach 24:26**

the one filling understanding like Euphrates and like Jordan in days of harvest,

**Sirach 24:27**

the one making discipline appear like light, like Gihon in days of vintage.

**Sirach 24:28**

The first did not finish knowing her, and so the last did not search her out;

**Sirach 24:29**

for her thought was multiplied from sea, and her counsel from a great abyss.

**Sirach 24:30**

And I, like a canal from a river and like an aqueduct, came out into a garden.

**Sirach 24:31**

I said, I will water my garden and make my garden bed drunk; and behold, my canal became to me a river, and my river became a sea.

**Sirach 24:32**

I will still illuminate discipline like dawn and make these things appear to far away.

**Sirach 24:33**

I will still pour out teaching like prophecy and leave it for generations of ages.

**Sirach 24:34**

See that I labored not for myself alone, but for all those seeking her.

### Chapter 25

**Sirach 25:1**

In three things I was beautified and stood beautiful before the Lord and men: agreement of brothers, and friendship of neighbors, and wife and husband behaving together toward themselves.

**Sirach 25:2**

But three kinds my soul hated, and I was greatly angered at their life: a proud poor man, and a rich liar, an old adulterer lacking understanding.

**Sirach 25:3**

In youth you have not gathered; and how would you find in your old age?

**Sirach 25:4**

How beautiful is judgment for gray hairs, and for elders to know counsel.

**Sirach 25:5**

How beautiful is wisdom of old men, and thought and counsel for glorified men.

**Sirach 25:6**

Much experience is crown of old men, and their boast is fear of the Lord.

**Sirach 25:7**

Nine thoughts I counted blessed in heart, and the tenth I will speak upon tongue: a man rejoicing over children, living and looking upon the fall of enemies;

**Sirach 25:8**

blessed is the one dwelling with an understanding wife, and one who did not slip with tongue, and one who did not serve one unworthy of him;

**Sirach 25:9**

blessed is one who found prudence, and the one telling into ears of those hearing;

**Sirach 25:10**

how great is the one who found wisdom, but there is none above the one fearing the Lord;

**Sirach 25:11**

fear of the Lord surpassed above all; the one holding it, to whom will he be likened? [12 Fear of the Lord is beginning of loving him, and faith is beginning of clinging to him.]

**Sirach 25:13**

Every wound, but not wound of heart; and every wickedness, but not wickedness of a woman;

**Sirach 25:14**

every calamity, but not calamity of those hating; and every vengeance, but not vengeance of enemies.

**Sirach 25:15**

There is no head above head of a serpent, and there is no anger above anger of an enemy.

**Sirach 25:16**

I would be pleased to dwell with a lion and dragon rather than to live with a wicked woman.

**Sirach 25:17**

Wickedness of a woman alters her appearance and darkens her face like a bear.

**Sirach 25:18**

Her husband will recline among his neighbor, and hearing, he groaned bitter things.

**Sirach 25:19**

Every evil is small compared to evil of a woman; may portion of a sinner fall upon her.

**Sirach 25:20**

A sandy ascent in feet of an elder, so is a talkative woman to a quiet man.

**Sirach 25:21**

Do not fall upon beauty of a woman, and do not desire a woman.

**Sirach 25:22**

Wrath and shamelessness and great shame is a woman if she supplies her husband.

**Sirach 25:23**

Lowly heart and gloomy face and wound of heart is a wicked woman; slack hands and loosened knees is one who does not bless her husband.

**Sirach 25:24**

From a woman is beginning of sin, and because of her we all die.

**Sirach 25:25**

Do not give water an outlet, nor a wicked woman authority.

**Sirach 25:26**

If she does not go according to your hand, cut her off from your flesh.

### Chapter 26

**Sirach 26:1**

Blessed is the husband of a good wife, and the number of his days is double.

**Sirach 26:2**

A courageous wife cheers her husband, and he will fill his years in peace.

**Sirach 26:3**

A good wife is a good portion; she will be given in the portion of those fearing the Lord.

**Sirach 26:4**

But of rich man and poor man, a good heart; in every time, a cheerful face.

**Sirach 26:5**

From three things my heart was cautious, and over the fourth face I made petition: slander of a city, and assembly of a mob, and false accusation, all more evil than death.

**Sirach 26:6**

Pain of heart and mourning is a rival wife against a wife, and a whip of tongue communicating with all.

**Sirach 26:7**

A shaken ox-yoke is a wicked woman; the one holding her is like one grasping a scorpion.

**Sirach 26:8**

Great wrath is a drunken woman, and she will not cover up her shamefulness.

**Sirach 26:9**

Fornication of a woman will be known in liftings of eyes and in her eyelids.

**Sirach 26:10**

Set firm guard over an unrestrained daughter, so that when she finds relief she may not use it for herself.

**Sirach 26:11**

Guard yourself behind a shameless eye, and do not wonder if it trespasses against you;

**Sirach 26:12**

like a thirsty traveler she will open her mouth and drink from every water nearby; before every peg she will sit and before an arrow she will open a quiver.

**Sirach 26:13**

Grace of a wife will delight her husband, and her knowledge will fatten his bones.

**Sirach 26:14**

A silent wife is a gift of the Lord, and there is no exchange for a disciplined soul.

**Sirach 26:15**

Grace upon grace is a modest wife, and no scale is worthy of a self-controlled soul.

**Sirach 26:16**

The sun rising in the highest places of the Lord, and beauty of a good wife in ornament of her house.

**Sirach 26:17**

A lamp shining upon a holy lampstand, and beauty of face upon a firm stature.

**Sirach 26:18**

Golden pillars upon a silver base, and beautiful feet upon breasts of one firmly set. [19 Child, keep the prime of your age healthy, and do not give your strength to strangers.

**Sirach 26:20**

After searching every plain for good-soiled allotment, sow your own seeds, trusting in your good birth;

**Sirach 26:21**

so your produce, surviving and having boldness of good birth, will become great.

**Sirach 26:22**

A hired woman will be counted equal to spit, but a married woman will be counted a tower of death to those using her.

**Sirach 26:23**

An ungodly woman will be given as portion to a lawless man, but a devout woman is given to the one fearing the Lord.

**Sirach 26:24**

A shameful woman will grind away dishonor, but a well-formed daughter will be ashamed before her husband.

**Sirach 26:25**

An unrestrained woman will be counted like a dog, but the one having shame will fear the Lord.

**Sirach 26:26**

A woman honoring her own husband will appear wise to all, but one dishonoring him in pride will be known to all as ungodly. Blessed is the husband of a good wife, for the number of his years will be double.

**Sirach 26:27**

A loud-voiced and talkative woman will be regarded like a trumpet of wars for rout, and the soul of every man similar to these will live amid disorders of war.]

**Sirach 26:28**

Over two things my heart has been grieved, and over the third wrath came upon me: a warrior man lacking through need, and understanding men if they are treated as refuse, one turning back from righteousness to sin; the Lord will prepare him for sword.

**Sirach 26:29**

With difficulty a merchant will be delivered from trespass, and a retailer will not be justified from sin.

### Chapter 27

**Sirach 27:1**

For sake of profit many sinned, and the one seeking to multiply will turn away an eye.

**Sirach 27:2**

Between joints of stones a peg will be fixed, and between selling and buying sin will be crushed in.

**Sirach 27:3**

Unless he holds fast in fear of the Lord with haste, his house will quickly be overturned.

**Sirach 27:4**

In shaking of a sieve refuse remains; so the scum of a man is in his reasoning.

**Sirach 27:5**

A furnace tests vessels of a potter, and trial of a man is in his reasoning.

**Sirach 27:6**

Fruit makes visible cultivation of a tree, so a word makes visible thought of a man's heart.

**Sirach 27:7**

Do not praise a man before reasoning, for this is trial of men.

**Sirach 27:8**

If you pursue the righteous thing, you will seize it and put it on like a full-length robe of glory.

**Sirach 27:9**

Birds will lodge with things like themselves, and truth will return to those working it.

**Sirach 27:10**

A lion lies in wait for prey; so sins for those working unrighteous things.

**Sirach 27:11**

Tale of a devout man is always wisdom, but the fool changes like moon.

**Sirach 27:12**

In the midst of those without understanding, keep watch for a time; but in the midst of those considering, continue.

**Sirach 27:13**

Tale of fools is an irritation, and their laughter is in wastefulness of sin.

**Sirach 27:14**

Chatter of one of many oaths will make hairs stand upright, and their quarrel is a stopping of ears.

**Sirach 27:15**

Quarrel of proud men is pouring out of blood, and their mutual reviling is evil hearing.

**Sirach 27:16**

The one revealing mysteries destroyed faithfulness, and he shall surely not find a friend for his soul.

**Sirach 27:17**

Love a friend and be faithful with him, but if you reveal his mysteries, you shall surely not pursue after him.

**Sirach 27:18**

For as a man destroyed his enemy, so you destroyed friendship of neighbor;

**Sirach 27:19**

and as you released a bird from your hand, so you let your neighbor go, and you will not hunt him.

**Sirach 27:20**

Do not pursue him, because he has stood far away and escaped like a gazelle from a snare.

**Sirach 27:21**

For a wound can be bound up and there is reconciliation after reviling, but one who revealed mysteries has given up hope.

**Sirach 27:22**

One winking with an eye crafts evil things, and no one will turn him away from it.

**Sirach 27:23**

Before your eyes he will sweeten your mouth, and over your words he will marvel; but afterward he will twist his mouth and by your words he will give offense.

**Sirach 27:24**

I hated many things and did not liken them to him, and the Lord will hate him.

**Sirach 27:25**

The one throwing a stone upward throws it upon his own head, and a deceitful blow will divide wounds.

**Sirach 27:26**

The one digging a pit will fall into it, and the one setting a snare will be caught in it.

**Sirach 27:27**

The one doing wicked things, upon him it will roll, and he shall surely not know from where it comes to him.

**Sirach 27:28**

Mockery and reproach are of proud men, and vengeance will lie in wait for him like a lion.

**Sirach 27:29**

Those rejoicing at fall of devout ones will be caught by a snare, and pain will consume them before their death.

**Sirach 27:30**

Rage and wrath, these also are abominations, and a sinful man will be master of them.

### Chapter 28

**Sirach 28:1**

The one taking vengeance will find vengeance from the Lord, and preserving, he will preserve his sins.

**Sirach 28:2**

Forgive wrongdoing to your neighbor, and then when you make petition, your sins will be loosed.

**Sirach 28:3**

A man preserves wrath against a man, and seeks healing from the Lord?

**Sirach 28:4**

He has no mercy upon a man like himself, and makes petition concerning his own sins?

**Sirach 28:5**

He himself, being flesh, preserves rage; who will atone for his sins?

**Sirach 28:6**

Remember the last things and stop being hostile, destruction and death, and remain in commandments.

**Sirach 28:7**

Remember commandments and do not rage against neighbor, and remember covenant of the Most High and overlook ignorance.

**Sirach 28:8**

Keep away from quarrel, and you will lessen sins; for a hot-tempered man will kindle a quarrel,

**Sirach 28:9**

and a sinful man will trouble friends and cast slander among those making peace.

**Sirach 28:10**

According to the fuel of fire, so it will be kindled, and according to the strength of the man his anger will be, and according to wealth he will lift up his wrath, and according to the firmness of the quarrel it will be kindled.

**Sirach 28:11**

A hurried strife kindles fire, and a hurried quarrel pours out blood.

**Sirach 28:12**

If you blow into a spark, it will be kindled, and if you spit upon it, it will be quenched; and both come out of your mouth.

**Sirach 28:13**

Curse whisperer and double-tongued one, for they destroyed many who were at peace.

**Sirach 28:14**

A third tongue shook many and separated them from nation to nation, and pulled down fortified cities and overturned houses of great men.

**Sirach 28:15**

A third tongue cast out courageous wives and deprived them of their labors.

**Sirach 28:16**

The one paying attention to it shall surely not find rest, nor will he tabernacle with quietness.

**Sirach 28:17**

A blow of whip makes welts, but a blow of tongue will break bones.

**Sirach 28:18**

Many fell by mouth of sword, and not as those who have fallen through tongue.

**Sirach 28:19**

Blessed is the one sheltered from it, who did not pass through in its anger, who did not draw its yoke and was not bound in its bonds;

**Sirach 28:20**

for its yoke is a yoke of iron, and its bonds are bonds of bronze.

**Sirach 28:21**

Its death is an evil death, and Hades is more profitable than it. [22] It shall surely not master devout ones, and they will not be burned in its flame.

**Sirach 28:23**

Those leaving the Lord will fall into it, and among them it will be kindled and shall surely not be quenched; it will be sent out against them like a lion, and like a leopard it will ruin them.

**Sirach 28:24**

Behold, fence your possession with thorns; bind up your silver and gold;

**Sirach 28:25**

and make a yoke and scale for your words, and make a door and bar for your mouth.

**Sirach 28:26**

Pay attention, lest you slip by it, lest you fall before one lying in wait.

### Chapter 29

**Sirach 29:1**

The one doing mercy will lend to neighbor, and the one strengthening with his hand keeps commandments.

**Sirach 29:2**

Lend to neighbor in time of his need, and again pay back to neighbor at the time.

**Sirach 29:3**

Make firm a word and be faithful with him, and in every time you will find your need.

**Sirach 29:4**

Many considered a loan as a find and gave trouble to those helping them.

**Sirach 29:5**

Until he receives, he will kiss his hand, and over the possessions of his neighbor he will humble his voice; and at time of repayment he will drag out time and repay words of weariness and blame the time.

**Sirach 29:6**

If he is strong, with difficulty he will recover half and count it as a find; but if not, he deprived him of his possessions and acquired him as an enemy for nothing; he will repay him curses and revilings, and instead of glory he will repay him dishonor.

**Sirach 29:7**

Therefore many turned away because of wickedness; they were cautious of being deprived for nothing.

**Sirach 29:8**

Nevertheless be patient toward a lowly man, and do not drag him away from mercy.

**Sirach 29:9**

For sake of commandment help a poor man, and according to his lack do not turn him away empty.

**Sirach 29:10**

Lose silver because of brother and friend, and do not let it rust under the stone to destruction.

**Sirach 29:11**

Set your treasure according to commandments of the Most High, and it will profit you more than gold.

**Sirach 29:12**

Shut up mercy in your storehouses, and it will deliver you from every affliction;

**Sirach 29:13**

more than a shield of might and more than a spear of strength, it will fight for you against an enemy.

**Sirach 29:14**

A good man will give surety for neighbor, and one who has lost shame will leave him behind.

**Sirach 29:15**

Do not forget graces of a surety, for he gave his soul for you.

**Sirach 29:16**

A sinner will overturn good things of a surety, and one ungrateful in thought will abandon the one rescuing him.

**Sirach 29:17**

Surety destroyed many who were directed straight and shook them like a wave of sea; it made powerful men emigrate, and they wandered among foreign nations.

**Sirach 29:19**

A sinner falling into surety and pursuing contracts will fall into judgments.

**Sirach 29:20**

Help neighbor according to your power, and pay attention to yourself, lest you fall.

**Sirach 29:21**

Beginning of life is water and bread and garment and a house covering shamefulness.

**Sirach 29:22**

Better is life of a poor man under shelter of beams than splendid foods among strangers.

**Sirach 29:23**

Have good pleasure over small and great, and you shall surely not hear reproach of sojourning.

**Sirach 29:24**

Life from house to house is evil, and where you sojourn, you will not open mouth.

**Sirach 29:25**

You will host and give drink to ungrateful ones, and besides these things you will hear bitter things:

**Sirach 29:26**

Come here, sojourner, furnish a table, and if anything is in your hand, feed me.

**Sirach 29:27**

Go out, sojourner, from before glory; my brother has been lodged as a guest with me; need of the house.

**Sirach 29:28**

These things are heavy to a man having prudence: rebuke of a house and reproach of a lender.

### Chapter 30

**Sirach 30:1**

Concerning Children. The one loving his son will continue whips for him, so that he may rejoice over his end.

**Sirach 30:2**

The one disciplining his son will profit by him and will boast over him among acquaintances;

**Sirach 30:3**

the one teaching his son will provoke the enemy to jealousy and will exult over him before friends.

**Sirach 30:4**

His father died, and it is as though he did not die; for he left behind one like himself after him.

**Sirach 30:5**

In his life he saw and rejoiced, and at his death he was not grieved.

**Sirach 30:6**

Before enemies he left behind an avenger and one repaying grace to friends.

**Sirach 30:7**

One pampering a son will bind up his wounds, and at every cry his inward parts will be troubled.

**Sirach 30:8**

An untamed horse turns out hard, and a son let loose turns out headstrong.

**Sirach 30:9**

Nurse a child, and he will astound you; play together with him, and he will grieve you.

**Sirach 30:10**

Do not laugh together with him, lest you suffer pain together, and in the end you will gnash your teeth.

**Sirach 30:11**

Do not give him authority in youth; crush his ribs while he is still an infant, lest perhaps when hardened he disobey you. [And do not overlook his ignorances.

**Sirach 30:12**

Bend his neck in youth.]

**Sirach 30:13**

Discipline your son and work on him, so that you may not stumble in his shamefulness. Concerning Health.

**Sirach 30:14**

Better is a poor man healthy and strong in condition than a rich man whipped in his body.

**Sirach 30:15**

Health and good condition are better than all gold, and a strong body than immeasurable wealth.

**Sirach 30:16**

There is no wealth better than health of body, and there is no gladness above joy of heart.

**Sirach 30:17**

Better is death than bitter life, and eternal rest than lasting sickness.

**Sirach 30:18**

Good things poured out upon a closed mouth are food-offerings set beside a tomb.

**Sirach 30:19**

What benefit is fruit-offering to an idol? For it neither eats nor smells; so is the one pursued by the Lord,

**Sirach 30:20**

looking with eyes and groaning, like a eunuch embracing a virgin and groaning.

**Sirach 30:21**

Do not give your soul to grief, and do not afflict yourself in your counsel.

**Sirach 30:22**

Gladness of heart is life of a man, and exultation of a man is lengthening of days.

**Sirach 30:23**

Love your soul and comfort your heart, and put grief far away from you; for grief destroyed many, and there is no profit in it.

**Sirach 30:24**

Jealousy and anger lessen days, and worry brings old age before time.

**Sirach 30:25**

A bright and good heart will care for its foods at meals.

### Chapter 31

**Sirach 31:1**

Sleeplessness of wealth wastes flesh, and its worry drives away sleep.

**Sirach 31:2**

Worry of sleeplessness will demand drowsiness, and severe sickness will sober sleep away.

**Sirach 31:3**

A rich man labored in gathering possessions, and in his rest he fills himself with his luxuries.

**Sirach 31:4**

A poor man labored in lack of livelihood, and in his rest he becomes needy.

**Sirach 31:5**

The one loving gold will not be justified, and the one pursuing corruption will himself be filled.

**Sirach 31:6**

Many were given to a fall for sake of gold, and their destruction came to be before their face.

**Sirach 31:7**

It is wood of stumbling to those excited over it, and every fool will be caught in it.

**Sirach 31:8**

Blessed is a rich man who was found blameless and who did not go after gold.

**Sirach 31:9**

Who is he? And we will call him blessed, for he did wonders among his people.

**Sirach 31:10**

Who was tested by it and was perfected? And it will be for him as a boast. Who was able to transgress and did not transgress, and to do evil things and did not do them?

**Sirach 31:11**

His good things will be made firm, and an assembly will tell out his mercies.

**Sirach 31:12**

You sat at a great table; do not open your throat over it, and do not say, many indeed are the things upon it.

**Sirach 31:13**

Remember that an evil eye is evil. What has been created more evil than an eye? Because of this it sheds tears from every face.

**Sirach 31:14**

Wherever it looks, do not stretch out a hand, and do not crowd with it in the dish.

**Sirach 31:15**

Understand the things of neighbor from yourself, and consider in every matter.

**Sirach 31:16**

Eat like a man the things set beside you, and do not chew greedily, lest you be hated.

**Sirach 31:17**

Stop first for sake of discipline, and do not be insatiable, lest perhaps you stumble;

**Sirach 31:18**

and if you have sat among more people, do not stretch out your hand before them.

**Sirach 31:19**

How sufficient is a little for a disciplined man, and on his bed he does not gasp.

**Sirach 31:20**

Sleep of health is upon a moderate bowel; he rose early, and his soul is with him. Pain of sleeplessness and cholera and twisting are with an insatiable man.

**Sirach 31:21**

And if you were forced with foods, rise in the middle and you will rest.

**Sirach 31:22**

Listen to me, child, and do not despise me, and in the last things you will find my words. In all your works become nimble, and every sickness shall surely not meet you.

**Sirach 31:23**

Lips will bless one splendid over breads, and testimony of his beauty is faithful.

**Sirach 31:24**

A city will mutter about one evil over bread, and testimony of his wickedness is exact.

**Sirach 31:25**

Do not act manly with wine, for wine destroyed many.

**Sirach 31:26**

A furnace tests tempering in dipping; so wine tests hearts in a quarrel of proud men.

**Sirach 31:27**

Wine is equal to life for a man, if you drink it by its measure. What life is there for one lacking wine? And it was created for gladness to men.

**Sirach 31:28**

Exultation of heart and gladness of soul is wine drunk in season, sufficient.

**Sirach 31:29**

Bitterness of soul is wine drunk much in irritation and stumbling.

**Sirach 31:30**

Drunkenness multiplies anger of a fool for stumbling, lessening strength and making wounds.

**Sirach 31:31**

At a banquet of wine do not reprove your neighbor, and do not despise him in his gladness. Do not say a word of reproach to him, and do not afflict him with a demand.

### Chapter 32

**Sirach 32:1**

Concerning Leaders. Have they appointed you a leader? Do not lift yourself up. Be among them as one of them; take thought for them and then sit down.

**Sirach 32:2**

And after doing all your need, recline, so that you may rejoice because of them and receive a crown for sake of good order.

**Sirach 32:3**

Speak, elder, for it is fitting for you, with exact knowledge, and do not hinder music.

**Sirach 32:4**

Where there is a hearing, do not pour out chatter, and do not display wisdom out of season.

**Sirach 32:5**

A seal of carbuncle upon gold ornament is a concert of music at a banquet of wine.

**Sirach 32:6**

A seal of emerald in a gold setting is a musical melody with sweet wine.

**Sirach 32:7**

Speak, young man, if you have need, hardly twice if you are asked.

**Sirach 32:8**

Summarize speech: in few things, many. Be as one knowing and at the same time keeping silent.

**Sirach 32:9**

In the midst of great men do not make yourself equal, and while another speaks do not babble many things.

**Sirach 32:10**

Before thunder lightning hastens, and before a modest man grace will go forth.

**Sirach 32:11**

Rise up at the hour and do not lag behind; run off to home and do not be idle.

**Sirach 32:12**

There play and do your thoughts, and do not sin by a proud word.

**Sirach 32:13**

And for these things bless the one who made you and makes you drunk from his good things.

**Sirach 32:14**

The one fearing the Lord will receive discipline, and those rising early will find good pleasure.

**Sirach 32:15**

The one seeking law will be filled with it, and the one pretending will be stumbled by it.

**Sirach 32:16**

Those fearing the Lord will find judgment, and they will kindle righteous acts like light.

**Sirach 32:17**

A sinful man turns aside reproof, and according to his will he will find a comparison.

**Sirach 32:18**

A man of counsel shall surely not overlook thought; a stranger and proud man will not be cowed by fear, even after doing with him without counsel.

**Sirach 32:19**

Do nothing without counsel, and when you do it, do not regret.

**Sirach 32:20**

Do not go on a way of stumbling, and do not stumble among stony places.

**Sirach 32:21**

Do not trust in an unstumbling way,

**Sirach 32:22**

and guard yourself from your children.

**Sirach 32:23**

In every work trust your soul, for this also is keeping commandments.

**Sirach 32:24**

The one trusting law pays attention to commandments, and the one trusting the Lord will not be lessened.

### Chapter 33

**Sirach 33:1**

To the one fearing the Lord no evil will meet, but in trial he will also deliver again.

**Sirach 33:2**

A wise man will not hate law, but the one pretending in it is like a ship in storm.

**Sirach 33:3**

An understanding man will trust law, and the law is faithful to him as an oracle of clear things.

**Sirach 33:4**

Prepare speech and so you will be heard; bind together discipline and answer.

**Sirach 33:5**
