A wagon wheel is the inward parts of a fool, and his reasoning is like a turning axle.

**Sirach 33:6**

A horse for breeding is like a mocking friend; under every rider it neighs.

**Sirach 33:7**

Why does day excel day, and all light of a day of a year is from the sun?

**Sirach 33:8**

By knowledge of the Lord they were separated, and he altered seasons and feasts.

**Sirach 33:9**

From them he lifted up and sanctified, and from them he set into a number of days.

**Sirach 33:10**

And all men are from ground, and Adam was created from earth.

**Sirach 33:11**

In abundance of knowledge the Lord separated them and altered their ways.

**Sirach 33:12**

Some of them he blessed and lifted up, and some of them he sanctified and brought near to himself; some of them he cursed and humbled and turned them back from their station.

**Sirach 33:13**

As clay of a potter is in his hand, all his ways according to his good pleasure, so men are in the hand of the one who made them, to repay them according to his judgment.

**Sirach 33:14**

Opposite evil is good, and opposite death is life; so opposite a devout man is a sinner.

**Sirach 33:15**

And so look into all works of the Most High, two by two, one opposite the one.

**Sirach 33:16**

And I, last, kept watch like one gleaning behind grape-gatherers;

**Sirach 33:17**

by blessing of the Lord I arrived first, and like one gathering grapes I filled a winepress.

**Sirach 33:18**

Understand that I did not labor for myself alone, but for all those seeking discipline.

**Sirach 33:19**

Listen to me, great men of people, and you leaders of an assembly, give ear.

**Sirach 33:20**

To son and wife, brother and friend, do not give authority over you in your life; and do not give your possessions to another, lest when you regret, you make petition concerning them.

**Sirach 33:21**

While you still live and breath is in you, do not exchange yourself with any flesh.

**Sirach 33:22**

For it is better that your children should petition you than that you should look to hands of your sons.

**Sirach 33:23**

In all your works become surpassing; do not give a blemish in your glory.

**Sirach 33:24**

On the day of completion of days of your life and in time of death, distribute inheritance. Concerning Servants.

**Sirach 33:25**

Fodder and rod and loads for a donkey; bread and discipline and work for a servant.

**Sirach 33:26**

Work with a servant, and you will find rest; loosen hands for him, and he will seek freedom.

**Sirach 33:27**

Yoke and strap will bend a neck, and for a wicked servant are racks and tortures.

**Sirach 33:28**

Put him into work, so that he may not be idle, for idleness taught much evil.

**Sirach 33:29**

Set him to works, as is fitting for him, and if he does not obey, make his fetters heavy.

**Sirach 33:30**

And do not be excessive with any flesh, and without judgment do nothing.

**Sirach 33:31**

If you have a servant, let him be as you, because with blood you acquired him.

**Sirach 33:32**

If you have a servant, lead him as a brother, because as your own soul you will need him.

**Sirach 33:33**

If you harm him and he rises up and runs away, on what road will you seek him?

### Chapter 34

**Sirach 34:1**

Empty and false hopes are for a man without understanding, and dreams give wings to fools.

**Sirach 34:2**

Like one grasping shadow and pursuing wind, so is the one holding fast to dreams.

**Sirach 34:3**

This according to that is vision of dreams, likeness of face opposite face.

**Sirach 34:4**

From an unclean thing, what will be cleansed? And from a false thing, what will be true?

**Sirach 34:5**

Divinations and omens and dreams are vain, and a heart imagines like one laboring in birth.

**Sirach 34:6**

Unless it is sent from the Most High in visitation, do not give your heart to them;

**Sirach 34:7**

for dreams led many astray, and those hoping in them fell out.

**Sirach 34:8**

Without falsehood law will be completed, and wisdom is perfection to a faithful mouth.

**Sirach 34:9**

A disciplined man knew many things, and the one of much experience will tell out understanding.

**Sirach 34:10**

One who was not tried knows few things, but the one who has wandered will multiply craftiness.

**Sirach 34:11**

I have seen many things in my wandering, and my understanding is more than my words.

**Sirach 34:12**

More than once I was endangered even to death, and I was delivered because of these things.

**Sirach 34:13**

Spirit of those fearing the Lord will live, for their hope is upon the one saving them.

**Sirach 34:14**

The one fearing the Lord will be cautious of nothing and shall surely not be cowardly, because he is his hope.

**Sirach 34:15**

Blessed is soul of one fearing the Lord. To whom does he hold fast, and who is his support?

**Sirach 34:16**

The eyes of the Lord are upon those loving him: a defense of power and support of strength, shelter from burning heat and shelter from midday, guard from stumbling and help from falling,

**Sirach 34:17**

lifting up soul and enlightening eyes, giving healing, life, and blessing.

**Sirach 34:18**

One sacrificing from unrighteousness, a mocked offering; and gifts of lawless ones are not for good pleasure.

**Sirach 34:19**

The Most High does not take pleasure in offerings of ungodly ones, nor is he atoned for sins by multitude of sacrifices.

**Sirach 34:20**

One offering a sacrifice from possessions of poor men is sacrificing a son before his father.

**Sirach 34:21**

Bread of those in need is life of poor men; the one depriving them of it is a man of blood.

**Sirach 34:22**

The one taking away livelihood murders neighbor, and the one depriving a hired man of wages pours out blood.

**Sirach 34:23**

One building and one tearing down; what did they profit more than labors?

**Sirach 34:24**

One praying and one cursing; to whose voice will the master listen?

**Sirach 34:25**

One baptized from a dead body and touching it again, what did he profit by his washing?

**Sirach 34:26**

So is a man fasting over his sins and going again and doing the same things. Who will listen to his prayer? And what did he profit by humbling himself?

### Chapter 35

**Sirach 35:1**

The one keeping law multiplies offerings; the one attending to commandments sacrifices a peace offering.

**Sirach 35:2**

One repaying grace offers fine flour, and the one doing mercy sacrifices praise.

**Sirach 35:3**

Good pleasure of the Lord is to depart from wickedness, and atonement is to depart from unrighteousness.

**Sirach 35:4**

Do not appear before the face of the Lord empty, for all these things are for sake of commandment.

**Sirach 35:5**

Offering of a righteous man fattens an altar, and its sweet odor is before the Most High.

**Sirach 35:6**

Sacrifice of a righteous man is acceptable, and its memorial will not be forgotten.

**Sirach 35:7**

With a good eye glorify the Lord, and do not diminish firstfruit of your hands.

**Sirach 35:8**

With every gift make your face cheerful, and with gladness sanctify a tithe.

**Sirach 35:9**

Give to the Most High according to his gift and with a good eye according to find of hand;

**Sirach 35:10**

because the Lord is one repaying, and he will repay you sevenfold.

**Sirach 35:11**

Do not bribe, for he will not accept it;

**Sirach 35:12**

and do not rely on an unrighteous sacrifice, because the Lord is judge, and there is no glory of face with him.

**Sirach 35:13**

He will not take face against a poor man, and he will listen to petition of one wronged.

**Sirach 35:14**

He shall surely not overlook supplication of an orphan, and a widow, if she pours out speech.

**Sirach 35:15**

Do not tears of a widow go down upon her cheek, and her cry against the one who brought them down?

**Sirach 35:16**

One serving in good pleasure will be accepted, and his petition will reach as far as clouds.

**Sirach 35:17**

Prayer of a lowly one passed through clouds, and until it draws near, he shall surely not be comforted;

**Sirach 35:18**

and it shall surely not depart until the Most High visits. And he will judge righteously and make judgment.

**Sirach 35:19**

And the Lord shall surely not delay, nor shall he be patient with them,

**Sirach 35:20**

until he crushes the loin of merciless ones and repays vengeance to nations,

**Sirach 35:21**

until he removes a multitude of arrogant ones and crushes scepters of unrighteous ones,

**Sirach 35:22**

until he repays a man according to his practices and works of men according to their thoughts,

**Sirach 35:23**

until he judges the judgment of his people, and he will gladden them in his mercy.

**Sirach 35:24**

Beautiful is mercy in time of his affliction, like clouds of rain in time of drought.

### Chapter 36

**Sirach 36:1**

Have mercy on us, Master, God of all, and look upon us and cast your fear upon all nations.

**Sirach 36:2**

Lift up your hand against foreign nations, and let them see your power.

**Sirach 36:3**

Just as before them you were sanctified among us, so before us may you be magnified among them;

**Sirach 36:4**

and let them recognize you, just as we also recognized, because there is no God besides you, Lord.

**Sirach 36:5**

Renew signs and alter wonders; glorify hand and right arm.

**Sirach 36:6**

Raise anger and pour out wrath; remove adversary and wipe out enemy.

**Sirach 36:7**

Hasten a time and remember oath, and let them tell out your great deeds.

**Sirach 36:8**

Let the one being saved be devoured in wrath of fire, and may those harming your people find destruction.

**Sirach 36:9**

Crush heads of rulers of enemies who say, There is none besides us.

**Sirach 36:10**

Gather all tribes of Jacob, and inherit them as from beginning.

**Sirach 36:11**

Have mercy on people, Lord, called by your name, and Israel, whom you likened to a firstborn.

**Sirach 36:12**

Pity city of your sanctuary, Jerusalem, city of your resting-place.

**Sirach 36:13**

Fill Zion with your praise, and your people with your glory.

**Sirach 36:14**

Give testimony to those who were your creation in beginning, and raise up prophecies upon your name.

**Sirach 36:15**

Give reward to those waiting for you, and let your prophets be trusted.

**Sirach 36:16**

Listen, Lord, to petition of your suppliants, according to blessing of Aaron concerning your people,

**Sirach 36:17**

and all those upon earth will know that you are Lord, the God of the ages.

**Sirach 36:18**

A belly will eat every food, but there is food better than food.

**Sirach 36:19**

A throat tastes foods of game; so an understanding heart tastes false words.

**Sirach 36:20**

A twisted heart will give grief, and a man of much experience will repay him.

**Sirach 36:21**

A woman will receive every male, but there is daughter better than daughter.

**Sirach 36:22**

Beauty of a woman cheers a face and surpasses every desire of man.

**Sirach 36:23**

If mercy and meekness are upon her tongue, her husband is not according to sons of men.

**Sirach 36:24**

The one acquiring a wife begins possession, a helper according to him and pillar of rest.

**Sirach 36:25**

Where there is no fence, possession will be plundered; and where there is no wife, he will groan, wandering.

**Sirach 36:26**

For who will trust a well-girded robber stumbling from city to city?

**Sirach 36:27**

So with a man who has no nest and lodges wherever he grows dark.

### Chapter 37

**Sirach 37:1**

Every friend will say, I also was friendly to him, but there is a friend who is a friend in name only.

**Sirach 37:2**

Is there not grief in it until death, a companion and friend turning into hostility?

**Sirach 37:3**

O wicked thought, from where did you roll in to cover dry land with deceitfulness?

**Sirach 37:4**

A companion of a friend delights in gladness, and in time of affliction he will be opposite.

**Sirach 37:5**

A companion labors together with a friend for sake of belly; before war he will take a shield.

**Sirach 37:6**

Do not forget a friend in your soul, and do not forget him in your possessions.

**Sirach 37:7**

Every counselor lifts up counsel, but there is one counseling for himself.

**Sirach 37:8**

Guard your soul from a counselor, and know beforehand what his need is; for he also will counsel for himself, lest perhaps he cast lot upon you

**Sirach 37:9**

and say to you, Your way is good, and stand opposite to see what will happen to you.

**Sirach 37:10**

Do not take counsel with one looking askance at you, and from those envying you hide counsel.

**Sirach 37:11**

Do not rely for every counsel with a woman concerning her rival, and with a coward concerning war, with a merchant concerning exchange and with a buyer concerning sale, with an envious man concerning thanksgiving and with a merciless man concerning kindness, with a lazy man concerning every work and with a hired man by the year concerning completion, with an idle servant concerning much work; do not rely on these for any counsel.

**Sirach 37:12**

But continue with a devout man, whom you know to be keeping commandments, who in his soul is according to your soul, and if you stumble, he will suffer pain with you.

**Sirach 37:13**

And establish counsel of heart, for there is none more faithful to you than it.

**Sirach 37:14**

For a man's soul is sometimes accustomed to report more than seven watchmen sitting high upon a watch.

**Sirach 37:15**

And above all these things make petition to the Most High, so that he may direct your way in truth.

**Sirach 37:16**

Beginning of every work is word, and before every action, counsel.

**Sirach 37:17**

As track of alteration of heart, four parts arise:

**Sirach 37:18**

good and evil, life and death; and tongue is continually ruling over them.

**Sirach 37:19**

There is a crafty man, instructor of many, and to his own soul he is useless.

**Sirach 37:20**

There is one displaying wisdom in words, hated; this one will lack every food,

**Sirach 37:21**

for grace was not given him from the Lord, because he was deprived of every wisdom.

**Sirach 37:22**

There is one wise for his own soul, and fruits of his understanding are faithful upon mouth.

**Sirach 37:23**

A wise man will discipline his own people, and fruits of his understanding are faithful.

**Sirach 37:24**

A wise man will be filled with blessing, and all those seeing him will call him blessed.

**Sirach 37:25**

Life of a man is in number of days, and days of Israel are without number.

**Sirach 37:26**

The wise one among his people will inherit faithfulness, and his name will live into the age.

**Sirach 37:27**

Child, in your life test your soul and see what is evil for it, and do not give it to it;

**Sirach 37:28**

for not all things are profitable for all, and not every soul takes pleasure in every thing.

**Sirach 37:29**

Do not be insatiable in every luxury, and do not pour yourself out upon foods;

**Sirach 37:30**

for in many foods there will be pain, and insatiability will draw near as far as cholera.

**Sirach 37:31**

Because of insatiability many died, but the one paying attention will add life.

### Chapter 38

**Sirach 38:1**

Honor a physician according to his needs with his honors, for the Lord also created him;

**Sirach 38:2**

for healing is from the Most High, and from a king he will receive a gift.

**Sirach 38:3**

Knowledge of a physician will lift up his head, and before great men he will be marveled at.

**Sirach 38:4**

The Lord created medicines from earth, and a prudent man will not be irritated by them.

**Sirach 38:5**

Was not water sweetened by wood, so that his strength might be known?

**Sirach 38:6**

And he gave knowledge to men to be glorified in his wonders.

**Sirach 38:7**

With these he healed and removed his pain;

**Sirach 38:8**

a perfumer will make a mixture with these, and his works shall surely not come to an end, and peace from him is upon the face of the earth.

**Sirach 38:9**

Child, in your sickness do not overlook it, but pray to the Lord, and he will heal you.

**Sirach 38:10**

Put away trespass and make hands straight, and cleanse heart from every sin.

**Sirach 38:11**

Give sweet odor and memorial of fine flour, and fatten an offering as one not existing.

**Sirach 38:12**

And give place to a physician, for the Lord also created him, and do not let him depart from you, for there is also need of him.

**Sirach 38:13**

There is a time when success is also in their hands;

**Sirach 38:14**

for they also will make petition to the Lord, so that he may prosper them with rest and healing for sake of livelihood.

**Sirach 38:15**

The one sinning before the one who made him, may he fall into hands of a physician.

**Sirach 38:16**

Child, bring down tears over a dead man, and as one suffering terrible things begin lamentation; but according to his judgment wrap his body, and do not overlook his burial.

**Sirach 38:17**

Make weeping bitter and make wailing hot, and make mourning according to his worth, one day and two for sake of slander, and be comforted because of grief;

**Sirach 38:18**

for from grief death comes out, and grief of heart will bend strength.

**Sirach 38:19**

In calamity grief also passes by, and life of a poor man is against heart.

**Sirach 38:20**

Do not give your heart to grief; put it away, remembering the last things.

**Sirach 38:21**

Do not forget; for there is no return, and you will not profit this one, and you will harm yourself.

**Sirach 38:22**

Remember his judgment, because so also is yours: yesterday for me and today for you.

**Sirach 38:23**

When a dead man is at rest, put his memorial to rest, and be comforted in him at the departure of his spirit.

**Sirach 38:24**

Wisdom of a scribe is in opportunity of leisure, and one lessened in his business will become wise.

**Sirach 38:25**

How will one become wise who holds a plow and boasts in a spear of goad, driving oxen and turning about in their works, and whose tale is among sons of bulls?

**Sirach 38:26**

He will give his heart to give out furrows, and his sleeplessness is for fodder of heifers.

**Sirach 38:27**

So every craftsman and architect, who spends night as day; those carving engravings of seals, and his endurance is to alter variety; he will give his heart to liken a painting, and his sleeplessness is to complete work.

**Sirach 38:28**

So a bronze-worker sitting near an anvil and studying works of iron; vapor of fire will fix his flesh, and in heat of furnace he will struggle; sound of hammer will renew his ear, and his eyes are opposite likeness of vessel; he will give his heart to completion of works, and his sleeplessness is to adorn them to completion.

**Sirach 38:29**

So a potter sitting at his work and turning a wheel with his feet, who lies in worry continually over his work, and all his work is by number.

**Sirach 38:30**

With his arm he will shape clay, and before his feet he will bend his strength; he will give his heart to complete the coating, and his sleeplessness is to clean a furnace.

**Sirach 38:31**

All these trusted in their hands, and each becomes wise in his own work.

**Sirach 38:32**

Without them a city will not be inhabited, and people will neither sojourn nor walk about;

**Sirach 38:33**

but they will not be sought in counsel of people, and in assembly they will not be exalted. They will not sit upon a judge's seat, and they will not understand covenant of judgment, nor shall they make righteousness and judgment appear, and they will not be found among parables,

**Sirach 38:34**

but they will establish creation of an age, and their petition is in work of craft.

### Chapter 39

**Sirach 39:1**

Except for the one who gives his soul and considers in law of the Most High, he will seek out wisdom of all ancients and be occupied in prophecies.

**Sirach 39:2**

He will preserve tales of named men and enter together into turns of parables.

**Sirach 39:3**

He will seek out hidden things of proverbs and turn about in riddles of parables.

**Sirach 39:4**

He will serve among great men and be seen before a leader; he will pass through a land of foreign nations, for he tested good and evil among men.

**Sirach 39:5**

He will give his heart to rise early toward the Lord who made him and will make petition before the Most High. And he will open his mouth in prayer and make petition concerning his sins.

**Sirach 39:6**

If the great Lord wills, he will be filled with a spirit of understanding; he himself will rain down words of his wisdom, and in prayer he will confess to the Lord.

**Sirach 39:7**

He himself will direct his counsel and knowledge, and he will consider in his hidden things.

**Sirach 39:8**

He himself will make discipline of his teaching appear and will boast in law of covenant of the Lord.

**Sirach 39:9**

Many will praise his understanding; until the age it will not be wiped out. His memorial will not depart, and his name will live to generations of generations.

**Sirach 39:10**

Nations will tell of his wisdom, and an assembly will declare his praise.

**Sirach 39:11**

If he remains, he will leave a name more than a thousand; and if he rests, he will add to it.

**Sirach 39:12**

Still, having considered, I will tell out, and like a full moon I was filled.

**Sirach 39:13**

Listen to me, holy sons, and sprout like a rose growing upon a stream of water,

**Sirach 39:14**

and like frankincense give sweet smell of odor, and flower a flower like a lily. Spread odor and praise a song; bless the Lord for all his works.

**Sirach 39:15**

Give greatness to his name and confess in his praise, with songs of lips and with lyres, and so you will say in confession:

**Sirach 39:16**

All works of the Lord are very good, and every command will be in its time. It is not possible to say, What is this? For what is this?

**Sirach 39:17**

For all things will be sought in their time. By his word water stood as a heap, and by saying of his mouth, reservoirs of waters.

**Sirach 39:18**

In his command is all good pleasure, and there is none who will lessen his salvation.

**Sirach 39:19**

Works of all flesh are before him, and it is not possible to be hidden from his eyes.

**Sirach 39:20**

From age to age he looked upon things, and nothing is marvelous before him.

**Sirach 39:21**

It is not possible to say, What is this? For what is this? For all things were created for their needs.

**Sirach 39:22**

His blessing covered over like a river, and like a flood made dry land drunk.

**Sirach 39:23**

So his wrath will inherit nations, as he turned waters into brine.

**Sirach 39:24**

His ways are straight for devout ones, so they are stumbling blocks for lawless ones.

**Sirach 39:25**

Good things were created for good ones from beginning, so evil things for sinners.

**Sirach 39:26**

Beginning of every need for life of man: water, fire, and iron and salt and fine flour of wheat and honey and milk, blood of grape and olive oil and garment.

**Sirach 39:27**

All these are for good things to the devout, so to sinners they will be turned into evil things.

**Sirach 39:28**

There are spirits that were created for vengeance, and in his anger they made their whips firm; in time of completion they will pour out strength and bring to rest the anger of the one who made them.

**Sirach 39:29**

Fire and hail and famine and death, all these were created for vengeance;

**Sirach 39:30**

teeth of beasts and scorpions and vipers and a sword taking vengeance for destruction of ungodly ones.

**Sirach 39:31**

They will rejoice in his command, and upon earth they will be made ready for needs, and in their times they will not transgress a word.

**Sirach 39:32**

Because of this, from beginning I was established and considered and left it in writing:

**Sirach 39:33**

All works of the Lord are good, and he will supply every need in its hour.

**Sirach 39:34**

And it is not possible to say, This is more evil than that, for all things will be approved in their time.

**Sirach 39:35**

And now with all heart and mouth, sing praise and bless the name of the Lord.

### Chapter 40

**Sirach 40:1**

Great occupation has been created for every man, and a heavy yoke upon sons of Adam, from day of going out from womb of their mother until day of return to mother of all:

**Sirach 40:2**

their reasonings and fear of heart, thought of expectation, day of death.

**Sirach 40:3**

From one sitting on a throne in glory even to one humbled in earth and ashes,

**Sirach 40:4**

from one wearing blue and crown even to one wrapped in raw linen,

**Sirach 40:5**

anger and jealousy and disturbance and tossing and fear of death and rage and strife; and in time of rest upon a bed, sleep of night alters his knowledge.

**Sirach 40:6**

A little as nothing in rest, and from there in sleep as in day of watch, troubled in vision of his heart, as one escaped from face of war.

**Sirach 40:7**

In time of his salvation he woke up and marveled into no fear.

**Sirach 40:8**

With all flesh, from man to beast, and upon sinners sevenfold besides these:

**Sirach 40:9**

death and blood and strife and sword, calamities, famine and crushing and whip;

**Sirach 40:10**

upon lawless ones all these were created, and because of them the flood came to be.

**Sirach 40:11**

All things, as many as are from earth, return into earth, and from waters turn back into sea.

**Sirach 40:12**

Every gift and unrighteousness will be wiped out, and faithfulness will stand into the age.

**Sirach 40:13**

Possessions of unrighteous ones will be dried up like a river, and like great thunder in rain it will echo out.

**Sirach 40:14**

When he opens hands he will rejoice; so transgressors will fail at completion.

**Sirach 40:15**

Offspring of ungodly ones will not multiply branches, and unclean roots are upon a sharp rock;

**Sirach 40:16**

reed upon every water and lip of river will be plucked out before every grass.

**Sirach 40:17**

Grace is like a garden in blessings, and mercy remains into the age.

**Sirach 40:18**

Life of a self-sufficient worker will be sweetened, and above both is the one finding treasure.

**Sirach 40:19**

Children and building of a city establish a name, and above both is counted a blameless wife.

**Sirach 40:20**

Wine and music cheer heart, and above both is love of wisdom.

**Sirach 40:21**

Flute and psaltery sweeten honey, and above both is a sweet tongue.

**Sirach 40:22**

Your eye will desire grace and beauty, and above both green shoot of seed.

**Sirach 40:23**

Friend and companion meet for a time, and above both is wife with husband.

**Sirach 40:24**

Brothers and help are for time of affliction, and above both mercy will rescue.

**Sirach 40:25**

Gold and silver will set foot firm, and above both counsel is approved.

**Sirach 40:26**

Possessions and strength will lift up heart, and above both is fear of the Lord. There is no lack in fear of the Lord, and there is no need to seek help in it.

**Sirach 40:27**

Fear of the Lord is like a garden of blessing, and above all glory it covered him.

**Sirach 40:28**

Child, do not live a life of begging; better to die than to beg.

**Sirach 40:29**

A man looking to a stranger's table, his livelihood is not in the reckoning of life; he will defile his soul with strangers' foods. But a knowledgeable and disciplined man will guard himself.

**Sirach 40:30**

Begging will be sweetened in mouth of a shameless man, and in his belly fire will burn.

### Chapter 41

**Sirach 41:1**

O death, how bitter your memorial is to a man at peace in his possessions, to a man undistracted and prospering in all things and still strong to receive food.

**Sirach 41:2**

O death, your judgment is good to a man in need and lacking strength, aged at the end and distracted about all things and disobedient and having lost endurance.

**Sirach 41:3**

Do not be cautious of judgment of death; remember those before you and last things.

**Sirach 41:4**

This is the judgment from the Lord for all flesh, and why do you reject the good pleasure of the Most High? Whether ten or a hundred or a thousand years, there is no reproof of life in Hades.

**Sirach 41:5**

Abominable children become children of sinners and those living together in sojournings of ungodly ones.

**Sirach 41:6**

Inheritance of children of sinners will perish, and with their seed reproach will continue.

**Sirach 41:7**

Children will blame an ungodly father, because through him they will be reproached.

**Sirach 41:8**

Woe to you, ungodly men, who have forsaken law of God Most High.

**Sirach 41:9**

And if you are born, you will be born for a curse, and if you die, you will be allotted to a curse.

**Sirach 41:10**

All things, as many as are from earth, will depart into earth; so ungodly ones from curse into destruction.

**Sirach 41:11**

Mourning of men is in their bodies, but name of sinners, not good, will be wiped out.

**Sirach 41:12**

Take thought concerning a name, for it remains for you more than a thousand great treasures of gold.

**Sirach 41:13**

A good life is a number of days, but a good name will remain into an age.

**Sirach 41:14**

Preserve discipline in peace, children. But hidden wisdom and unseen treasure, what profit is in both?

**Sirach 41:15**

Better is a man hiding his foolishness than a man hiding his wisdom.

**Sirach 41:16**

Therefore be ashamed at my word; for it is not good to guard every shame, and not all things are approved by all in faithfulness.

**Sirach 41:17**

Be ashamed before father and mother concerning fornication, and before leader and ruler concerning falsehood,

**Sirach 41:18**

before judge and ruler concerning trespass, before congregation and people concerning lawlessness,

**Sirach 41:19**

before partner and friend concerning unrighteousness, and before the place where you sojourn concerning theft,

**Sirach 41:20**

before truth of God and covenant, and before setting an elbow upon breads,

**Sirach 41:21**

before contempt in taking and giving, and before those greeting you concerning silence,

**Sirach 41:22**

before sight of a prostitute woman, and before turning away face of a relative,

**Sirach 41:23**

before taking away a portion and gift, and before paying attention to a married woman,

**Sirach 41:24**

before meddling with his maidservant, and do not stand by her bed;

**Sirach 41:25**

before friends concerning words of reproach, and after giving, do not reproach,

**Sirach 41:26**

before repeating a report and word of hearing, and before revelations of hidden words;

**Sirach 41:27**

and you will be truly modest and finding grace before every man.

### Chapter 42

**Sirach 42:1**

Do not be ashamed concerning these things, and do not accept a face for sinning:

**Sirach 42:2**

concerning law of the Most High and covenant, and concerning judgment to justify an ungodly man,

**Sirach 42:3**

concerning word of partner and travelers, and concerning giving of inheritance of companions,

**Sirach 42:4**

concerning exactness of balance and weights, concerning possession of many and few things,

**Sirach 42:5**

concerning profit of selling by merchants, and concerning much discipline of children and making a wicked servant's side bleed.

**Sirach 42:6**

For a wicked wife a seal is good, and where there are many hands, lock up.

**Sirach 42:7**

Whatever you hand over, let it be by number and weight, and giving and receiving, all in writing.

**Sirach 42:8**

Concerning discipline of a senseless man and fool, and one aged at the end being judged with young men; and you will be truly disciplined and tested before every living one.

**Sirach 42:9**

A daughter is secret sleeplessness to a father, and care for her drives away sleep: in her youth, lest perhaps she pass her prime; and having lived with a husband, lest perhaps she be hated;

**Sirach 42:10**

in virginity, lest perhaps she be defiled and become pregnant in her father's house; being with a husband, lest perhaps she transgress; and having lived with him, lest perhaps she be barren.

**Sirach 42:11**

Set firm guard over an unrestrained daughter, lest perhaps she make you a rejoicing-stock to enemies, chatter in a city and summons of people, and put you to shame among a multitude of many.

**Sirach 42:12**

Do not look at beauty in every man, and do not sit together among women;

**Sirach 42:13**

for from garments a moth comes out, and from a woman wickedness of a woman.

**Sirach 42:14**

Better is wickedness of a man than a woman doing good, and a woman putting to shame into reproach.

**Sirach 42:15**

Indeed I will remember works of the Lord, and what I have seen I will tell out; by words of the Lord are his works.

**Sirach 42:16**

The sun giving light looked upon everything, and his work is full of his glory.

**Sirach 42:17**

The Lord did not give power to the holy ones to tell out all his wonders, which the Lord Almighty made firm, that the whole might be established in his glory.

**Sirach 42:18**

He searched out abyss and heart and considered in their craftinesses; for the Lord knew every knowledge and looked into sign of age,

**Sirach 42:19**

reporting things that have passed and things coming, and revealing tracks of hidden things.

**Sirach 42:20**

No thought passed him by; not even one word was hidden from him.

**Sirach 42:21**

He adorned the great things of his wisdom, and as he is before the age and into the age; neither was anything added nor lessened, and he had no need of any counselor.

**Sirach 42:22**

How desirable are all his works, and how they are to behold like a spark.

**Sirach 42:23**

All these live and remain into the age for every need, and all obey.

**Sirach 42:24**

All things are double, one opposite the one, and he made nothing lacking.

**Sirach 42:25**

One strengthened good things of the other, and who will be filled while seeing his glory?

### Chapter 43

**Sirach 43:1**

Boasting of height is firm span of purity, appearance of heaven in vision of glory.

**Sirach 43:2**

The sun in appearance declares at its going out, a wonderful vessel, work of the Most High.

**Sirach 43:3**

At its midday it dries up land, and before its burning heat who will stand?

**Sirach 43:4**

One blowing a furnace in works of heat; threefold is sun burning up mountains, breathing out fiery vapors and shining beams, dimming eyes.

**Sirach 43:5**

Great is the Lord who made it, and by his words it hastened its course.

**Sirach 43:6**

And the moon is in all things for its time, display of times and sign of age.

**Sirach 43:7**

From the moon is sign of feast, a light-bearer diminishing toward completion.

**Sirach 43:8**

Month is according to her name, increasing wonderfully in change, vessel of camps in height, shining in firm span of heaven.

**Sirach 43:9**

Beauty of heaven, glory of stars, an ornament giving light in the highest places of the Lord.

**Sirach 43:10**

By words of the Holy One they will stand according to judgment and shall surely not grow weak in their watches.

**Sirach 43:11**

Look at the bow and bless the one who made it, exceedingly beautiful in its brightness.

**Sirach 43:12**

It circled heaven in a circle of glory; hands of the Most High stretched it out.

**Sirach 43:13**

By his command he hastened snow and speeds lightning flashes of his judgment.

**Sirach 43:14**

Because of this treasures were opened, and clouds flew out like birds.

**Sirach 43:15**

By his majesty he strengthened clouds, and stones of hail were broken small.

**Sirach 43:16**

And at his appearances mountains will be shaken; by his will south wind will blow.

**Sirach 43:17**

Voice of his thunder made earth labor, and storm of north wind and whirlwind of spirit.

**Sirach 43:18**

Like birds flying down he scatters snow, and its descent is like locust settling; eye will marvel at beauty of its whiteness, and heart will be astonished at its rain.

**Sirach 43:19**

And he pours frost like salt upon earth, and when frozen it becomes points of stakes.

**Sirach 43:20**

Cold north wind will blow, and crystal will freeze upon water; it will settle upon every gathering of water, and water will put on as a breastplate.

**Sirach 43:21**

It will devour mountains and burn up wilderness and extinguish green grass like fire.

**Sirach 43:22**

Healing of all things is mist in haste; dew meeting burning heat will cheer.

**Sirach 43:23**

By his reasoning he stilled abyss and planted islands in it.

**Sirach 43:24**

Those sailing the sea tell of its danger, and by hearings of our ears we marvel.

**Sirach 43:25**

And there are strange and wonderful works, variety of every living creature, creation of sea-monsters.

**Sirach 43:26**

Because of him his messenger prospers, and by his word all things hold together.

**Sirach 43:27**

We will say many things and shall surely not reach; and completion of words: the whole is he.

**Sirach 43:28**

While glorifying, where will we have strength? For he himself is the great one beyond all his works.

**Sirach 43:29**

Fearsome is the Lord and exceedingly great, and wonderful is his power.

**Sirach 43:30**

While glorifying the Lord, lift him high as much as you are able, for he will exceed still more. And while lifting him high, multiply in strength; do not labor, for you shall surely not reach.

**Sirach 43:31**

Who has seen him and will tell out? And who will magnify him as he is?

**Sirach 43:32**

Many hidden things are greater than these, for we have seen few of his works.

**Sirach 43:33**

For the Lord made all things, and to the devout he gave wisdom.

### Chapter 44

**Sirach 44:1**

Hymn of Fathers. Let us indeed praise glorious men and our fathers by generation.

**Sirach 44:2**

The Lord created much glory, his greatness from age.

**Sirach 44:3**

Those ruling in their kingdoms, and men named in power, counseling in their understanding, reporting in prophecies;

**Sirach 44:4**

leaders of people in deliberations and understanding of scribal work of people, wise words in their discipline;

**Sirach 44:5**

those seeking out melodies of music and telling verses in writing;

**Sirach 44:6**

rich men furnished with strength, being at peace in their sojournings;

**Sirach 44:7**

all these were glorified in generations, and in their days were a boast.

**Sirach 44:8**

Some of them left a name, so that praises might be told out;

**Sirach 44:9**

and there are some of whom there is no memorial, and they perished as not existing, and became as not having come to be, and their children after them.

**Sirach 44:10**

But these are men of mercy, whose righteous acts were not forgotten.

**Sirach 44:11**

With their seed a good inheritance will remain, their offspring.

**Sirach 44:12**

In the covenants their seed stood, and their children because of them.

**Sirach 44:13**

Their seed will remain until age, and their glory will not be wiped out.

**Sirach 44:14**

Their bodies were buried in peace, and their name lives to generations.

**Sirach 44:15**

Peoples will tell of their wisdom, and an assembly declares their praise.

**Sirach 44:16**

Enoch was well-pleasing to the Lord and was transferred, an example of repentance to generations.

**Sirach 44:17**

Noah was found complete, righteous; in time of wrath he became an exchange. Because of him a remnant came to be for the earth when a flood came to be.

**Sirach 44:18**

Covenants of age were set toward him, so that all flesh would not be wiped out by flood.

**Sirach 44:19**

Abraham was great father of a multitude of nations, and no one like him was found in glory;

**Sirach 44:20**

he kept law of the Most High and came to be in covenant with him; in his flesh he established covenant, and in trial he was found faithful.

**Sirach 44:21**

Because of this, by oath he established for him that nations would be blessed in his seed, to multiply him like dust of earth and to lift up his seed like stars and to make them inherit from sea to sea and from river to end of earth.

**Sirach 44:22**

And in Isaac he established thus, because of Abraham his father, blessing of all men and covenant,

**Sirach 44:23**

and he made it rest upon head of Jacob. He recognized him in his blessings and gave to him in inheritance, and he divided his portions; among tribes he distributed twelve.

### Chapter 45

**Sirach 45:1**

And from him he brought out a man of mercy, finding grace in eyes of all flesh, beloved by God and men, Moses, whose memorial is in blessings.

**Sirach 45:2**

He likened him to glory of holy ones and magnified him in fears of enemies.

**Sirach 45:3**

By his words he made signs cease; he glorified him before face of kings. He commanded him for his people and showed him some of his glory.

**Sirach 45:4**

In faithfulness and meekness he sanctified him; he chose him from all flesh.

**Sirach 45:5**

He made him hear his voice and brought him into the thick darkness and gave him commandments face to face, law of life and knowledge, to teach Jacob covenant and Israel his judgments.

**Sirach 45:6**

He lifted up Aaron, a holy one like him, his brother from tribe of Levi.

**Sirach 45:7**

He established for him covenant of age and gave him priesthood of people. He called him blessed in good order and girded him with a robe of glory.

**Sirach 45:8**

He clothed him with completion of boasting and strengthened him with vessels of power: breeches and full-length robe and ephod,

**Sirach 45:9**

and he encircled him with gold pomegranates, with very many bells round about, to sound a voice in his steps, to make a sound heard in the temple as a memorial to sons of his people;

**Sirach 45:10**

with holy robe, gold and blue and purple, work of an embroiderer, breastpiece of judgment, declarations of truth, spun scarlet, work of craftsman,

**Sirach 45:11**

with precious stones of seal engraving, in a setting of gold, work of a stonecutter, for a memorial in carved writing according to number of tribes of Israel;

**Sirach 45:12**

a golden crown above a turban, impression of a seal of holiness, boast of honor, work of strength, desirable things of eyes adorned beautifully.

**Sirach 45:13**

Before him such things had not come to be until age; no foreigner clothed himself with them except his sons only, and his offspring continually.

**Sirach 45:14**

His sacrifices will be wholly offered twice daily continually.

**Sirach 45:15**

Moses filled the hands and anointed him with holy oil. It became for him an everlasting covenant, and for his seed in days of heaven, to minister to him together and to serve as priest and bless his people in his name.

**Sirach 45:16**

He chose him from every living one to bring fruit-offering to the Lord, incense and sweet odor for memorial, to atone concerning your people.

**Sirach 45:17**

He gave him authority in his commandments, in covenants of judgments, to teach Jacob the testimonies and to enlighten Israel in his law.

**Sirach 45:18**

Foreigners gathered against him and were jealous of him in the wilderness, the men around Dathan and Abiram and the congregation of Korah, in anger and wrath.

**Sirach 45:19**

The Lord saw and was not pleased, and they were completed in angry wrath; he did wonders against them, to consume them in fire of his flame.

**Sirach 45:20**

And he added glory to Aaron and gave him inheritance; firstfruits of firstborn things he divided to him; bread first he prepared to fullness.

**Sirach 45:21**

For they will eat sacrifices of the Lord, which he gave to him and to his seed.

**Sirach 45:22**

Nevertheless in land of people he will not inherit, and there is no portion for him among people; for he himself is your portion and inheritance.

**Sirach 45:23**

And Phinehas son of Eleazar was third in glory, by being zealous in fear of the Lord and standing in turning of people, in goodness of eagerness of his soul. And he atoned concerning Israel.

**Sirach 45:24**

Because of this, covenant of peace was established for him, to preside over holy ones and his people, so that greatness of priesthood might be for him and for his seed into the ages.

**Sirach 45:25**

And a covenant to David son of Jesse from tribe of Judah: inheritance of a king is for son from only son; inheritance of Aaron is also for his seed.

**Sirach 45:26**

May he give you wisdom in your heart to judge his people in righteousness, so that their good things may not be made invisible, and their glory to their generations.

### Chapter 46

**Sirach 46:1**

Mighty in wars was Joshua son of Nun, and successor of Moses in prophecies, who became, according to his name, great for salvation of his chosen ones, to avenge enemies rising up, so that he might make Israel inherit.

**Sirach 46:2**

How he was glorified in lifting up his hands and in stretching out sword against cities.

**Sirach 46:3**

Who before him stood thus? For the Lord himself brought enemies upon him.

**Sirach 46:4**

Was not the sun held back by his hand, and one day became as two?

**Sirach 46:5**

He called upon the Most High, the ruler, when enemies pressed him round about, and the great Lord listened to him with hailstones of mighty power.

**Sirach 46:6**

He dashed down upon a warring nation and in descent destroyed those standing against him, so that nations might know his full armor, because his war was before the Lord; for he also followed after the ruler.

**Sirach 46:7**

And in days of Moses he did mercy, he and Caleb son of Jephunneh, to stand against an assembly, to hinder people from sin and to quiet wicked murmuring.

**Sirach 46:8**

And they, being two, were delivered from six hundred thousand footmen, to bring them into inheritance, into land flowing with milk and honey.

**Sirach 46:9**

And the Lord gave strength to Caleb, and until old age it remained with him, for him to step upon the height of the land, and his seed held it as inheritance,

**Sirach 46:10**

so that all sons of Israel might see that it is good to go after the Lord.

**Sirach 46:11**

And the judges, each by his own name, as many whose heart did not fornicate and as many as did not turn away from the Lord, may their memorial be in blessings.

**Sirach 46:12**

May their bones sprout again from their place, and their name be exchanged again upon sons of their glorified ones.

**Sirach 46:13**

Beloved by his Lord, Samuel, prophet of the Lord, established a kingdom and anointed rulers over his people.

**Sirach 46:14**

By law of the Lord he judged an assembly, and the Lord visited Jacob.

**Sirach 46:15**

By his faithfulness he was made exact as prophet, and by his faithfulness he was known as faithful in vision.

**Sirach 46:16**

And he called upon the Lord, the ruler, when his enemies pressed him round about, with offering of a suckling lamb.

**Sirach 46:17**

And the Lord thundered from heaven and with a great sound made his voice heard,

**Sirach 46:18**

and he wiped out leaders of Tyrians and all rulers of Philistines.

**Sirach 46:19**

And before time of eternal sleep he testified before the Lord and his anointed: Possessions, even to sandals, from all flesh I have not taken. And no man accused him.

**Sirach 46:20**

And after he slept, he prophesied and showed the king his end and lifted up his voice from earth in prophecy, to wipe out lawlessness of people.

### Chapter 47

**Sirach 47:1**

And after this Nathan rose up to prophesy in days of David.

**Sirach 47:2**

As fat separated from a peace offering, so was David from sons of Israel.

**Sirach 47:3**

With lions he played as with kids, and with bears as with lambs of sheep.

**Sirach 47:4**

In his youth did he not kill a giant and remove reproach from people, by lifting hand with stone of sling and casting down boasting of Goliath?

**Sirach 47:5**

For he called upon the Lord Most High, and he gave strength in his right hand to remove a man powerful in war, to lift up horn of his people.

**Sirach 47:6**

So they glorified him among ten-thousands and praised him with blessings of the Lord, when a diadem of glory was brought to him.

**Sirach 47:7**

For he wiped out enemies round about and treated Philistines, the adversaries, as nothing; until today he crushed their horn.

**Sirach 47:8**

In every work of his he gave confession to the Holy Most High with a word of glory; with all his heart he sang praise and loved the one who made him.

**Sirach 47:9**

And he set singers before the altar, and by their sound to sweeten melodies.

**Sirach 47:10**

He gave comeliness at feasts and adorned times until completion, as they praised his holy name and made the sanctuary resound from morning.

**Sirach 47:11**

The Lord took away his sins and lifted up his horn into age and gave him covenant of kings and throne of glory in Israel.

**Sirach 47:12**

After him a knowledgeable son rose up, and because of him he dwelt in broadness.

**Sirach 47:13**

Solomon reigned in days of peace, for whom God gave rest round about, so that he might set up a house for his name and prepare a sanctuary into the age.

**Sirach 47:14**

How wise you were in your youth, and you were filled like a river with understanding.

**Sirach 47:15**

Your soul covered earth, and you filled it with parables of riddles.

**Sirach 47:16**

Your name reached faraway islands, and you were loved in your peace.

**Sirach 47:17**

In songs and proverbs and parables and interpretations, lands marveled at you.

**Sirach 47:18**

In name of the Lord God, the one called God of Israel, you gathered gold like tin and multiplied silver like lead.

**Sirach 47:19**

You bent aside your loins to women and were brought under authority in your body.

**Sirach 47:20**

You gave a blemish in your glory and profaned your seed, to bring wrath upon your children, and you were pierced over your folly,

**Sirach 47:21**

for tyranny to become divided and a disobedient kingdom to begin from Ephraim.

**Sirach 47:22**

But the Lord shall surely not leave his mercy behind, and he shall surely not destroy any of his works, nor shall he wipe out offspring of his chosen one, and seed of the one who loved him he shall surely not remove. And to Jacob he gave a remnant, and to David a root from him.

**Sirach 47:23**

And Solomon rested with his fathers and left behind after him from his seed the folly of people and one lacking understanding, Rehoboam, who made people revolt by his counsel, and Jeroboam son of Nebat, who made Israel sin and gave Ephraim a way of sin.

**Sirach 47:24**

And their sins were multiplied exceedingly, to make them stand away from their land.

**Sirach 47:25**

And they sought out every wickedness until vengeance came upon them.

### Chapter 48

**Sirach 48:1**

And Elijah the prophet rose up like fire, and his word burned like a lamp.

**Sirach 48:2**

He brought famine upon them, and by his zeal made them few.

**Sirach 48:3**

By word of the Lord he held back heaven; so he brought down fire three times.

**Sirach 48:4**

How you were glorified, Elijah, in your wonders; and who is like you, to boast?

**Sirach 48:5**

The one raising a dead man from death and out of Hades by word of the Most High;

**Sirach 48:6**

the one bringing kings down into destruction and glorified men from their bed;

**Sirach 48:7**

the one hearing reproof at Sinai and judgments of vengeance at Horeb;

**Sirach 48:8**

the one anointing kings for repayment and prophets as successors after him;

**Sirach 48:9**

the one taken up in a whirlwind of fire, in a chariot of fiery horses;

**Sirach 48:10**

the one written down in reproofs for times, to calm wrath before anger, to turn heart of father toward son, and to establish tribes of Jacob.

**Sirach 48:11**

Blessed are those who saw you and those adorned in love, for we also will live life.

**Sirach 48:12**

Elijah, who was covered in a whirlwind; and Elisha was filled with his spirit. And in his days he was not shaken by ruler, and no one oppressed him.

**Sirach 48:13**

No word overpowered him, and in sleep his body prophesied.

**Sirach 48:14**

And in his life he did wonders, and at his death his works were marvelous.

**Sirach 48:15**

In all these things the people did not repent and did not stand away from their sins, until they were plundered from their land and scattered in all the earth. And the people were left few, with a ruler for the house of David.

**Sirach 48:16**

Some of them did what was pleasing, but some multiplied sins.

**Sirach 48:17**

Hezekiah fortified his city and brought water into its midst; he dug hard rock with iron and built fountains for waters.

**Sirach 48:18**

In his days Sennacherib came up and sent Rabshakeh, and he departed. And his hand lifted up against Zion, and he boasted greatly in his pride.

**Sirach 48:19**

Then their hearts and hands were shaken, and they labored like women giving birth.

**Sirach 48:20**

And they called upon the merciful Lord, stretching out their hands toward him. And the Holy One quickly listened to them from heaven and redeemed them by hand of Isaiah.

**Sirach 48:21**

He struck the camp of the Assyrians, and his messenger wiped them out.

**Sirach 48:22**

For Hezekiah did what was pleasing to the Lord and was strong in ways of David his father, which Isaiah the prophet commanded, the great and faithful one in his vision.

**Sirach 48:23**

In his days the sun was held back, and he added life to the king.

**Sirach 48:24**

By a great spirit he saw the last things and comforted those mourning in Zion.

**Sirach 48:25**

Until the age he showed things that would be, and hidden things before they came to pass.

### Chapter 49

**Sirach 49:1**

Memorial of Josiah is like composition of incense prepared by work of a perfumer; in every mouth it will be sweetened like honey and like music at a banquet of wine.

**Sirach 49:2**

He himself was directed in turning of people and removed abominations of lawlessness.

**Sirach 49:3**

He directed his heart toward the Lord; in days of lawless ones he strengthened godliness.

**Sirach 49:4**

Except David and Hezekiah and Josiah, all trespassed with trespass; for they left law of the Most High, kings of Judah failed.

**Sirach 49:5**

For they gave their horn to others and their glory to a foreign nation.

**Sirach 49:6**

They burned chosen city of sanctuary and made her roads desolate by hand of Jeremiah;

**Sirach 49:7**

for they afflicted him, and he was sanctified as prophet in womb, to uproot and afflict and destroy, likewise to build and plant.

**Sirach 49:8**

Ezekiel, who saw vision of glory, which he showed him upon chariot of cherubim.

**Sirach 49:9**

For he also remembered the enemies in rain and to do good to those directing ways.

**Sirach 49:10**

And of the twelve prophets, may their bones sprout again from their place; for he comforted Jacob and redeemed them in faith of hope.

**Sirach 49:11**

How shall we magnify Zerubbabel? He himself was like a seal upon a right hand,

**Sirach 49:12**

so also Joshua son of Jozadak, who in their days built a house and lifted up a holy people for the Lord, prepared for glory of age.

**Sirach 49:13**

And Nehemiah, his memorial is for much, who raised for us walls that had fallen, and set up gates and bars, and raised our house-plots.

**Sirach 49:14**

Not even one was created like Enoch, such a one upon the earth; for he himself was taken up from the earth.

**Sirach 49:15**

Nor was a man born like Joseph, leader of brothers, support of people; and his bones were visited.

**Sirach 49:16**

Shem and Seth were glorified among men, and Adam above every living creature in creation.

### Chapter 50

**Sirach 50:1**

Simon son of Onias, the great priest, who in his life repaired the house and in his days strengthened the temple;

**Sirach 50:2**

and by him height of a double wall was founded, high support of the enclosure of the temple.

**Sirach 50:3**

In his days a reservoir of waters was made low, a pit like the sea in circumference.

**Sirach 50:4**

He took thought for his people from falling and strengthened a city under siege.

**Sirach 50:5**

How he was glorified in the turning about of people, at the exit of house of curtain;

**Sirach 50:6**

like morning star in midst of clouds, like full moon in days,

**Sirach 50:7**

like sun shining upon temple of the Most High and like a bow giving light in clouds of glory,

**Sirach 50:8**

like flower of roses in days of new fruits, like lilies at outlet of water, like shoot of frankincense in days of summer,

**Sirach 50:9**

like fire and frankincense upon a firepan, like a gold vessel hammered whole, adorned with every precious stone,

**Sirach 50:10**

like an olive tree sprouting fruits and like a cypress lifted high in clouds.

**Sirach 50:11**

When he took up robe of glory and clothed himself in completion of boasting, in ascent of holy altar he glorified the enclosure of sanctuary.

**Sirach 50:12**

And when he received portions from hands of priests, he himself was standing beside hearth of altar, round about him a crown of brothers, like a cedar sprout in Lebanon; and they encircled him like stems of palms.

**Sirach 50:13**

And all sons of Aaron were in their glory, and offering of the Lord was in their hands before every assembly of Israel.

**Sirach 50:14**

And completing services upon altars, to adorn offering of the Most High Almighty,

**Sirach 50:15**

he stretched out his hand over a libation bowl and poured out from blood of grape; he poured out at foundations of altar a sweet-smelling odor to the Most High, King of all.

**Sirach 50:16**

Then sons of Aaron cried out; with hammered trumpets they sounded, making a great voice heard for memorial before the Most High.

**Sirach 50:17**

Then all the people together hastened and fell upon face to the earth, to worship their Lord, Almighty God Most High.

**Sirach 50:18**

And the psalm-singers praised with their voices; in a very great house melody was sweetened.

**Sirach 50:19**

And the people made petition to the Lord Most High in prayer before the merciful one, until order of the Lord was completed, and they completed his service.

**Sirach 50:20**

Then, coming down, he lifted up his hands over every assembly of sons of Israel, to give blessing of the Lord from his lips and to boast in his name.

**Sirach 50:21**

And they repeated in worship, to display the blessing from the Most High.

**Sirach 50:22**

And now bless the God of all, the one doing great things everywhere, the one lifting up our days from womb and doing with us according to his mercy.

**Sirach 50:23**

May he give us gladness of heart and for peace to come to be in our days in Israel, according to days of the age;

**Sirach 50:24**

to entrust his mercy with us, and in his days let him redeem us.

**Sirach 50:25**

With two nations my soul was angry, and the third is not a nation:

**Sirach 50:26**

those sitting on Mount Samaria and Philistines and the foolish people dwelling in Shechem.

**Sirach 50:27**

Discipline of understanding and knowledge I engraved in this book, Jesus son of Sirach, Jerusalemite, who rained down wisdom from his heart.

**Sirach 50:28**

Blessed is one who will turn about in these things, and placing them upon his heart, he will become wise.

**Sirach 50:29**

For if he does them, he will be strong for all things, because light of the Lord is his track.

### Chapter 51

**Sirach 51:1**

Prayer of Jesus Son of Sirach. I will confess to you, Lord King, and praise you, God my Savior; I confess to your name,

**Sirach 51:2**

because you became shelterer and helper to me, and you redeemed my body from destruction and from snare of slander of tongue, from lips working falsehood, and before those standing by you became helper to me.

**Sirach 51:3**

And you redeemed me according to multitude of mercy and your name, from gnashings ready for food, from hand of those seeking my soul, from many afflictions that I had,

**Sirach 51:4**

from choking of fire around and from midst of fire that I did not kindle,

**Sirach 51:5**

from depth of belly of Hades and from unclean tongue and false word.

**Sirach 51:6**

To a king, slander of unrighteous tongue. My soul drew near as far as death, and my life was near Hades below.

**Sirach 51:7**

They surrounded me on every side, and there was no one helping; I looked for help of men, and there was none.

**Sirach 51:8**

And I remembered your mercy, Lord, and your work from age, because you deliver those waiting for you and save them from hand of nations.

**Sirach 51:9**

And I lifted up my supplication from earth, and concerning rescue from death I made petition.

**Sirach 51:10**

I called upon the Lord, Father of my lord, not to abandon me in days of affliction, in time of proud ones, of helplessness.

**Sirach 51:11**

I will praise your name continually and sing praise in confession. And my petition was heard,

**Sirach 51:12**

for you saved me from destruction and delivered me from an evil time. Because of this I will confess and praise you and bless the name of the Lord.

**Sirach 51:13**

While I was still younger, before I was led astray, I sought wisdom openly in my prayer.

**Sirach 51:14**

Before the temple I was asking concerning her, and until last things I will seek her out.

**Sirach 51:15**

From flower like a ripening grape, my heart rejoiced in her. My foot stepped in uprightness; from my youth I tracked her.

**Sirach 51:16**

I bent my ear a little and received, and I found for myself much discipline.

**Sirach 51:17**

Progress came to be for me in her; to the one giving me wisdom I will give glory.

**Sirach 51:18**

For I considered doing her and was zealous for the good, and I shall surely not be ashamed.

**Sirach 51:19**

My soul struggled in her, and in practice of hunger I was exact. I spread out my hands toward height and mourned her ignorances.

**Sirach 51:20**

I directed my soul toward her; I acquired heart with her from beginning, and in purification I found her. Because of this I shall surely not be abandoned.

**Sirach 51:21**

And my belly was troubled to seek her out; because of this I acquired a good possession.

**Sirach 51:22**

The Lord gave me tongue as my reward, and with it I will praise him.

**Sirach 51:23**

Draw near to me, undisciplined ones, and lodge in house of discipline.

**Sirach 51:24**

Why is it that you lack in these things, and your souls thirst exceedingly?

**Sirach 51:25**

I opened my mouth and spoke: Acquire for yourselves without silver.

**Sirach 51:26**

Put your neck under yoke, and let your soul receive discipline; it is near to find her.

**Sirach 51:27**

See with your eyes that I labored little and found for myself much rest.

**Sirach 51:28**

Partake of discipline with a great amount of silver, and acquire much gold in her.

**Sirach 51:29**

May your soul be gladdened in his mercy, and may you not be ashamed in his praise.

**Sirach 51:30**

Work your work before time, and he will give your reward in his time.

## Baruch

### Chapter 1

**Baruch 1:1**

And these are the words of the book, which Baruch son of Neriah, son of Maaseiah, son of Zedekiah, son of Asadiah, son of Hilkiah, wrote in Babylon,

**Baruch 1:2**

in the fifth year, on the seventh of the month, in the time when the Chaldeans took Jerusalem and burned it with fire.

**Baruch 1:3**

And Baruch read the words of this book in ears of Jeconiah son of Jehoiakim king of Judah, and in ears of all the people who came to the book,

**Baruch 1:4**

and in ears of the powerful and sons of the kings, and in ears of the elders, and in ears of all the people, from small to great, all those dwelling in Babylon by the river Sud.

**Baruch 1:5**

And they wept and fasted and prayed before the Lord,

**Baruch 1:6**

and gathered silver, according as each hand was able,

**Baruch 1:7**

and they sent it to Jerusalem to Jehoiakim son of Hilkiah, son of Shallum, the priest, and to the priests and to all the people who were found with him in Jerusalem,

**Baruch 1:8**

when he received the vessels of the house of the Lord, those carried out from the temple, to return them to the land of Judah, on the tenth of Sivan, silver vessels which Zedekiah son of Josiah king of Judah made,

**Baruch 1:9**

after Nebuchadnezzar king of Babylon exiled Jeconiah and the rulers and the prisoners and the powerful and the people of the land from Jerusalem and brought him into Babylon.

**Baruch 1:10**

And they said, Behold, we have sent silver to you, and buy with the silver whole-burnt offerings and offerings concerning sin and frankincense, and make manna and bring them up upon the altar of the Lord our God.

**Baruch 1:11**

And pray concerning the life of Nebuchadnezzar king of Babylon and for the life of Balthasar his son, so that their days may be like the days of heaven upon the earth.

**Baruch 1:12**

And the Lord will give strength to us and enlighten our eyes, and we will live under the shadow of Nebuchadnezzar king of Babylon and under the shadow of Balthasar his son, and we will serve them many days and find grace before them.

**Baruch 1:13**

And pray concerning us to the Lord our God, because we sinned against the Lord our God, and the anger of the Lord and his wrath has not turned away from us until this day.

**Baruch 1:14**

And you shall read this book, which we sent to you, to make confession in the house of the Lord on a day of feast and in days of season,

**Baruch 1:15**

and you shall say, To the Lord our God belongs righteousness, but to us shame of faces, as this day, to a man of Judah and to those dwelling in Jerusalem,

**Baruch 1:16**

and to our kings and our rulers and our priests and our prophets and our fathers,

**Baruch 1:17**

because we sinned before the Lord

**Baruch 1:18**

and disobeyed him and did not listen to the voice of the Lord our God, to walk in commandments of the Lord that he gave before our face.

**Baruch 1:19**

From the day when the Lord brought our fathers out of the land of Egypt even until this day, we were disobedient toward the Lord our God and acted rashly so as not to listen to his voice.

**Baruch 1:20**

And the evils and the curse clung to us, which the Lord appointed to Moses his servant on the day when he brought our fathers out of the land of Egypt to give us a land flowing with milk and honey, as this day.

**Baruch 1:21**

And we did not listen to the voice of the Lord our God, according to all the words of the prophets whom he sent to us,

**Baruch 1:22**

and we went away, each in the thought of his wicked heart, to work for other gods, to do evil things before eyes of the Lord our God.

### Chapter 2

**Baruch 2:1**

And the Lord established his word that he spoke against us and against our judges who judged Israel and against our kings and against our rulers and against the man of Israel and Judah,

**Baruch 2:2**

to bring upon us great evils, which he did not do under all heaven as was done in Jerusalem, according to the things written in the law of Moses,

**Baruch 2:3**

that we should eat, a man the flesh of his son and a man the flesh of his daughter.

**Baruch 2:4**

And he gave them under hands of all the kingdoms around us, for reproach and for untrodden waste among all the peoples around, where the Lord scattered them there.

**Baruch 2:5**

And they came to be underneath and not above, because we sinned against the Lord our God by not listening to his voice.

**Baruch 2:6**

To the Lord our God belongs righteousness, but to us and to our fathers belongs shame of faces, as this day.

**Baruch 2:7**

All these evils that the Lord spoke against us came upon us.

**Baruch 2:8**

And we did not make petition before the face of the Lord, that each should turn away from the thoughts of their wicked heart.

**Baruch 2:9**

And the Lord kept watch over the evils, and the Lord brought them upon us, because the Lord is righteous in all his works that he commanded us.

**Baruch 2:10**

And we did not listen to his voice, to walk in commandments of the Lord that he gave before our face.

**Baruch 2:11**

And now, Lord God of Israel, who brought your people out of the land of Egypt with a strong hand and with signs and with wonders and with great power and with a high arm and made for yourself a name, as this day,

**Baruch 2:12**

we sinned, we acted ungodly, we acted unrighteously, Lord our God, against all your righteous acts.

**Baruch 2:13**

Let your anger turn away from us, because we were left few among the nations where you scattered us there.

**Baruch 2:14**

Listen, Lord, to our prayer and our petition, and deliver us for your sake, and give us grace before the face of those who exiled us,

**Baruch 2:15**

so that all the earth may know that you are the Lord our God, because your name was called upon Israel and upon his race.

**Baruch 2:16**

Lord, look down from your holy house and consider us; and incline your ear, Lord, and hear.

**Baruch 2:17**

Open your eyes, Lord, and see, because the dead in Hades, whose spirit was taken from their inward parts, will not give glory and righteous act to the Lord,

**Baruch 2:18**

but the soul grieving over greatness, which walks bowed and weak, and the failing eyes, and the hungry soul, will give you glory and righteousness, Lord.

**Baruch 2:19**

For we do not cast our mercy before your face upon the righteous acts of our fathers and our kings, Lord our God,

**Baruch 2:20**

because you sent your anger and your wrath upon us, just as you spoke by hand of your servants the prophets, saying,

**Baruch 2:21**

Thus said the Lord: Bend your shoulder to work for the king of Babylon, and sit upon the land that I gave to your fathers.

**Baruch 2:22**

And if you do not listen to the voice of the Lord to work for the king of Babylon,

**Baruch 2:23**

I will make fail from cities of Judah and outside Jerusalem voice of gladness and voice of joy, voice of bridegroom and voice of bride, and all the land will become an untrodden waste from inhabitants.

**Baruch 2:24**

And we did not listen to your voice to work for the king of Babylon, and you established your words, which you spoke by hands of your servants the prophets, that bones of our kings and bones of our fathers would be carried out from their place.

**Baruch 2:25**

And behold, they are thrown out to the burning heat of day and to frost of night, and they died in evil pains, by famine and by sword and by sending away.

**Baruch 2:26**

And you made the house upon which your name was called as this day, because of wickedness of house of Israel and house of Judah.

**Baruch 2:27**

And you acted toward us, Lord our God, according to all your fairness and according to all your great compassion,

**Baruch 2:28**

just as you spoke by hand of your servant Moses, on the day when you commanded him to write your law before sons of Israel, saying:

**Baruch 2:29**

If you do not listen to my voice, surely this great, great buzzing will turn back into a small thing among the nations where I will scatter them there.

**Baruch 2:30**

For I knew that they would surely not listen to me, because they are a stiff-necked people. And they will return upon their heart in the land of their exile

**Baruch 2:31**

and will know that I am the Lord their God. And I will give them a heart and ears that hear,

**Baruch 2:32**

and they will praise me in land of their exile and remember my name,

**Baruch 2:33**

and they will turn away from their hard back and from their evil practices, because they will remember the way of their fathers who sinned before the Lord.

**Baruch 2:34**

And I will turn them back to the land that I swore to their fathers, to Abraham and to Isaac and to Jacob, and they will rule it. And I will multiply them, and they shall surely not be made small.

**Baruch 2:35**

And I will establish for them an everlasting covenant, to be God to them, and they will be a people to me; and I will no longer move my people Israel from the land that I gave them.

### Chapter 3

**Baruch 3:1**

Lord Almighty, God of Israel, a soul in straits and a spirit of weariness has cried out to you.

**Baruch 3:2**

Hear, Lord, and have mercy, because we sinned before you,

**Baruch 3:3**

because you sit forever, and we are perishing forever.

**Baruch 3:4**

Lord Almighty, God of Israel, now hear the prayer of the dead of Israel and of sons of those who sinned before you, who did not listen to the voice of the Lord their God, and the evils clung to us.

**Baruch 3:5**

Do not remember unrighteous acts of our fathers, but remember your hand and your name in this time;

**Baruch 3:6**

because you are the Lord our God, and we will praise you, Lord.

**Baruch 3:7**

Because for this you gave your fear upon our heart, to call upon your name. And we will praise you in our exile, because we turned away from our heart all unrighteousness of our fathers who sinned before you.

**Baruch 3:8**

Behold, today we are in our exile, where you scattered us there, for reproach and for curse and for liability according to all unrighteous acts of our fathers, who withdrew from the Lord our God.

**Baruch 3:9**

Hear, Israel, commandments of life; give ear to know prudence.

**Baruch 3:10**

What is it, Israel? Why are you in land of enemies? You grew old in a foreign land; you were defiled together with the dead;

**Baruch 3:11**

you were counted with those going into Hades.

**Baruch 3:12**

You abandoned the spring of wisdom.

**Baruch 3:13**

If you had walked in the way of God, you would dwell in peace forever.

**Baruch 3:14**

Learn where prudence is, where strength is, where understanding is, to know at the same time where length of days and life are, where light of eyes and peace are.

**Baruch 3:15**

Who found her place, and who entered into her treasuries?

**Baruch 3:16**

Where are the rulers of the nations and those ruling over the wild beasts upon the earth,

**Baruch 3:17**

those making sport among the birds of heaven and treasuring up silver and gold, in which men trusted, and there is no end of their acquisition,

**Baruch 3:18**

those fashioning silver and being anxious, and there is no finding out of their works?

**Baruch 3:19**

They vanished and went down into Hades, and others rose up in their place.

**Baruch 3:20**

Younger ones saw light and dwelt upon the earth, but they did not know the way of knowledge,

**Baruch 3:21**

nor did they understand her paths, nor take hold of her; their sons came to be far from their way.

**Baruch 3:22**

Nor was it heard in Canaan, nor was it seen in Teman,

**Baruch 3:23**

nor did sons of Hagar, those seeking out understanding upon the earth, merchants of Merran and Teman, storytellers and seekers of understanding, know the way of wisdom, nor did they remember her paths.

**Baruch 3:24**

O Israel, how great is the house of God, and how long is the place of his possession;

**Baruch 3:25**

great and having no end, high and immeasurable.

**Baruch 3:26**

There were born the renowned giants, those from the beginning, becoming large-bodied, knowing war.

**Baruch 3:27**

God did not choose these, nor did he give them the way of knowledge;

**Baruch 3:28**

and they perished because they did not have prudence; they perished through their lack of counsel.

**Baruch 3:29**

Who went up into heaven and took her and brought her down from the clouds?

**Baruch 3:30**

Who crossed beyond the sea and found her and will bring her for choice gold?

**Baruch 3:31**

There is no one knowing her way, nor one considering her path;

**Baruch 3:32**

but the one knowing all things knows her; he found her by his understanding; the one who prepared the earth for age-long time filled it with four-footed livestock.

**Baruch 3:33**

The one sending the light, and it goes; he called it, and it obeyed him with trembling.

**Baruch 3:34**

But the stars shone in their watches and were gladdened.

**Baruch 3:35**

He called them, and they said, We are present; they shone with gladness for the one who made them.

**Baruch 3:36**

This is our God; another will not be counted beside him.

**Baruch 3:37**

He found every way of knowledge and gave her to Jacob his servant and to Israel loved by him;

**Baruch 3:38**

after this she was seen upon earth and lived together among men.

### Chapter 4

**Baruch 4:1**

This is the book of the commandments of God and the law existing forever; all those holding her are for life, but those leaving her will die.

**Baruch 4:2**

Turn back, Jacob, and take hold of her; make your way toward the radiance opposite her light.

**Baruch 4:3**

Do not give your glory to another, nor the things profitable to you to a foreign nation.

**Baruch 4:4**

We are blessed, Israel, because the things pleasing to God are known to us.

**Baruch 4:5**

Take courage, my people, remembrance of Israel.

**Baruch 4:6**

You were sold to the nations, not for destruction, but because you provoked God to anger you were handed over to adversaries;

**Baruch 4:7**

for you provoked the one who made you, sacrificing to demons and not to God.

**Baruch 4:8**

You forgot the eternal God who nourished you, and you also grieved Jerusalem, the one who brought you up;

**Baruch 4:9**

for she saw the wrath that came upon you from God and said, Hear, neighbors of Zion; God brought great mourning upon me.

**Baruch 4:10**

For I saw the captivity of my sons and my daughters, which the Eternal One brought upon them.

**Baruch 4:11**

For I reared them with gladness, but I sent them away with weeping and mourning.

**Baruch 4:12**

Let no one rejoice over me, the widow and left by many; I was made desolate because of the sins of my children, because they turned aside from the law of God,

**Baruch 4:13**

and they did not know his righteous acts, nor walk in ways of commandments of God, nor step upon paths of discipline in his righteousness.

**Baruch 4:14**

Let the neighbors of Zion come, and remember the captivity of my sons and daughters, which the Eternal One brought upon them;

**Baruch 4:15**

for he brought upon them a nation from far away, a shameless nation and foreign-speaking, who did not respect an elder nor have mercy on a child,

**Baruch 4:16**

and they led away the beloved ones of the widow and made the lonely one desolate of her daughters.

**Baruch 4:17**

But I, how am I able to help you?

**Baruch 4:18**

For the one who brought the evils upon you will deliver you from the hand of your enemies.

**Baruch 4:19**

Walk, children, walk, for I was left desolate.

**Baruch 4:20**

I stripped off the robe of peace, but put on sackcloth of my petition; I will cry out to the Eternal One in my days.

**Baruch 4:21**

Take courage, children; cry out to God, and he will deliver you from domination, from hand of enemies.

**Baruch 4:22**

For I hoped upon the Eternal One for your salvation, and joy came to me from the Holy One because of the mercy that will come to you quickly from the Eternal One, your Savior.

**Baruch 4:23**

For I sent you out with weeping and mourning, but God will give you back to me with joy and gladness forever.

**Baruch 4:24**

For just as now the neighbors of Zion have seen your captivity, so they will quickly see your salvation from your God, which will come upon you with great glory and brightness of the Eternal One.

**Baruch 4:25**

Children, be patient with the wrath from God that came upon you. Your enemy pursued you, and you will see his destruction quickly and will step upon their necks.

**Baruch 4:26**

My delicate ones walked rough ways; they were taken away like a flock snatched by enemies.

**Baruch 4:27**
