But after these words Timothy gathered another camp and camped opposite Raphon, from beyond the torrent.

**1 Maccabees 5:38**

And Judas sent to spy out the camp, and they reported to him, saying, All the nations around us have been gathered to them, a very great force,

**1 Maccabees 5:39**

and they have hired Arabs as help for themselves, and they have camped beyond the torrent, ready to come against you for war. And Judas went to meet them.

**1 Maccabees 5:40**

And Timothy said to the rulers of his force, when Judas and his camp drew near to the water torrent, If he crosses over to us first, we will not be able to withstand him, because he will surely be able against us;

**1 Maccabees 5:41**

but if he becomes cowardly and camps beyond the river, we will cross over to him and be able against him.

**1 Maccabees 5:42**

But when Judas drew near to the water torrent, he stationed the scribes of the people at the torrent and commanded them, saying, Do not allow any man to camp, but let all come into the battle.

**1 Maccabees 5:43**

And he crossed over against them first, and all the people behind him, and all the nations were crushed before his face, and they threw away their weapons and fled into the sacred precinct in Carnaim.

**1 Maccabees 5:44**

And they seized the city beforehand and burned the sacred precinct with fire, with all those in it, and Carnaim was routed, and they could no longer withstand before the face of Judas.

**1 Maccabees 5:45**

And Judas gathered all Israel who were in Gilead, from small to great, and their wives and their children and their baggage, a very great camp, to come into the land of Judah.

**1 Maccabees 5:46**

And they came as far as Ephron, and this was a great city at the entrance, very strong; it was not possible to turn away from it to the right or left, but only to go through the middle of it.

**1 Maccabees 5:47**

And those from the city shut them out and blocked the gates with stones.

**1 Maccabees 5:48**

And Judas sent to them with peaceful words, saying, We will pass through your land to depart into our land, and no one will do evil to you; we will only pass through on foot. And they were not willing to open to him.

**1 Maccabees 5:49**

And Judas commanded proclamation to be made in the camp, that each one should camp in the place where he was.

**1 Maccabees 5:50**

And the men of the force camped, and they fought against the city that whole day and that whole night, and the city was handed over into his hands.

**1 Maccabees 5:51**

And he destroyed every male with the mouth of the sword and rooted it out and took its spoils and passed through the city over those who had been killed.

**1 Maccabees 5:52**

And they crossed over the Jordan into the great plain opposite Bethshan.

**1 Maccabees 5:53**

And Judas was gathering together those who lagged behind and encouraging the people through all the way, until they came into the land of Judah.

**1 Maccabees 5:54**

And they went up to Mount Zion with gladness and joy and brought whole-burnt offerings, because no one from them had fallen until they returned in peace.

**1 Maccabees 5:55**

And in the days when Judas and Jonathan were in Gilead, and Simon his brother was in Galilee opposite Ptolemais,

**1 Maccabees 5:56**

Joseph son of Zechariah and Azariah, rulers of the force, heard of the brave deeds and of the war, what things they had done,

**1 Maccabees 5:57**

and he said, Let us also make a name for ourselves, and let us go fight against the nations around us.

**1 Maccabees 5:58**

And they commanded those from the force that was with them, and they went against Jamnia.

**1 Maccabees 5:59**

And Gorgias came out from the city, and his men, to meet them for war.

**1 Maccabees 5:60**

And Joseph and Azariah were routed and pursued as far as the borders of Judea, and about two thousand men from the people of Israel fell on that day.

**1 Maccabees 5:61**

And a great rout happened among the people of Israel, because they did not listen to Judas and his brothers, supposing to act bravely.

**1 Maccabees 5:62**

But they were not from the seed of those men to whom salvation of Israel had been given through their hand.

**1 Maccabees 5:63**

And the man Judas and his brothers were glorified greatly before all Israel and all the nations where their name was heard,

**1 Maccabees 5:64**

and they gathered together to them, praising them.

**1 Maccabees 5:65**

And Judas and his brothers went out and fought the sons of Esau in the land toward the south, and he struck Hebron and her daughters and pulled down its fortress and burned its towers all around.

**1 Maccabees 5:66**

And he departed to go into land of foreigners. And he was passing through Samaria.

**1 Maccabees 5:67**

On that day priests fell in battle, wishing to act bravely, because they went out into war without counsel.

**1 Maccabees 5:68**

And Judas turned aside into Azotus, land of foreigners, and pulled down their altars, and the carved images of their gods he burned with fire, and he plundered the spoils of the cities and returned into the land of Judah.

### Chapter 6

**1 Maccabees 6:1**

And King Antiochus was passing through the upper countries, and he heard that Elymais in Persia was a city glorious in wealth, both silver and gold,

**1 Maccabees 6:2**

and that the temple in it was very rich, and that golden coverings and breastplates and weapons were there, which Alexander son of Philip, the Macedonian king who reigned first among the Greeks, had left there.

**1 Maccabees 6:3**

And he came and was seeking to seize the city and plunder it, and he was not able, because the matter became known to those from the city,

**1 Maccabees 6:4**

and they withstood him for war, and he fled and departed from there with great grief, to return to Babylon.

**1 Maccabees 6:5**

And someone came reporting to him in Persia that the camps that had gone into the land of Judah had been routed,

**1 Maccabees 6:6**

and that Lysias had gone first with a strong force and had been routed before their face, and they had grown strong with weapons and force and many spoils, which they took from the camps that they cut down,

**1 Maccabees 6:7**

and that they pulled down the abomination that he built upon the altar in Jerusalem, and surrounded the holy place with high walls as formerly, and Bethsura, his city.

**1 Maccabees 6:8**

And it came to pass, when the king heard these words, he was astonished and shaken greatly, and he fell upon the bed and fell into sickness from grief, because it had not happened to him as he had intended.

**1 Maccabees 6:9**

And he was there many days, because great grief was renewed upon him, and he reckoned that he was dying.

**1 Maccabees 6:10**

And he called all his friends and said to them, Sleep departs from my eyes, and I have collapsed in heart from care,

**1 Maccabees 6:11**

and I said in my heart, To what affliction have I come, and to what great storm in which I now am? For I was useful and beloved in my authority.

**1 Maccabees 6:12**

But now I remember the evils that I did in Jerusalem, and I took all the gold and silver vessels that were in it and sent out to take away the inhabitants of Judah for nothing.

**1 Maccabees 6:13**

Therefore I know that because of these things these evils found me, and behold, I am perishing with great grief in a foreign land.

**1 Maccabees 6:14**

And he called Philip, one of his friends, and set him over all his kingdom.

**1 Maccabees 6:15**

And he gave him the diadem and his robe and the ring, to bring Antiochus his son and rear him to reign.

**1 Maccabees 6:16**

And King Antiochus died there in the one hundred forty-ninth year.

**1 Maccabees 6:17**

And Lysias recognized that the king had died, and he set Antiochus his son to reign instead of him, whom he had reared young, and he called his name Eupator.

**1 Maccabees 6:18**

And those from the citadel were shutting Israel in around the holy things and seeking evils continually, and they were a support to the nations.

**1 Maccabees 6:19**

And Judas reckoned to take them away, and he assembled all the people to besiege them.

**1 Maccabees 6:20**

And they were gathered together and besieged them in the one hundred fiftieth year, and he made missile-stands and engines against them.

**1 Maccabees 6:21**

And some from them came out from the enclosure, and some of the impious from Israel joined themselves to them,

**1 Maccabees 6:22**

and they went to the king and said, Until when will you not make judgment and avenge our brothers?

**1 Maccabees 6:23**

We were pleased to serve your father and to walk by the things spoken by him and to follow his ordinances.

**1 Maccabees 6:24**

And the sons of our people are besieging the citadel because of this, and they are estranged from us; moreover, as many of us as were found were put to death, and our inheritances were plundered.

**1 Maccabees 6:25**

And they have stretched out a hand not only against us, but also against all their borders.

**1 Maccabees 6:26**

And behold, today they have camped against the citadel in Jerusalem to seize it, and they have fortified the holy place and Bethsura.

**1 Maccabees 6:27**

And if you do not seize them beforehand quickly, they will do greater things than these, and behold, you will be able to restrain them.

**1 Maccabees 6:28**

And the king was angry when he heard, and he gathered all his friends and the rulers of his force and those over the reins.

**1 Maccabees 6:29**

And hired forces came to him from other kingdoms and from islands of seas.

**1 Maccabees 6:30**

And the number of his forces was one hundred thousand foot soldiers and twenty thousand horse, and thirty-two elephants knowing war.

**1 Maccabees 6:31**

And they came through Idumea and camped against Bethsura, and they fought many days and made engines. And they came out and burned them with fire and fought manfully.

**1 Maccabees 6:32**

And Judas departed from the citadel and camped at Bethzachariah, opposite the camp of the king.

**1 Maccabees 6:33**

And the king rose early in the morning and led the camp in its rush along the road to Bethzachariah, and the forces were arrayed for war and trumpeted with the trumpets.

**1 Maccabees 6:34**

And they showed the elephants blood of grape and mulberries, to make them stand by for the war.

**1 Maccabees 6:35**

And they divided the beasts into the phalanxes, and they stationed with each elephant one thousand men armored in chain mail, with bronze helmets upon their heads, and five hundred chosen horse arranged with each beast.

**1 Maccabees 6:36**

These were ready beforehand; wherever the beast was, they were, and wherever it went, they went together. They did not depart from it.

**1 Maccabees 6:37**

And strong wooden towers covered over them were upon each beast, fastened upon it with devices, and on each were thirty-two men of force, the ones fighting upon them, and its Indian.

**1 Maccabees 6:38**

And the remaining horse they stationed on this side and that, on the two parts of the camp, brandishing and being sheltered in the ravines.

**1 Maccabees 6:39**

And when the sun shone upon the gold and bronze shields, the mountains shone from them and blazed like lamps of fire.

**1 Maccabees 6:40**

And a certain part of the king's camp was stretched out upon the high mountains and some upon the low places, and they were coming securely and in order.

**1 Maccabees 6:41**

And all who heard the sound of their multitude and the marching of the multitude and the clashing of the weapons were shaken, for the camp was very great and strong.

**1 Maccabees 6:42**

And Judas and his camp drew near for battle line, and six hundred men fell from the king's camp.

**1 Maccabees 6:43**

And Eleazar Avaran saw one of the beasts armored with a royal breastplate, and it was surpassing all the beasts, and he supposed that the king was on it.

**1 Maccabees 6:44**

And he gave himself to save his people and to preserve an eternal name for himself.

**1 Maccabees 6:45**

And he ran toward it with boldness into the middle of the phalanx and was putting to death on the right and left, and they were splitting apart from him here and there.

**1 Maccabees 6:46**

And he went in under the elephant and struck under it and killed it, and it fell upon the ground on top of him, and he died there.

**1 Maccabees 6:47**

And they saw the strength of the kingdom and the rush of the forces, and they turned away from them.

**1 Maccabees 6:48**

But those from the king's camp were going up to meet them at Jerusalem, and the king camped in Judea and at Mount Zion.

**1 Maccabees 6:49**

And he made peace with those from Bethsura, and they came out from the city, because they did not have food there to be shut up in it, because it was sabbath for the land.

**1 Maccabees 6:50**

And the king seized Bethsura and appointed a garrison there to keep it.

**1 Maccabees 6:51**

And he camped against the holy place many days and set up missile-stands there, and engines and fire-throwers and stone-throwers and small scorpions for throwing bolts and slings.

**1 Maccabees 6:52**

And they also made engines against their engines and fought many days.

**1 Maccabees 6:53**

But there were no foods in the vessels because it was the seventh year, and those who had been saved into Judea from the nations ate up the remainder of the provision.

**1 Maccabees 6:54**

And few men were left in the holy places, because the famine prevailed over them, and they were scattered, each to his own place.

**1 Maccabees 6:55**

And Lysias heard that Philip, whom King Antiochus while still living had set to rear Antiochus his son for him to reign,

**1 Maccabees 6:56**

had returned from Persia and Media, and the forces that had gone with the king with him, and that he was seeking to take over the affairs.

**1 Maccabees 6:57**

And he hurried to depart and say to the king and the leaders of the force and the men, We are failing day by day, and our food is little, and the place where we are camping is strong, and the affairs of the kingdom lie upon us.

**1 Maccabees 6:58**

Now therefore let us give a right hand to these men and make peace with them and with all their nation,

**1 Maccabees 6:59**

and let us establish for them that they should walk by their laws as formerly. For because of their laws, which we scattered, they became angry and did all these things.

**1 Maccabees 6:60**

And the word was pleasing before the king and the rulers, and he sent to them to make peace, and they accepted.

**1 Maccabees 6:61**

And the king and the rulers swore to them. Upon these terms they came out from the fortress.

**1 Maccabees 6:62**

And the king entered Mount Zion and saw the fortress of the place and set aside the oath that he had sworn, and he commanded the wall around it to be pulled down.

**1 Maccabees 6:63**

And he departed in haste and returned to Antioch and found Philip ruling the city, and he fought against him and seized the city by force.

### Chapter 7

**1 Maccabees 7:1**

In the one hundred fifty-first year, Demetrius son of Seleucus went out from Rome and went up with a few men to a city by the sea and reigned there.

**1 Maccabees 7:2**

And it came to pass, as he was entering the house of the kingdom of his fathers, the forces seized Antiochus and Lysias to bring them to him.

**1 Maccabees 7:3**

And the matter became known to him, and he said, Do not show me their faces.

**1 Maccabees 7:4**

And the forces killed them, and Demetrius sat upon the throne of his kingdom.

**1 Maccabees 7:5**

And all lawless and impious men from Israel came to him, and Alcimus was leading them, wishing to serve as priest.

**1 Maccabees 7:6**

And they accused the people before the king, saying, Judas and his brothers have destroyed your friends, and they have scattered us from our land.

**1 Maccabees 7:7**

Now therefore send a man whom you trust, and let him go and see all the destruction that he has done to us and to the country of the king, and let him punish them and all who help them.

**1 Maccabees 7:8**

And the king chose Bacchides, one of the king's friends, ruling in the region beyond the river, and great in the kingdom and faithful to the king,

**1 Maccabees 7:9**

and he sent him and Alcimus the impious, and he established the priesthood for him and commanded him to make vengeance among the sons of Israel.

**1 Maccabees 7:10**

And they departed and came with a great force into the land of Judah, and he sent messengers to Judas and his brothers with peaceful words, with deceit.

**1 Maccabees 7:11**

And they did not pay attention to their words, for they saw that they had come with a great force.

**1 Maccabees 7:12**

And a gathering of scribes gathered together to Alcimus and Bacchides, to seek just things,

**1 Maccabees 7:13**

and the Hasideans were first among the sons of Israel, and they were seeking peace from them.

**1 Maccabees 7:14**

For they said, A man, a priest from the seed of Aaron, has come with the forces, and he will not wrong us.

**1 Maccabees 7:15**

And he spoke peaceful words with them and swore to them, saying, We will not seek evil for you and your friends.

**1 Maccabees 7:16**

And they trusted him, and he seized sixty men from them and killed them in one day, according to the word that he wrote:

**1 Maccabees 7:17**

The flesh of your holy ones and their blood they poured out around Jerusalem, and there was no one burying them.

**1 Maccabees 7:18**

And fear and trembling of them fell upon all the people, because they said, There is no truth and judgment in them, for they transgressed the agreement and the oath that they swore.

**1 Maccabees 7:19**

And Bacchides departed from Jerusalem and camped in Bethzaith, and he sent and seized many of the men who had deserted from him, and some of the people, and slaughtered them into the great pit.

**1 Maccabees 7:20**

And he appointed the country to Alcimus and left with him a force to help him, and Bacchides went away to the king.

**1 Maccabees 7:21**

And Alcimus struggled over the high priesthood,

**1 Maccabees 7:22**

and all those who troubled their people were gathered together to him, and they prevailed over the land of Judah and made a great blow in Israel.

**1 Maccabees 7:23**

And Judas saw all the evil that Alcimus and those with him did among the sons of Israel, beyond the nations,

**1 Maccabees 7:24**

and he went out into all the borders of Judea around, and he made vengeance among the men who had deserted, and they were restrained from going into the country.

**1 Maccabees 7:25**

But when Alcimus saw that Judas and those with him had grown strong, and he knew that he was not able to withstand them, he returned to the king and accused them of evil things.

**1 Maccabees 7:26**

And the king sent Nicanor, one of his glorious rulers, one hating and being hostile to Israel, and commanded him to take away the people.

**1 Maccabees 7:27**

And Nicanor came into Jerusalem with a great force and sent to Judas and his brothers with peaceful words, with deceit, saying,

**1 Maccabees 7:28**

Let there be no battle between me and you. I will come with a few men, so that I may see your faces with peace.

**1 Maccabees 7:29**

And he came to Judas, and they greeted one another peaceably, and the enemies were ready to snatch Judas away.

**1 Maccabees 7:30**

And the matter became known to Judas, that he had come against him with deceit, and he was alarmed at him and was not willing any longer to see his face.

**1 Maccabees 7:31**

And Nicanor knew that his counsel had been uncovered, and he went out to meet Judas in war near Chapharsalama.

**1 Maccabees 7:32**

And about five thousand men of those with Nicanor fell, and they fled into the city of David.

**1 Maccabees 7:33**

And after these words Nicanor went up to Mount Zion. And some of the priests from the holy things and some of the elders of the people came out to greet him peaceably and to show him the whole-burnt offering being offered for the king.

**1 Maccabees 7:34**

And he sneered at them and laughed them down and defiled them and spoke arrogantly.

**1 Maccabees 7:35**

And he swore with anger, saying, Unless Judas and his camp are handed over into my hands now, then it will be, if I return in peace, I will burn this house with fire. And he went out with great anger.

**1 Maccabees 7:36**

And the priests entered and stood before the face of the altar and the temple and wept and said,

**1 Maccabees 7:37**

You, Lord, chose this house for your name to be called upon it, to be a house of prayer and supplication for your people.

**1 Maccabees 7:38**

Make vengeance on this man and on his camp, and let them fall by sword. Remember their blasphemies, and do not give them an abiding place.

**1 Maccabees 7:39**

And Nicanor went out from Jerusalem and camped in Beth-horon, and a force of Syria met him.

**1 Maccabees 7:40**

And Judas camped in Adasa with three thousand men. And Judas prayed and said,

**1 Maccabees 7:41**

Those from the king of the Assyrians, when they blasphemed, your angel went out, Lord, and struck one hundred eighty-five thousand among them.

**1 Maccabees 7:42**

So crush this camp before us today, and let the rest know that he spoke evilly against your holy things, and judge him according to his evil.

**1 Maccabees 7:43**

And the camps joined in war on the thirteenth of the month Adar, and the camp of Nicanor was crushed, and he himself fell first in the war.

**1 Maccabees 7:44**

But when his camp saw that Nicanor had fallen, they threw away their weapons and fled.

**1 Maccabees 7:45**

And they pursued them a journey of one day from Adasa until coming to Gazara, and they trumpeted behind them with the trumpets of signals.

**1 Maccabees 7:46**

And they came out from all the villages of Judea around and outflanked them, and these turned back toward those, and all fell by sword, and not even one of them was left.

**1 Maccabees 7:47**

And they took the spoils and the plunder, and they removed Nicanor's head and his right hand, which he had stretched out arrogantly, and they brought them and displayed them beside Jerusalem.

**1 Maccabees 7:48**

And the people were very glad, and they kept that day as a day of great gladness.

**1 Maccabees 7:49**

And they established to keep this day each year, the thirteenth of Adar.

**1 Maccabees 7:50**

And the land of Judah had quiet for a few days.

### Chapter 8

**1 Maccabees 8:1**

And Judas heard the name of the Romans, that they are mighty in strength, and that they are well-pleased with all who join themselves to them, and as many as come to them, they establish friendship with them,

**1 Maccabees 8:2**

and that they are mighty in strength. And they recounted to him their wars and the brave deeds that they do among the Galatians, and that they prevailed over them and brought them under tribute,

**1 Maccabees 8:3**

and what things they did in the country of Spain, to prevail over the mines of silver and gold there.

**1 Maccabees 8:4**

And they prevailed over the whole place by their counsel and long-suffering, though the place was very far away from them, and over the kings who came against them from the end of the earth, until they crushed them and struck a great blow among them, and the rest give them tribute year by year.

**1 Maccabees 8:5**

And Philip and Perseus, king of the Kittim, and those lifted up against them, they crushed in war and prevailed over them.

**1 Maccabees 8:6**

And Antiochus the Great, king of Asia, who went against them for war, having one hundred twenty elephants and horse and chariots and a very great force, was crushed by them,

**1 Maccabees 8:7**

and they took him alive and established for him and those reigning after him to give them great tribute, and to give hostages and a cession,

**1 Maccabees 8:8**

and the Indian country and Media and Lydia and some of their most beautiful countries; and taking them from him, they gave them to King Eumenes.

**1 Maccabees 8:9**

And those from Greece took counsel to come and take them away,

**1 Maccabees 8:10**

and the matter became known to them, and they sent one general against them and fought against them, and many wounded men fell from them, and they took captive their wives and their children and plundered them and prevailed over their land and pulled down their fortresses and enslaved them until this day.

**1 Maccabees 8:11**

And the remaining kingdoms and the islands, as many as ever withstood them, they destroyed and enslaved them.

**1 Maccabees 8:12**

But with their friends and those relying upon them they preserved friendship, and they prevailed over the kingdoms near and far, and as many as heard their name were afraid of them.

**1 Maccabees 8:13**

And as many as they wish to help and to reign, these reign; but whom they wish, they remove. And they were exalted greatly.

**1 Maccabees 8:14**

And in all these things none of them put on a diadem, and they did not clothe themselves in purple so as to become proud in it.

**1 Maccabees 8:15**

And they made a council chamber for themselves, and day by day three hundred twenty deliberated continually concerning the multitude, to set them in good order.

**1 Maccabees 8:16**

And they entrust their rule to one man each year, and to rule all their land, and all listen to the one, and there is no envy nor jealousy among them.

**1 Maccabees 8:17**

And Judas chose Eupolemus son of John son of Accos, and Jason son of Eleazar, and sent them to Rome to establish friendship and alliance for them,

**1 Maccabees 8:18**

and to take the yoke away from them, because they saw the kingdom of the Greeks enslaving Israel with slavery.

**1 Maccabees 8:19**

And they went to Rome, and the journey was very long, and they entered into the council chamber and answered and said,

**1 Maccabees 8:20**

Judas Maccabeus and his brothers and the multitude of the Jews sent us to you to establish alliance and peace with you and for us to be written as your allies and friends.

**1 Maccabees 8:21**

And the word was pleasing before them.

**1 Maccabees 8:22**

And this is the copy of the letter that was copied on bronze tablets and sent to Jerusalem to be with them there as a memorial of peace and alliance.

**1 Maccabees 8:23**

May it go well with the Romans and with the nation of Jews at sea and upon dry land forever, and may sword and enemy be far from them.

**1 Maccabees 8:24**

But if war first stands against Rome or all their allies in all their dominion,

**1 Maccabees 8:25**

the nation of the Jews will ally with them, as the occasion may prescribe to them, with a full heart.

**1 Maccabees 8:26**

And to those warring against them they will not give nor supply grain, weapons, silver, or ships, as seemed good to the Romans; and they will keep their obligations, taking nothing.

**1 Maccabees 8:27**

And according to the same things, if war first happens to the nation of Jews, the Romans will ally from the soul, as the occasion may prescribe to them.

**1 Maccabees 8:28**

And grain, weapons, silver, or ships will not be given to those allying against them, as seemed good to Rome; and they will keep their obligations, and not with deceit. [29] According to these words the Romans established terms with the people of the Jews.

**1 Maccabees 8:30**

But if after these words both sides take counsel to add or remove, they will do it by their choice, and whatever they add or remove will be authoritative.

**1 Maccabees 8:31**

And concerning the evils that King Demetrius is completing against them, we have written to him, saying, Why have you made your yoke heavy upon our friends, the allied Jews?

**1 Maccabees 8:32**

If therefore they still petition against you, we will make judgment for them and war against you by sea and by dry land.

### Chapter 9

**1 Maccabees 9:1**

And Demetrius heard that Nicanor and his forces had fallen in war, and he again added to send Bacchides and Alcimus into the land of Judah, and the right wing with them.

**1 Maccabees 9:2**

And they went the way to Galgala and camped against Maisaloth, which is in Arbela, and seized it beforehand and destroyed many souls of men.

**1 Maccabees 9:3**

And in the first month of the one hundred fifty-second year they camped against Jerusalem.

**1 Maccabees 9:4**

And they departed and went to Berea with twenty thousand men and two thousand horse.

**1 Maccabees 9:5**

And Judas was camped in Elasa, and three thousand chosen men with him.

**1 Maccabees 9:6**

And they saw the multitude of the forces, that they were many, and they were very afraid; and many slipped away from the camp, and no one was left of them except eight hundred men.

**1 Maccabees 9:7**

And Judas saw that his camp had slipped away and that the war was pressing him, and he was crushed in heart, because he did not have time to gather them,

**1 Maccabees 9:8**

and he was undone and said to those who were left, Let us arise and go up against our opponents, if perhaps we may be able to fight them.

**1 Maccabees 9:9**

And they were turning him back, saying, We surely will not be able; but rather let us save our own souls now, and return with our brothers and fight against them. But we are few.

**1 Maccabees 9:10**

And Judas said, May it not happen to me to do this thing, to flee from them. And if our time has drawn near, then let us die in manliness for the sake of our brothers, and let us not leave an occasion against our glory.

**1 Maccabees 9:11**

And the force departed from the camp and stood to meet them, and the horse was divided into two parts, and the slingers and the archers were going before the force, and all the mighty front fighters.

**1 Maccabees 9:12**

But Bacchides was in the right wing. And the phalanx drew near from the two parts, and they were sounding with the trumpets,

**1 Maccabees 9:13**

and those with Judas also trumpeted with the trumpets; and the earth was shaken by the sound of the camps, and the war was joined from morning until evening.

**1 Maccabees 9:14**

And Judas saw that Bacchides and the solid strength of the camp were on the right, and all the good-souled in heart came together with him,

**1 Maccabees 9:15**

and the right wing was crushed by them, and he pursued after them as far as Mount Azotus.

**1 Maccabees 9:16**

And those in the left wing saw that the right wing had been crushed, and they turned on the footsteps of Judas and those with him from behind.

**1 Maccabees 9:17**

And the war became heavy, and many wounded fell from these and from those.

**1 Maccabees 9:18**

And Judas fell, and the rest fled.

**1 Maccabees 9:19**

And Jonathan and Simon took up Judas their brother and buried him in the tomb of his fathers in Modein.

**1 Maccabees 9:20**

And all Israel wept for him and lamented him with a great lamentation, and they mourned many days and said,

**1 Maccabees 9:21**

How has the mighty one fallen, the one saving Israel?

**1 Maccabees 9:22**

And the rest of the words of Judas and of the wars and of the brave deeds that he did, and of their greatness, were not written down, for they were very many.

**1 Maccabees 9:23**

And it came to pass, after the death of Judas, the lawless peered out in all the borders of Israel, and all those working injustice sprang up.

**1 Maccabees 9:24**

In those days there came to be a very great famine, and the country deserted with them.

**1 Maccabees 9:25**

And Bacchides chose the impious men and set them as lords of the country.

**1 Maccabees 9:26**

And they were seeking out and searching for the friends of Judas and bringing them to Bacchides, and he was taking vengeance on them and mocking them.

**1 Maccabees 9:27**

And great affliction came to be in Israel, such as had not come to be from the day that a prophet had not been seen among them.

**1 Maccabees 9:28**

And all the friends of Judas were gathered and said to Jonathan,

**1 Maccabees 9:29**

Since your brother Judas has died, there is no man like him to go out against the enemies and Bacchides and against those hostile to our nation.

**1 Maccabees 9:30**

Now therefore we have chosen you today to be for us in his place as ruler and leader, to fight our war.

**1 Maccabees 9:31**

And Jonathan accepted the leadership at that time and rose up in place of Judas his brother.

**1 Maccabees 9:32**

And Bacchides knew and was seeking to kill him.

**1 Maccabees 9:33**

And Jonathan and Simon his brother knew, and all those with him, and they fled into the wilderness of Tekoa and camped by the water of the pit of Asphar.

**1 Maccabees 9:34**

And Bacchides knew on the day of the Sabbaths, and he and all his army came beyond the Jordan.

**1 Maccabees 9:35**

And Jonathan sent his brother, leader of the crowd, and urged the Nabataeans his friends to store their much baggage for them.

**1 Maccabees 9:36**

And the sons of Jambri came out from Medaba and seized John and all that he had, and they went away having it.

**1 Maccabees 9:37**

But after these words they reported to Jonathan and Simon his brother that the sons of Jambri were making a great wedding and were bringing the bride from Nadabath, daughter of one of the great magnates of Canaan, with a great escort.

**1 Maccabees 9:38**

And they remembered John their brother and went up and hid under the shelter of the mountain.

**1 Maccabees 9:39**

And they lifted up their eyes and saw, and behold, noise and much baggage; and the bridegroom came out, and his friends and his brothers, to meet them with tambourines and musicians and many weapons.

**1 Maccabees 9:40**

And those around Jonathan rose up against them from the ambush and killed them, and many wounded fell, and the rest fled into the mountain, and they took all their spoils.

**1 Maccabees 9:41**

And the wedding was turned into mourning, and the sound of their musicians into lamentation.

**1 Maccabees 9:42**

And they avenged the vengeance of the blood of their brother and returned to the marsh of the Jordan.

**1 Maccabees 9:43**

And Bacchides heard and came on the day of the Sabbaths as far as the banks of the Jordan with a great force.

**1 Maccabees 9:44**

And Jonathan said to those with him, Let us arise now and fight for our souls, for today is not like yesterday and the third day.

**1 Maccabees 9:45**

For behold, the war is before us and behind us, and the water of the Jordan is on this side and that, and marsh and thicket; there is no place to turn aside.

**1 Maccabees 9:46**

Now therefore cry out to heaven, so that you may be delivered from the hand of your enemies.

**1 Maccabees 9:47**

And the war joined, and Jonathan stretched out his hand to strike Bacchides, and he turned aside from him backward.

**1 Maccabees 9:48**

And Jonathan and those with him leapt into the Jordan and swam through to the other side, and they did not cross the Jordan against them.

**1 Maccabees 9:49**

And about one thousand men fell beside Bacchides on that day.

**1 Maccabees 9:50**

And he returned to Jerusalem, and they built strong cities in Judea: the fortress in Jericho, and Emmaus, and Beth-horon, and Bethel, and Thamnatha-pharathon, and Tephon, with high walls and gates and bars.

**1 Maccabees 9:51**

And he placed a garrison in them to be hostile to Israel.

**1 Maccabees 9:52**

And he fortified the city in Bethsura and Gazara and the citadel, and he placed forces and provisions of foods in them.

**1 Maccabees 9:53**

And he took the sons of the leaders of the country as hostages and put them in the citadel in Jerusalem under guard.

**1 Maccabees 9:54**

And in the one hundred fifty-third year, in the second month, Alcimus commanded to pull down the wall of the inner court of the holy things; and he pulled down the works of the prophets and began to pull down.

**1 Maccabees 9:55**

At that time Alcimus was struck, and his works were hindered, and his mouth was stopped, and he was paralyzed and was no longer able to speak a word or to command concerning his house.

**1 Maccabees 9:56**

And Alcimus died at that time with great torment.

**1 Maccabees 9:57**

And Bacchides saw that Alcimus had died, and he returned to the king. And the land of Judah had quiet two years.

**1 Maccabees 9:58**

And all the lawless took counsel, saying, Behold, Jonathan and those with him are dwelling in quiet, trusting. Now therefore we will bring Bacchides, and he will seize them all in one night.

**1 Maccabees 9:59**

And going, they counseled him.

**1 Maccabees 9:60**

And he departed to come with a great force and sent letters secretly to all his allies in Judea, so that they might seize Jonathan and those with him; and they were not able, because their counsel became known to them.

**1 Maccabees 9:61**

And they seized from the men of the country the chiefs of evil, about fifty men, and killed them.

**1 Maccabees 9:62**

And Jonathan and Simon and those with him withdrew to Bethbasi, which is in the wilderness, and he built its demolished parts, and they strengthened it.

**1 Maccabees 9:63**

And Bacchides knew and gathered all his multitude and commanded those from Judea.

**1 Maccabees 9:64**

And he came and camped against Bethbasi and fought against it many days and made engines.

**1 Maccabees 9:65**

And Jonathan left Simon his brother in the city and went out into the country and went out with a number of men.

**1 Maccabees 9:66**

And he struck Odomera and his brothers and the sons of Phasiron in their encampment, and he began to strike and to go up with the forces.

**1 Maccabees 9:67**

And Simon and those with him went out from the city and burned the engines with fire.

**1 Maccabees 9:68**

And they fought against Bacchides, and he was crushed by them. And they pressed him greatly, because his counsel and his expedition were empty.

**1 Maccabees 9:69**

And he was angry with wrath against the lawless men who had counseled him to come into the country, and he killed many of them and took counsel to depart into his own land.

**1 Maccabees 9:70**

And Jonathan recognized it and sent ambassadors to him to arrange peace with him and to return the captivity to them.

**1 Maccabees 9:71**

And he accepted and did according to his words and swore to him not to seek evil against him all the days of his life.

**1 Maccabees 9:72**

And he returned to him the captivity that he had taken captive formerly from the land of Judah, and turning back, he went away into his own land and did not again add to come into their borders.

**1 Maccabees 9:73**

And sword ceased from Israel, and Jonathan lived in Machmas. And Jonathan began to judge the people and made the impious vanish from Israel.

### Chapter 10

**1 Maccabees 10:1**

And in the one hundred sixtieth year Alexander son of Antiochus Epiphanes went up and seized Ptolemais, and they received him, and he reigned there.

**1 Maccabees 10:2**

And King Demetrius heard and gathered very many forces and went out to meet him for war.

**1 Maccabees 10:3**

And Demetrius sent letters to Jonathan with peaceful words, so as to magnify him.

**1 Maccabees 10:4**

For he said, Let us be first to set peace with him, before he sets peace with Alexander against us.

**1 Maccabees 10:5**

For he will remember all the evils that we completed against him and against his brothers and against his nation.

**1 Maccabees 10:6**

And he gave him authority to gather forces and prepare weapons and to be his ally, and he said that the hostages in the citadel should be handed over to him.

**1 Maccabees 10:7**

And Jonathan came into Jerusalem and read the letters in the ears of all the people and of those from the citadel.

**1 Maccabees 10:8**

And they feared a great fear, because they heard that the king had given him authority to gather forces.

**1 Maccabees 10:9**

And those from the citadel handed over the hostages to Jonathan, and he returned them to their parents.

**1 Maccabees 10:10**

And Jonathan lived in Jerusalem and began to build and renew the city.

**1 Maccabees 10:11**

And he told those doing the works to build the walls and Mount Zion around with square stones for fortification, and they did so.

**1 Maccabees 10:12**

And the foreigners who were in the fortresses that Bacchides had built fled.

**1 Maccabees 10:13**

And each left his place and went away into his own land.

**1 Maccabees 10:14**

Only in Bethsura some of those who had left the law and the ordinances remained, for it was a refuge place for them.

**1 Maccabees 10:15**

And King Alexander heard the promises that Demetrius had sent to Jonathan, and they recounted to him the wars and the brave deeds that he and his brothers had done, and the labors that they had endured,

**1 Maccabees 10:16**

and he said, Will we find one such man? And now we will make him our friend and ally.

**1 Maccabees 10:17**

And he wrote letters and sent them to him according to these words, saying,

**1 Maccabees 10:18**

King Alexander to brother Jonathan, greeting.

**1 Maccabees 10:19**

We have heard concerning you that you are a man mighty in strength and suitable to be our friend.

**1 Maccabees 10:20**

And now we have appointed you today high priest of your nation, and for you to be called friend of the king, and he sent him purple and a golden crown, and to be mindful of our things and preserve friendship toward us.

**1 Maccabees 10:21**

And Jonathan put on the holy robe in the seventh month of the one hundred sixtieth year, at the Feast of Tabernacles, and he gathered forces and prepared many weapons.

**1 Maccabees 10:22**

And Demetrius heard these words and was grieved and said,

**1 Maccabees 10:23**

What is this that we have done, that Alexander has gotten ahead of us in laying down friendship with the Jews for support?

**1 Maccabees 10:24**

I also will write to them words of encouragement and exaltation and ordinances, so that they may be with me for help.

**1 Maccabees 10:25**

And he sent to them according to these words: King Demetrius to the nation of the Jews, greeting.

**1 Maccabees 10:26**

Since you have preserved the agreements toward us and remained in our friendship and did not join yourselves to our enemies, we have heard and rejoiced.

**1 Maccabees 10:27**

And now remain still to preserve faith toward us, and we will repay you good things in return for what you do with us.

**1 Maccabees 10:28**

And we will release to you many releases and give you gifts.

**1 Maccabees 10:29**

And now I release you and remit all the Jews from the tributes and the price of salt and from the crowns,

**1 Maccabees 10:30**

and instead of the third of the sowing and instead of the half of the fruit of the trees that falls to me to take, I remit from today and beyond, not to take it from the land of Judah or from the three districts being added to it from Samaria and Galilee, from this day and for all time.

**1 Maccabees 10:31**

And let Jerusalem be holy and remitted, and its borders, the tithes and the tolls.

**1 Maccabees 10:32**

I also remit the authority of the citadel in Jerusalem and give it to the high priest, so that he may appoint in it men whom he himself may choose to keep it.

**1 Maccabees 10:33**

And every soul of the Jews that was taken captive from the land of Judah into all my kingdom, I freely release; and let all remit the tributes, even those of their livestock.

**1 Maccabees 10:34**

And all the feasts and the Sabbaths and new moons and appointed days, and three days before a feast and three days after a feast, let all these days be days of exemption and release for all the Jews who are in my kingdom.

**1 Maccabees 10:35**

And no one will have authority to exact from or trouble any of them concerning any matter.

**1 Maccabees 10:36**

And let up to thirty thousand men from the Jews be enrolled into the forces of the king, and an allowance will be given to them as is fitting for all the forces of the king.

**1 Maccabees 10:37**

And some of them will be appointed in the great fortresses of the king, and some of them will be appointed over needs of the kingdom that are matters of trust. And those over them and their rulers shall be from them, and let them walk by their laws, just as the king commanded in the land of Judah.

**1 Maccabees 10:38**

And the three districts that have been added to Judea from the country of Samaria, let them be added to Judea, to be reckoned as under one, so as to obey no other authority except the high priest.

**1 Maccabees 10:39**

Ptolemais and the region attached to it I have given as a gift to the holy things in Jerusalem, for the proper expense of the holy things.

**1 Maccabees 10:40**

And I give yearly fifteen thousand shekels of silver from the accounts of the king, from the places that belong.

**1 Maccabees 10:41**

And all the surplus that those from the needs did not pay, as in the first years, from now they will give for the works of the house.

**1 Maccabees 10:42**

And besides these things, the five thousand shekels of silver that they were taking from the needs of the holy place from the account year by year, these also are remitted because they belong to the priests who minister.

**1 Maccabees 10:43**

And as many as flee into the temple in Jerusalem and in all its borders, owing royal debts and any matter, let them be released, and all that belongs to them in my kingdom.

**1 Maccabees 10:44**

And for the building and renewal of the works of the holy things, the expense will be given from the account of the king.

**1 Maccabees 10:45**

And for building the walls of Jerusalem and fortifying around, the expense will be given from the account of the king, and for building the walls in Judea.

**1 Maccabees 10:46**

But when Jonathan and the people heard these words, they did not trust them nor accept them, because they remembered the great evil that he had done in Israel and how he had afflicted them greatly.

**1 Maccabees 10:47**

And they were well-pleased with Alexander, because he had become to them a leader of peaceful words, and they were allied with him all the days.

**1 Maccabees 10:48**

And King Alexander gathered great forces and camped opposite Demetrius.

**1 Maccabees 10:49**

And the two kings joined war, and the camp of Demetrius fled, and Alexander pursued him and prevailed against them.

**1 Maccabees 10:50**

And he made the war very strong until the sun set, and Demetrius fell on that day.

**1 Maccabees 10:51**

And Alexander sent ambassadors to Ptolemy king of Egypt according to these words, saying,

**1 Maccabees 10:52**

Since I returned into the land of my kingdom and sat upon the throne of my fathers and seized the rule, and crushed Demetrius and gained control of our country,

**1 Maccabees 10:53**

and joined battle against him, and he and his camp were crushed by us, and we sat upon the throne of his kingdom,

**1 Maccabees 10:54**

and now let us establish friendship toward one another; and now give me your daughter as wife, and I will make a marriage alliance with you and give gifts to you and to her worthy of you.

**1 Maccabees 10:55**

And King Ptolemy answered, saying, Good was the day in which you returned into the land of your fathers and sat upon the throne of their kingdom.

**1 Maccabees 10:56**

And now I will do for you what you wrote, but meet me at Ptolemais, so that we may see one another, and I will make a marriage alliance with you, just as you have said.

**1 Maccabees 10:57**

And Ptolemy went out from Egypt, he and Cleopatra his daughter, and they entered Ptolemais in the one hundred sixty-second year.

**1 Maccabees 10:58**

And King Alexander met him, and he gave Cleopatra his daughter to him, and he made her wedding in Ptolemais as kings do, with great glory.

**1 Maccabees 10:59**

And King Alexander wrote to Jonathan to come to meet him.

**1 Maccabees 10:60**

And he went with glory to Ptolemais and met the two kings, and he gave silver and gold to them and to their friends, and many gifts, and he found favor before them.

**1 Maccabees 10:61**

And pestilent men from Israel gathered together against him, lawless men, to petition against him, and the king paid no attention to them.

**1 Maccabees 10:62**

And the king commanded, and they stripped Jonathan of his garments and clothed him in purple; and they did so.

**1 Maccabees 10:63**

And the king seated him with himself and said to his rulers, Go out with him into the middle of the city and proclaim that no one should petition against him concerning any matter, and that no one should trouble him concerning any word.

**1 Maccabees 10:64**

And it came to pass, when those petitioning against him saw his glory, just as it was proclaimed, and saw him clothed in purple, they all fled.

**1 Maccabees 10:65**

And the king glorified him and wrote him among the first friends and set him as general and district-ruler.

**1 Maccabees 10:66**

And Jonathan returned to Jerusalem with peace and gladness.

**1 Maccabees 10:67**

And in the one hundred sixty-fifth year Demetrius son of Demetrius came from Crete into the land of his fathers.

**1 Maccabees 10:68**

And King Alexander heard and was greatly grieved and returned to Antioch.

**1 Maccabees 10:69**

And Demetrius appointed Apollonius, who was over Coele-Syria, and he gathered a great force and camped in Jamnia. And he sent to Jonathan the high priest, saying,

**1 Maccabees 10:70**

You alone lift yourself up against us, and I have become a laughingstock and a reproach because of you. And why do you exercise authority against us in the mountains?

**1 Maccabees 10:71**

Now therefore, if you trust in your forces, come down to us into the plain, and let us compare ourselves there, because with me is the force of the cities.

**1 Maccabees 10:72**

Ask and learn who I am and who the rest are who help us, and they say, There is no standing of foot for you before our face, because your fathers were routed twice in their land.

**1 Maccabees 10:73**

And now you will not be able to withstand the horse and such a force in the plain, where there is neither stone nor pebble nor place to flee.

**1 Maccabees 10:74**

But when Jonathan heard the words of Apollonius, he was moved in mind, and he chose ten thousand men and went out from Jerusalem, and Simon his brother met him to help him.

**1 Maccabees 10:75**

And he camped against Joppa, and they shut him out from the city, because a garrison of Apollonius was in Joppa, and they fought against it.

**1 Maccabees 10:76**

And those from the city, becoming afraid, opened, and Jonathan became lord of Joppa.

**1 Maccabees 10:77**

And Apollonius heard, and he camped with three thousand horse and a great force, and went to Azotus as though passing through, and at the same time he was going forward into the plain, because he had a multitude of horse and trusted in it.

**1 Maccabees 10:78**

And Jonathan pursued after him to Azotus, and the camps joined in war.

**1 Maccabees 10:79**

And Apollonius left one thousand horse hidden behind them.

**1 Maccabees 10:80**

And Jonathan knew that there was an ambush behind him, and they surrounded his camp and shot shafts into the people from morning until evening.

**1 Maccabees 10:81**

But the people stood just as Jonathan commanded, and their horses grew weary.

**1 Maccabees 10:82**

And Simon drew out his force and joined against the battle line, for the horse was undone, and they were crushed by him and fled.

**1 Maccabees 10:83**

And the horse was scattered in the plain, and they fled into Azotus and entered Beth-dagon, their idol-shrine, to be saved.

**1 Maccabees 10:84**

And Jonathan burned Azotus and the cities around it and took their spoils, and the temple of Dagon and those who fled together into it he burned with fire.

**1 Maccabees 10:85**

And those who had fallen by sword, with those burned, came to eight thousand men.

**1 Maccabees 10:86**

And Jonathan departed from there and camped against Ashkelon, and those from the city came out to meet him with great glory.

**1 Maccabees 10:87**

And Jonathan returned to Jerusalem with those with him, having many spoils.

**1 Maccabees 10:88**

And it came to pass, when King Alexander heard these words, he added to glorify Jonathan.

**1 Maccabees 10:89**

And he sent him a golden brooch, as it is customary to give to the kinsmen of kings, and he gave him Ekron and all its borders as an inheritance.

### Chapter 11

**1 Maccabees 11:1**

And the king of Egypt gathered many forces, like the sand by the lip of the sea, and many ships, and he sought to prevail over the kingdom of Alexander by deceit and to add it to his kingdom.

**1 Maccabees 11:2**

And he went out into Syria with peaceful words, and those from the cities were opening to him and meeting him, because it was a command of King Alexander to meet him, because he was his father-in-law.

**1 Maccabees 11:3**

But as Ptolemy entered into the cities, he stationed forces as a garrison in each city.

**1 Maccabees 11:4**

And when he drew near to Azotus, they showed him the temple of Dagon burned with fire, and Azotus and its surrounding towns pulled down, and the bodies thrown down, and those burned whom he had burned in the war; for they had made heaps of them in his way.

**1 Maccabees 11:5**

And they recounted to the king what Jonathan had done, in order to blame him; and the king was silent.

**1 Maccabees 11:6**

And Jonathan met the king at Joppa with glory, and they greeted one another and lodged there.

**1 Maccabees 11:7**

And Jonathan went with the king as far as the river called Eleutherus and returned to Jerusalem.

**1 Maccabees 11:8**

But King Ptolemy became lord of the coastal cities as far as Seleucia by the sea and was reasoning evil thoughts concerning Alexander.

**1 Maccabees 11:9**

And he sent ambassadors to King Demetrius, saying, Come, let us make a covenant with each other, and I will give you my daughter, whom Alexander has, and you will reign over the kingdom of your father.

**1 Maccabees 11:10**

For I regret having given him my daughter, for he sought to kill me.

**1 Maccabees 11:11**

And he blamed him because he desired his kingdom.

**1 Maccabees 11:12**

And taking his daughter away from him, he gave her to Demetrius, and he was alienated from Alexander, and their enmity appeared.

**1 Maccabees 11:13**

And Ptolemy entered Antioch and put on the diadem of Asia, and he put two diadems around his head, that of Asia and Egypt.

**1 Maccabees 11:14**

But King Alexander was in Cilicia at those times, because those from those places were revolting.

**1 Maccabees 11:15**

And Alexander heard and came against him in war. And Ptolemy led out the force and met him with a strong hand and routed him.

**1 Maccabees 11:16**

And Alexander fled into Arabia to be sheltered there. But King Ptolemy was exalted.

**1 Maccabees 11:17**

And Zabdiel the Arab removed the head of Alexander and sent it to Ptolemy.

**1 Maccabees 11:18**

And King Ptolemy died on the third day, and those who were in the fortresses were destroyed by those in the fortresses.

**1 Maccabees 11:19**

And Demetrius reigned in the one hundred sixty-seventh year.

**1 Maccabees 11:20**

In those days Jonathan gathered those from Judea to war against the citadel in Jerusalem, and he made many engines against it.

**1 Maccabees 11:21**

And some men hating their nation, lawless men, went to the king and reported to him that Jonathan was besieging the citadel.

**1 Maccabees 11:22**

And when he heard, he was angry. But as soon as he heard, he immediately broke camp and came to Ptolemais and wrote to Jonathan not to besiege the citadel and to meet him for conference in Ptolemais very quickly.

**1 Maccabees 11:23**

But when Jonathan heard, he commanded the siege to continue, and he chose some of the elders of Israel and of the priests and gave himself to danger.

**1 Maccabees 11:24**

And taking silver and gold and clothing and many other gifts, he went to the king at Ptolemais and found favor before him.

**1 Maccabees 11:25**

And some lawless men from the nation were petitioning against him.

**1 Maccabees 11:26**

And the king did to him just as those before him had done to him, and exalted him before all his friends,

**1 Maccabees 11:27**

and established for him the high priesthood and whatever other honors he had formerly, and made him lead among the first friends.

**1 Maccabees 11:28**

And Jonathan asked the king to make Judea tax-free, and the three toparchies and Samaria, and promised him three hundred talents.

**1 Maccabees 11:29**

And the king was well-pleased and wrote letters to Jonathan concerning all these things, having this form:

**1 Maccabees 11:30**

King Demetrius to Jonathan the brother, greeting, and to the nation of Jews.

**1 Maccabees 11:31**

The copy of the letter that we wrote to Lasthenes our kinsman concerning you, we have written also to you, so that you may know.

**1 Maccabees 11:32**

King Demetrius to Lasthenes the father, greeting.

**1 Maccabees 11:33**

We have judged to do good to the nation of the Jews, our friends and those preserving the just things toward us, because of their goodwill toward us.

**1 Maccabees 11:34**

Therefore we have established for them both the borders of Judea and the three districts, Aphairema and Lydda and Ramathaim, which were added to Judea from Samaria, and all the things belonging to them, for all those sacrificing in Jerusalem, instead of the royal dues that the king formerly took from them year by year from the produce of the land and from the fruits of trees.

**1 Maccabees 11:35**

And the other things belonging to us from now on, from the tithes and the tolls belonging to us, and the salt lakes and the crowns belonging to us, all these we remit sufficiently to them.

**1 Maccabees 11:36**

And not one of these things will be set aside from now and for all time.

**1 Maccabees 11:37**

Now therefore take care to make a copy of these things, and let it be given to Jonathan and set on the holy mountain in a conspicuous place.

**1 Maccabees 11:38**

And King Demetrius saw that the land had quiet before him and that nothing resisted him, and he released all his forces, each to his own place, except the foreign forces that he had enlisted from the islands of the nations. And all the forces of his fathers became hostile to him.

**1 Maccabees 11:39**

But Tryphon was formerly one of those with Alexander, and he saw that all the forces were grumbling against Demetrius, and he went to Imalkue the Arab, who was rearing Antiochus, the young child of Alexander.

**1 Maccabees 11:40**

And he stayed near him so that he might hand him over to him, so that he might reign instead of his father. And he reported to him all that Demetrius had completed and the hostility with which his forces were hostile to him, and he remained there many days.

**1 Maccabees 11:41**

And Jonathan sent to King Demetrius, that he should cast out those from the citadel from Jerusalem, and those in the fortresses, for they were warring against Israel.

**1 Maccabees 11:42**

And Demetrius sent to Jonathan, saying, Not only these things will I do for you and for your nation, but with glory I will glorify you and your nation, if I find opportunity.

**1 Maccabees 11:43**

Now therefore you will do rightly by sending me men who will ally with me, because all my forces have revolted.

**1 Maccabees 11:44**

And Jonathan sent him three thousand men mighty in strength to Antioch, and they came to the king, and the king was glad at their arrival.

**1 Maccabees 11:45**

And those from the city gathered together into the middle of the city, about one hundred twenty thousand men, and they were wishing to kill the king.

**1 Maccabees 11:46**

And the king fled into the court, and those from the city seized the passages of the city and began to fight.

**1 Maccabees 11:47**

And the king called the Jews for help, and they gathered together to him all at once and were scattered in the city all at once, and they killed about one hundred thousand in the city on that day.

**1 Maccabees 11:48**

And they burned the city and took many spoils on that day and saved the king.

**1 Maccabees 11:49**

And those from the city saw that the Jews had prevailed over the city as they wished, and they became weak in their minds and cried to the king with supplication, saying,

**1 Maccabees 11:50**

Give us right hands, and let the Jews stop warring against us and the city.

**1 Maccabees 11:51**

And they threw down their weapons and made peace, and the Jews were glorified before the king and before all those in his kingdom, and they returned to Jerusalem having many spoils.

**1 Maccabees 11:52**

And King Demetrius sat upon the throne of his kingdom, and the land had quiet before him.

**1 Maccabees 11:53**

And he lied in all that he had said, and was alienated from Jonathan, and did not repay according to the good will with which he had repaid him, and he afflicted him greatly.

**1 Maccabees 11:54**

But after these things Tryphon returned, and Antiochus with him, a younger child; and he reigned and put on a diadem.

**1 Maccabees 11:55**

And all the forces that Demetrius had scattered gathered together to him, and they fought against him, and he fled and was routed.

**1 Maccabees 11:56**

And Tryphon took the beasts and prevailed over Antioch.

**1 Maccabees 11:57**

And Antiochus the younger wrote to Jonathan, saying, I establish the high priesthood for you, and I appoint you over the four districts, and for you to be among the friends of the king.

**1 Maccabees 11:58**

And he sent him gold vessels and service, and gave him authority to drink in gold vessels and to be in purple and to have a golden brooch.

**1 Maccabees 11:59**

And he appointed Simon his brother as general from the Ladder of Tyre as far as the borders of Egypt.

**1 Maccabees 11:60**

And Jonathan went out and passed through beyond the river and in the cities, and all the forces of Syria gathered to him for alliance, and he came to Ashkelon, and those from the city met him gloriously.

**1 Maccabees 11:61**

And he went away from there to Gaza, and those from Gaza shut him out, and he besieged it and burned its surrounding towns with fire and plundered them.

**1 Maccabees 11:62**

And those from Gaza asked Jonathan, and he gave them right hands and took the sons of their rulers as hostages and sent them away to Jerusalem; and he passed through the country as far as Damascus.

**1 Maccabees 11:63**

And Jonathan heard that the rulers of Demetrius were present at Kadesh in Galilee with a great force, wishing to remove him from his office.

**1 Maccabees 11:64**

And he met them, but left Simon his brother in the country.

**1 Maccabees 11:65**

And Simon camped against Bethsura and was fighting against it many days and shut it in.

**1 Maccabees 11:66**

And they asked him to receive right hands, and he gave them; and he cast them out from there and seized the city and placed a garrison over it.

**1 Maccabees 11:67**

And Jonathan and his camp camped by the water of Gennesar, and they rose early in the morning into the plain of Nasor.

**1 Maccabees 11:68**

And behold, a camp of foreigners met him in the plain, and they set out an ambush against him in the mountains, but they themselves met him opposite.

**1 Maccabees 11:69**

And the ambushes rose up from their places and joined war. And all those with Jonathan fled;

**1 Maccabees 11:70**

not even one of them was left, except Mattathias son of Abessalom and Judas son of Chalphi, rulers of the army of the forces.

**1 Maccabees 11:71**

And Jonathan tore his garments and put earth upon his head and prayed.

**1 Maccabees 11:72**

And he turned back to them in war and routed them, and they fled.

**1 Maccabees 11:73**

And those who were fleeing, who were with him, saw and returned to him, and they pursued with him as far as Kadesh, as far as their camp, and they camped there.

**1 Maccabees 11:74**

And about three thousand men from the foreigners fell on that day. And Jonathan returned to Jerusalem.

### Chapter 12

**1 Maccabees 12:1**

And Jonathan saw that the time was working together with him, and he chose men and sent them to Rome to establish and renew the friendship with them.

**1 Maccabees 12:2**

And to the Spartans and other places he sent letters according to the same things.

**1 Maccabees 12:3**

And they went to Rome and entered into the council chamber and said, Jonathan the high priest and the nation of the Jews sent us to renew the friendship with them and the alliance according to the former time.

**1 Maccabees 12:4**

And they gave letters to them to the authorities in each place, so that they would send them forward into the land of Judah with peace.

**1 Maccabees 12:5**

And this is the copy of the letters that Jonathan wrote to the Spartans:

**1 Maccabees 12:6**

Jonathan high priest, and the senate of the nation, and the priests, and the rest of the people of the Jews, to the Spartans, the brothers, greeting.

**1 Maccabees 12:7**

Even formerly letters were sent to Onias the high priest from Darius, the one reigning among you, that you are our brothers, as the copy beneath shows.

**1 Maccabees 12:8**

And Onias received the man who was sent gloriously and received the letters, in which it was made clear concerning alliance and friendship.

**1 Maccabees 12:9**

And we therefore, being in need of none of these things, having as encouragement the holy books that are in our hands,

**1 Maccabees 12:10**

have attempted to send to you to renew brotherhood and friendship, so that we may not become alienated from you; for many times have passed since you sent to us.

**1 Maccabees 12:11**

We therefore at every time, without ceasing, both in the feasts and in the other fitting days, remember you at the sacrifices that we offer and in the prayers, as it is necessary and fitting to remember brothers.

**1 Maccabees 12:12**

And we rejoice over your glory.

**1 Maccabees 12:13**

But many afflictions and many wars have surrounded us, and the kings around us have warred against us.

**1 Maccabees 12:14**

Therefore we were not willing to trouble you and our remaining allies and friends in these wars.

**1 Maccabees 12:15**

For we have the help from heaven helping us, and we were rescued from our enemies, and our enemies were humbled.

**1 Maccabees 12:16**

Therefore we chose Numenius son of Antiochus and Antipater son of Jason and have sent them to the Romans to renew the former friendship and alliance with them.

**1 Maccabees 12:17**

Therefore we commanded them also to go to you and greet you and give you the letters from us concerning the renewal and our brotherhood.

**1 Maccabees 12:18**

And now you will do well by answering us concerning these things.

**1 Maccabees 12:19**

And this is the copy of the letters that they sent to Onias:

**1 Maccabees 12:20**

Areius, king of the Spartans, to Onias the great priest, greeting.

**1 Maccabees 12:21**

It has been found in writing concerning the Spartans and Jews that they are brothers and that they are from the race of Abraham.

**1 Maccabees 12:22**

And now, since we have known these things, you will do well by writing to us concerning your peace.

**1 Maccabees 12:23**

And we also write back to you: your livestock and your possessions are ours, and ours are yours. Therefore we command that they report to you according to these things.

**1 Maccabees 12:24**

And Jonathan heard that the rulers of Demetrius had returned with a greater force than before to war against him.

**1 Maccabees 12:25**

And he departed from Jerusalem and met them in the country of Hamath, for he did not give them respite to set foot in his country.

**1 Maccabees 12:26**

And he sent spies into their camp, and they returned and reported to him that they were arranged this way to fall upon them by night.

**1 Maccabees 12:27**

But when the sun set, Jonathan commanded those with him to stay awake and be upon their weapons and be prepared for war through the whole night, and he sent out advance guards around the camp.

**1 Maccabees 12:28**

And the opponents heard that Jonathan and those with him were prepared for war, and they were afraid and were struck in their heart, and they kindled fires in their camp.

**1 Maccabees 12:29**

But Jonathan and those with him did not know until morning, for they saw the lights burning.

**1 Maccabees 12:30**

And Jonathan pursued after them and did not overtake them, for they had crossed the Eleutherus River.

**1 Maccabees 12:31**

And Jonathan turned aside against the Arabs called Zabadeans and struck them and took their spoils.

**1 Maccabees 12:32**

And breaking camp, he came to Damascus and passed through all the country.

**1 Maccabees 12:33**

And Simon went out and passed through as far as Ashkelon and the nearby fortresses, and he turned aside to Joppa and seized it beforehand,

**1 Maccabees 12:34**

for he heard that they wanted to hand over the fortress to those with Demetrius; and he placed a garrison there, so that they might keep it.

**1 Maccabees 12:35**

And Jonathan returned and assembled the elders of the people and took counsel with them to build fortresses in Judea,

**1 Maccabees 12:36**

and to raise the walls of Jerusalem higher and to raise a great height between the citadel and the city, to separate it from the city, so that it would be by itself, so that they might neither buy nor sell.

**1 Maccabees 12:37**

And they were gathered to build the city, and they reached the wall of the torrent from the east, and they repaired the place called Chaphenatha.

**1 Maccabees 12:38**

And Simon built Adida in the Shephelah and fortified it and set doors and bars in place.

**1 Maccabees 12:39**

And Tryphon sought to reign over Asia and to put on the diadem and to stretch out a hand against King Antiochus.

**1 Maccabees 12:40**

And he was afraid that Jonathan might not permit him and might war against him, and he was seeking a way to seize Jonathan, to destroy him. And departing, he came to Bethshan.

**1 Maccabees 12:41**

And Jonathan went out to meet him with forty thousand chosen men for battle line and came to Bethshan.

**1 Maccabees 12:42**

And Tryphon saw that Jonathan was present with a great force, and he was cautious about stretching out hands against him,

**1 Maccabees 12:43**

and he received him gloriously and introduced him to all his friends and gave him gifts and commanded his forces to obey him as himself.

**1 Maccabees 12:44**

And he said to Jonathan, Why have you wearied all this people, when no war has arisen for us?

**1 Maccabees 12:45**

And now send them away to their houses, but choose for yourself a few men who will be with you, and come with me to Ptolemais, and I will hand it over to you, and the remaining fortresses and the remaining forces and all those over the needs; and returning, I will go away, for because of this I am here.

**1 Maccabees 12:46**

And trusting him, he did as he said and sent the forces away, and they went away into the land of Judah.

**1 Maccabees 12:47**

But he left with himself three thousand men, of whom he left two thousand in Galilee, and one thousand came with him.

**1 Maccabees 12:48**

But when Jonathan entered into Ptolemais, the Ptolemaeans shut the gates and seized him, and all who entered with him they killed with sword.

**1 Maccabees 12:49**

And Tryphon sent forces and horse into Galilee and the great plain to destroy all those with Jonathan.

**1 Maccabees 12:50**

And they recognized that Jonathan had been seized and had perished, and those with him; and they encouraged themselves and went together, ready for war.

**1 Maccabees 12:51**

And those pursuing saw that it was about their soul, and they returned.

**1 Maccabees 12:52**

And they all came with peace into the land of Judah, and they mourned Jonathan and those with him, and they were very afraid; and all Israel mourned a great mourning.

**1 Maccabees 12:53**

And all the nations around them sought to rub them out, for they said, They have no ruler and helper. Now therefore let us war against them and remove their memorial from among men.

### Chapter 13

**1 Maccabees 13:1**

And Simon heard that Tryphon had gathered a great force to come into the land of Judah and rub it out.

**1 Maccabees 13:2**

And he saw that the people were trembling and afraid, and he went up to Jerusalem and gathered the people

**1 Maccabees 13:3**

and encouraged them and said to them, You yourselves know what I and my brothers and the house of my father have done for the laws and the holy things, and the wars and the distresses that we have seen.

**1 Maccabees 13:4**

For this reason all my brothers perished for the sake of Israel, and I alone have been left.

**1 Maccabees 13:5**

And now may it not happen to me to spare my soul in any time of affliction, for I am not better than my brothers.

**1 Maccabees 13:6**

But I will make vengeance for my nation and for the holy things and for our wives and children, because all the nations have gathered together to rub us out for the sake of enmity.

**1 Maccabees 13:7**

And the spirit of the people was rekindled as they heard these words,

**1 Maccabees 13:8**

and they answered with a great voice, saying, You are our leader in place of Judas and Jonathan your brother.

**1 Maccabees 13:9**

Fight our war, and all that you say to us, we will do.

**1 Maccabees 13:10**

And he gathered all the warrior men and hastened to finish the walls of Jerusalem and fortified it around.

**1 Maccabees 13:11**

And he sent Jonathan son of Abessalom, and with him a sufficient force, to Joppa, and he cast out those who were in it and remained there in it.

**1 Maccabees 13:12**

And Tryphon departed from Ptolemais with a great force to enter into the land of Judah, and Jonathan was with him under guard.

**1 Maccabees 13:13**

But Simon camped in Adida, opposite the plain.

**1 Maccabees 13:14**

And Tryphon recognized that Simon had risen up in place of Jonathan his brother and that he was about to join war with him, and he sent ambassadors to him, saying,

**1 Maccabees 13:15**

Concerning the silver that Jonathan your brother owed to the royal treasury, because of the needs that he had, we are holding him.

**1 Maccabees 13:16**

And now send one hundred talents of silver and two of his sons as hostages, so that when released he may not revolt from us, and we will release him.

**1 Maccabees 13:17**

And Simon knew that they were speaking to him with deceit, but he sent to take the silver and the young children, lest he should take up great hostility toward the people,

**1 Maccabees 13:18**

saying, Because I did not send him the silver and the young children, he perished.

**1 Maccabees 13:19**

And he sent the young children and the one hundred talents, and he lied and did not release Jonathan.

**1 Maccabees 13:20**

And after these things Tryphon came to set foot in the country and rub it out, and he went around by the way to Adora. And Simon and his camp were moving opposite him in every place wherever he went.

**1 Maccabees 13:21**

But those from the citadel were sending ambassadors to Tryphon, hurrying him to come to them through the wilderness and to send them foods.

**1 Maccabees 13:22**

And Tryphon prepared all his horse to come that night, and there was very much snow, and he did not come because of the snow. And he departed and came into Gilead.

**1 Maccabees 13:23**

But when he drew near to Baskama, he killed Jonathan, and he was buried there.

**1 Maccabees 13:24**

And Tryphon returned and went away into his own land.

**1 Maccabees 13:25**

And Simon sent and took the bones of Jonathan his brother and buried them in Modein, the city of his fathers.

**1 Maccabees 13:26**

And all Israel lamented him with a great lamentation and mourned him many days.

**1 Maccabees 13:27**

And Simon built over the tomb of his father and his brothers and raised it high to view, with polished stone behind and in front.

**1 Maccabees 13:28**

And he set up seven pyramids upon it, one opposite another, for his father and his mother and his four brothers.

**1 Maccabees 13:29**

And for these he made devices, setting great pillars around, and on the pillars he made suits of armor for an eternal name, and beside the suits of armor carved ships, to be seen by all who sail the sea.

**1 Maccabees 13:30**

This is the tomb that he made in Modein, until this day.

**1 Maccabees 13:31**

But Tryphon was going with deceit with Antiochus the younger king, and he killed him,

**1 Maccabees 13:32**

and reigned instead of him and put on the diadem of Asia and made a great blow upon the land.

**1 Maccabees 13:33**

And Simon built the fortresses of Judea and walled them around with high towers and great walls and gates and bars, and he put foods in the fortresses.

**1 Maccabees 13:34**

And Simon chose men and sent to King Demetrius to make release for the country, because all the acts of Tryphon were plunderings.

**1 Maccabees 13:35**

And King Demetrius sent to him according to these words and answered him and wrote him such a letter:

**1 Maccabees 13:36**

King Demetrius to Simon, high priest and friend of kings, and to elders and nation of Jews, greeting.

**1 Maccabees 13:37**

We have received the golden crown and the palm branch that you sent, and we are ready to make great peace with you and to write to those over the needs to remit releases to you.

**1 Maccabees 13:38**

And whatever things we have established toward you stand established, and the fortresses that you have built shall belong to you.

**1 Maccabees 13:39**

And we remit errors and sins until this day, and the crown that you owed; and if anything else was being taxed in Jerusalem, let it no longer be taxed.

**1 Maccabees 13:40**

And if any of you are suitable to be enrolled among those around us, let them be enrolled, and let peace come to be between us. [41] In the one hundred seventieth year, the yoke of the nations was lifted from Israel,

**1 Maccabees 13:42**

and the people of Israel began to write in the documents and contracts: In the first year under Simon, great high priest and general and leader of Jews.

**1 Maccabees 13:43**

In those days Simon camped against Gazara and surrounded it with camps, and he made siege towers and brought one to the city and struck one tower and seized it.

**1 Maccabees 13:44**

And those in the siege tower leapt into the city, and a great commotion came to be in the city.

**1 Maccabees 13:45**

And those in the city went up with their wives and children upon the wall, having torn their garments, and they cried with a great voice, asking Simon to give them right hands,

**1 Maccabees 13:46**

and they said, Do not deal with us according to our evils, but according to your mercy.

**1 Maccabees 13:47**

And Simon was reconciled to them and did not fight them. And he cast them out from the city and cleansed the houses in which the idols were, and so he entered into it, hymning and blessing.

**1 Maccabees 13:48**

And he cast every uncleanness out of it and settled there men who do the law, and he fortified it further and built a dwelling for himself in it.

**1 Maccabees 13:49**

But those from the citadel in Jerusalem were prevented from going out and entering into the country and buying and selling, and they became very hungry, and a sufficient number of them perished by famine.

**1 Maccabees 13:50**
