Let all things be known to our lord the king, that when we came into the country of Judea and arrived at the city Jerusalem, we found the elders of the Jews from the captivity in the city Jerusalem, building a great new house for the Lord with expensive polished stones, with wood being placed in the walls.

**1 Esdras 6:9**

And those works are being done with haste, and the work is prospering in their hands and being completed with all glory and care.

**1 Esdras 6:10**

Then we inquired of these elders, saying, By whose command are you building this house and founding these works?

**1 Esdras 6:11**

Therefore we questioned them for the sake of making known to you and writing to you the men who were leading, and we asked them the name-registration of those presiding.

**1 Esdras 6:12**

And they answered us, saying, We are servants of the Lord who created Heaven and Earth.

**1 Esdras 6:13**

And the house had been built many years before by a great and strong king of Israel, and it was completed.

**1 Esdras 6:14**

And when our fathers, having provoked bitterly, sinned against the heavenly Lord of Israel, he gave them into the hands of Nebuchadnezzar, king of Babylon, king of the Chaldeans.

**1 Esdras 6:15**

And after pulling down the house, they burned it with fire and took the people captive to Babylon.

**1 Esdras 6:16**

But in the first year of Cyrus reigning over the country of Babylonia, King Cyrus wrote to build this house.

**1 Esdras 6:17**

And the holy vessels, the gold and silver ones, which Nebuchadnezzar brought out from the house in Jerusalem and had set aside in his own temple, King Cyrus again brought them out from the temple in Babylonia, and they were handed over to Sabanassar Zerubbabel the governor.

**1 Esdras 6:18**

And it was commanded to him, and he carried away all these vessels to deposit them in the temple in Jerusalem, and that the temple of the Lord should be built on the place.

**1 Esdras 6:19**

Then Sabanassar came and laid the foundations of the house of the Lord in Jerusalem, and from then until now it has been being built but has not received completion.

**1 Esdras 6:20**

Now therefore, if it is judged good, O king, let search be made in the royal libraries of Cyrus.

**1 Esdras 6:21**

And if it is found that the building of the house of the Lord in Jerusalem happened with the judgment of King Cyrus, and it is judged good by our lord the king, let him address us concerning these things.

**1 Esdras 6:22**

Then King Darius commanded search to be made in the libraries lying in Babylon, and in Ecbatana the fortress in the country of Media, one scroll was found, in which these things had been recorded:

**1 Esdras 6:23**

In the first year of Cyrus reigning, King Cyrus commanded the house of the Lord in Jerusalem to be built, where they sacrifice with continual fire,

**1 Esdras 6:24**

whose height is 60 cubits and width 60 cubits, with three courses of polished stone and one course of new local wood, and the expense to be given from the house of King Cyrus,

**1 Esdras 6:25**

and the holy vessels of the house of the Lord, both gold and silver, that Nebuchadnezzar brought out from the house in Jerusalem and carried away to Babylon, to be restored to the house in Jerusalem, where they had been lying, so that they may be put there.

**1 Esdras 6:26**

And he commanded Sisinēs, governor of Syria and Phoenicia, and Sathrabouzanes and the fellow-companions and the leaders appointed in Syria and Phoenicia, to take care to keep away from the place, but to permit the servant of the Lord, Zerubbabel, governor of Judea, and the elders of the Jews to build that house of the Lord on the place.

**1 Esdras 6:27**

And I also have commanded that it be built wholly, and to see carefully that they work together with those from the captivity of Judea until the house of the Lord is completed.

**1 Esdras 6:28**

And from the taxation of Coele-Syria and Phoenicia, an exact allotment is to be given to these men for sacrifice to the Lord, to Zerubbabel the governor, for bulls and rams and lambs,

**1 Esdras 6:29**

and likewise wheat and salt and wine and oil continually year by year, as the priests in Jerusalem may dictate to be spent day by day without dispute,

**1 Esdras 6:30**

so that libations may be offered to God Most High for the king and the children, and that they may pray concerning their life.

**1 Esdras 6:31**

And I have commanded that whoever transgresses or annuls any of the things written, a beam be taken from his own things and he be hanged upon it, and his possessions become royal.

**1 Esdras 6:32**

Because of these things, may the Lord, whose name has been called there, make vanish every king and nation who stretches out his hand to hinder or harm that house of the Lord in Jerusalem.

**1 Esdras 6:33**

I, King Darius, have decreed that these things be done carefully.

### Chapter 7

**1 Esdras 7:1**

Then Sisinēs, governor of Coele-Syria and Phoenicia, and Sathrabouzanes and the fellow-companions, following closely the things commanded by King Darius,

**1 Esdras 7:2**

were overseeing the holy works more carefully, working together with the elders of the Jews and the temple officers.

**1 Esdras 7:3**

And the holy works became prosperous while Haggai and Zechariah the prophets were prophesying.

**1 Esdras 7:4**

And they completed these things by commandment of the Lord God of Israel, and with the judgment of Cyrus and Darius and Artaxerxes, kings of Persians.

**1 Esdras 7:5**

And the holy house was completed by the twenty-third day of the month Adar, in the sixth year of King Darius.

**1 Esdras 7:6**

And the sons of Israel and the priests and the Levites and the remaining ones from the captivity who had been added did according to the things in the book of Moses.

**1 Esdras 7:7**

And they brought for the dedication of the temple of the Lord 100 bulls, 200 rams, 400 lambs,

**1 Esdras 7:8**

and 12 male goats for the sin of all Israel, according to the number of the 12 tribe-rulers of Israel.

**1 Esdras 7:9**

And the priests and the Levites stood according to tribes, clothed for the works of the Lord God of Israel, according to the book of Moses, and the gatekeepers at each gate.

**1 Esdras 7:10**

And the sons of Israel from the captivity kept the Passover on the fourteenth day of the first month, because the priests and the Levites had been purified together,

**1 Esdras 7:11**

and all the sons of the captivity, because they had been purified, because the Levites had all been purified together.

**1 Esdras 7:12**

And they sacrificed the Passover for all the sons of the captivity and for their brothers the priests and for themselves.

**1 Esdras 7:13**

And the sons of Israel from the captivity ate, all those who had separated from the abominations of the nations of the land, seeking the Lord.

**1 Esdras 7:14**

And they kept the feast of unleavened bread seven days, rejoicing before the Lord,

**1 Esdras 7:15**

because he had turned the counsel of the king of Assyrians toward them, to strengthen their hands for the works of the Lord God of Israel.

### Chapter 8

**1 Esdras 8:1**

And later than these things, when Artaxerxes king of the Persians was reigning, Ezra son of Saraios, son of Ezechrios, son of Chelkias, son of Salem, went up,

**1 Esdras 8:2**

son of Saddouk, son of Achitob, son of Amarios, son of Ozios, son of Bokka, son of Abisai, son of Phinehas, son of Eleazar, son of Aaron the first priest.

**1 Esdras 8:3**

This Ezra went up from Babylon, being a skilled scribe in the law of Moses given out by the God of Israel,

**1 Esdras 8:4**

and the king gave him glory, since he found favor before him in all his requests.

**1 Esdras 8:5**

And some from the sons of Israel and the priests and Levites and temple singers and gatekeepers and temple servants went up together to Jerusalem

**1 Esdras 8:6**

in the seventh year of Artaxerxes reigning, in the fifth month; this was the seventh year for the king. For departing from Babylon on the new moon of the first month, they came to Jerusalem according to the prosperity given to them from the Lord upon him.

**1 Esdras 8:7**

For Ezra contained much knowledge so as to leave aside nothing from the law of the Lord and from the commandments, to teach all Israel ordinances and judgments.

**1 Esdras 8:8**

Now when the written command from King Artaxerxes came to Ezra the priest and reader of the law of the Lord, of which the following is a copy:

**1 Esdras 8:9**

King Artaxerxes to Ezra the priest and reader of the law of the Lord, greeting.

**1 Esdras 8:10**

And I, judging humane things, have commanded those who wish from the nation of the Jews, choosing, and from the priests and the Levites, and these in our kingdom, to go with you to Jerusalem.

**1 Esdras 8:11**

Therefore as many as are mindful, let them set out together, just as it seemed good to me and to the seven friend-counselors,

**1 Esdras 8:12**

so that they may inspect the things concerning Judea and Jerusalem according to what it has in the law of the Lord,

**1 Esdras 8:13**

and carry away gifts to the Lord of Israel, which I and the friends vowed, to Jerusalem, and all gold and silver that may be found in the country of Babylonia for the Lord in Jerusalem, together with what has been gifted by the nation to the temple of the Lord their God in Jerusalem,

**1 Esdras 8:14**

to be gathered, both the gold and the silver, for bulls and rams and lambs and the things following these,

**1 Esdras 8:15**

so as to offer sacrifices to the Lord upon the altar of the Lord their God in Jerusalem.

**1 Esdras 8:16**

And all that you may wish to do with your brothers with gold and silver, complete according to the will of your God,

**1 Esdras 8:17**

and the holy vessels of the Lord that are given to you for the need of the temple of your God in Jerusalem,

**1 Esdras 8:18**

and whatever other things fall to you for the need of the temple of your God, you shall give from the royal treasury.

**1 Esdras 8:19**

And I, behold, King Artaxerxes, have commanded the treasurers of Syria and Phoenicia that whatever Ezra the priest and reader of the law of the God Most High sends for, they shall carefully give, up to 100 talents of silver,

**1 Esdras 8:20**

and likewise up to 100 cors of wheat and 100 measures of wine,

**1 Esdras 8:21**

and other things from abundance. Let all things be carefully completed according to the law of God for God Most High, lest wrath come upon the kingdom of the king and his sons.

**1 Esdras 8:22**

And to you also it is said that upon all the priests and Levites and temple singers and gatekeepers and temple servants and workers of this temple, not one tax nor any other imposition shall come to be, and no one shall have authority to lay anything upon them.

**1 Esdras 8:23**

And you, Ezra, according to the wisdom of God, appoint judges and magistrates, so that they may judge in all Syria and Phoenicia all those who know the law of your God; and those who do not know it, you shall teach.

**1 Esdras 8:24**

And all who transgress the law of your God and the royal law shall be carefully punished, whether by death, or by punishment, or by a silver penalty, or by deportation.

**1 Esdras 8:25**

And Ezra the scribe said, Blessed alone is the Lord, the God of my fathers, who gave these things into the heart of the king, to glorify his house in Jerusalem,

**1 Esdras 8:26**

and he honored me before the king and the counselors and all his friends and great men.

**1 Esdras 8:27**

And I became bold according to the support of the Lord my God, and I gathered men from Israel to go up together with me.

**1 Esdras 8:28**

And these are the leaders according to their paternal houses and divisions who went up with me from Babylon in the reign of King Artaxerxes:

**1 Esdras 8:29**

From the sons of Phinehas, Gerson; from the sons of Ithamar, Gamaliel; from the sons of David, Lattous son of Sechenias;

**1 Esdras 8:30**

from the sons of Phoros, Zacharias, and with him were enrolled 150 men;

**1 Esdras 8:31**

from the sons of Phaath Moab, Eliaonias son of Zaraios, and with him 200 men;

**1 Esdras 8:32**

from the sons of Zathoes, Sechenias son of Jezelos, and with him 300 men; from the sons of Adin, Obeth son of Jonathan, and with him 250 men;

**1 Esdras 8:33**

from the sons of Elam, Jesias son of Gotholias, and with him 70 men;

**1 Esdras 8:34**

from the sons of Saphatias, Zaraias son of Michael, and with him 70 men;

**1 Esdras 8:35**

from the sons of Joab, Abadias son of Jezelos, and with him 212 men;

**1 Esdras 8:36**

from the sons of Banias, Salimoth son of Josaphias, and with him 160 men;

**1 Esdras 8:37**

from the sons of Babi, Zacharias son of Bebai, and with him 28 men;

**1 Esdras 8:38**

from the sons of Astath, John son of Akatan, and with him 110 men;

**1 Esdras 8:39**

from the sons of Adonikam, the last ones, and these are their names: Eliphala son of Geouel and Samaias, and with them 70 men;

**1 Esdras 8:40**

from the sons of Bago, Outhi son of Istalkour, and with him 70 men.

**1 Esdras 8:41**

And I gathered them at the river called Thera, and we camped there three days, and I examined them.

**1 Esdras 8:42**

And from the priests and from the Levites I found none there.

**1 Esdras 8:43**

And I sent to Eleazar and Idouelos and Maia and Masman and Alnathan and Samaia and Joribos, Nathan, Ennatan, Zacharias, and Mosollamos, the leaders and knowledgeable men,

**1 Esdras 8:44**

and I told them to go to Loddaeos, the leader in the place of the treasury,

**1 Esdras 8:45**

commanding them to speak with Loddaeos and his brothers and the treasurers in the place, to send us those who would serve as priests in the house of our Lord.

**1 Esdras 8:46**

And according to the mighty hand of our Lord, they brought us knowledgeable men from the sons of Mooli, son of Levi, son of Israel: Asebebias and his sons and brothers, 18 in all,

**1 Esdras 8:47**

and Asebia and Annouos and Osaias, a brother from the sons of Chanounaios, and their sons, 20 men.

**1 Esdras 8:48**

And from the temple servants, whom David and the leaders gave for the work of the Levites, 220 temple servants; the name-registration of all was marked.

**1 Esdras 8:49**

And I vowed a fast there for the young men before our Lord,

**1 Esdras 8:50**

to seek from him prosperity for us and for our children and cattle who were with us.

**1 Esdras 8:51**

For I was ashamed to ask the king for foot soldiers and horsemen and an escort for safety against those opposing us.

**1 Esdras 8:52**

For we had told the king that the strength of our Lord would be with those seeking him for every restoration.

**1 Esdras 8:53**

And again we entreated our Lord for all these things, and we obtained favor.

**1 Esdras 8:54**

And I separated from the tribe-rulers of the priests 12 men, and Eserebias and Samias, and with them 12 men from their brothers,

**1 Esdras 8:55**

and I set before them the silver and the gold and the holy vessels of the house of our Lord, which the king and his counselors and the great men and all Israel had gifted.

**1 Esdras 8:56**

And having weighed them, I handed over to them 650 talents of silver, and silver vessels of 100 talents, and 100 talents of gold, and 20 golden objects, and 12 bronze vessels of good bronze, gleaming like gold.

**1 Esdras 8:57**

And I said to them, You also are holy to the Lord, and the vessels are holy, and the gold and the silver are a vow to the Lord, the Lord of our fathers.

**1 Esdras 8:58**

Keep watch and guard them until you hand them over to the tribe-rulers of the priests and the Levites and to the leaders of the paternal houses of Israel in Jerusalem, in the chambers of the house of our God.

**1 Esdras 8:59**

And the priests and the Levites who received the silver and the gold and the vessels brought them into Jerusalem, into the temple of the Lord.

**1 Esdras 8:60**

And departing from the river Thera on the twelfth day of the first month, we came into Jerusalem according to the mighty hand of our Lord upon us. And he rescued us from every enemy on the way in, and we came into Jerusalem.

**1 Esdras 8:61**

And after three days there, on the fourth day the weighed silver and gold was handed over in the house of our Lord to Marmoth son of Ourias the priest. [62] And with him was Eleazar son of Phinehas, and with him were Josabdos son of Jesus and Moeth son of Sabannos, the Levites. Everything was by number and weight, and all their weight was written at that same hour.

**1 Esdras 8:63**

And those who had come from the captivity brought sacrifices to the God of Israel, the Lord: 12 bulls for all Israel, 96 rams, 72 lambs, and 12 male goats for salvation; all were a sacrifice to the Lord.

**1 Esdras 8:64**

And they gave the commandments of the king to the royal stewards and to the governors of Coele-Syria and Phoenicia, and they glorified the nation and the temple of the Lord.

**1 Esdras 8:65**

And when these things were completed, the leaders came to me, saying,

**1 Esdras 8:66**

The nation of Israel and the rulers and the priests and the Levites have not separated from the foreign nations of the land and their uncleannesses, the Canaanites and Hittites and Perizzites and Jebusites and Moabites and Egyptians and Idumeans.

**1 Esdras 8:67**

For they have dwelt together with their daughters, both they and their sons, and the holy seed has been mixed into the foreign nations of the land; and the leaders and the great men have shared in this lawlessness from the beginning of the matter.

**1 Esdras 8:68**

And as soon as I heard these things, I tore my garments and the sacred clothing and plucked out some of the hair of my head and beard, and I sat thoughtful and deeply grieved.

**1 Esdras 8:69**

And all who were ever moved by the word of the Lord God of Israel gathered to me, while I was mourning over the lawlessness; and I sat deeply grieved until the evening sacrifice.

**1 Esdras 8:70**

And rising from the fast, having my garments and sacred clothing torn, bending my knees and stretching out my hands to the Lord, I said,

**1 Esdras 8:71**

Lord, I am ashamed and confounded before your face,

**1 Esdras 8:72**

for our sins have multiplied over our heads, and our ignorances have carried up to Heaven

**1 Esdras 8:73**

even from the times of our fathers, and we are in great sin until this day.

**1 Esdras 8:74**

And because of our sins and the sins of our fathers, we were handed over, with our brothers and with our kings and with our priests, to the kings of the earth, to sword and captivity and plunder with shame, until this day.

**1 Esdras 8:75**

And now, by some small measure, mercy has come to us from you, Lord, for a root and a name to be left to us in the place of your sanctuary,

**1 Esdras 8:76**

and for a light-bearer to be uncovered for us in the house of the Lord our God, to give us nourishment in the time of our slavery. And while we were serving, we were not abandoned by our Lord,

**1 Esdras 8:77**

but he made us be in favor before the kings of Persians, to give us nourishment

**1 Esdras 8:78**

and to glorify the temple of our Lord and to raise desolate Zion, to give us a firm support in Judea and Jerusalem.

**1 Esdras 8:79**

And now what shall we say, Lord, having these things? For we transgressed your commandments, which you gave by the hand of your servants the prophets, saying,

**1 Esdras 8:80**

The land into which you are entering to inherit is a land defiled with the defilement of the foreigners of the land, and they have filled it with their uncleanness.

**1 Esdras 8:81**

And now do not make your daughters dwell together with their sons, and do not take their daughters for your sons.

**1 Esdras 8:82**

And you shall not seek to make peace with them for all time, so that, becoming strong, you may eat the good things of the land and leave it as an inheritance to your children forever.

**1 Esdras 8:83**

And all the things happening to us happen because of our evil works and our great sins. For you, Lord, lightened our sins and

**1 Esdras 8:84**

gave us such a root; again we turned back to transgress your law by being mixed with the uncleanness of the nations of the land.

**1 Esdras 8:85**

Were you not angry with us to destroy us until no root and seed and name of ours were left?

**1 Esdras 8:86**

Lord of Israel, you are true, for we have been left as a root today.

**1 Esdras 8:87**

Behold, now we are before you in our lawless deeds, for it is no longer possible to stand before you because of these things.

**1 Esdras 8:88**

And while Ezra was praying and confessing, weeping and fallen to the ground before the temple, a very great crowd gathered to him from Jerusalem, men and women and young people, for there was great weeping among the multitude.

**1 Esdras 8:89**

And Jeconiah son of Jeel, from the sons of Israel, cried out and said, Ezra, we have sinned against the Lord and have made foreign women from the nations of the land dwell with us. And now this matter is over all Israel.

**1 Esdras 8:90**

In this, let there be oath-swearing for us toward the Lord, to cast out all our wives from the foreigners, with their children, as it has been judged by you and by as many as obey the law of the Lord.

**1 Esdras 8:91**

Rise up and complete it, for the matter is toward you, and we are with you to make strength.

**1 Esdras 8:92**

And Ezra rose and made the tribe-rulers of the priests and Levites of all Israel swear to do according to these things; and they swore.

### Chapter 9

**1 Esdras 9:1**

And Ezra rose from the court of the temple and went to the chamber of Jonas son of Eliasib.

**1 Esdras 9:2**

And lodging there, he tasted no bread and drank no water, mourning over the great lawless deeds of the multitude.

**1 Esdras 9:3**

And a proclamation came to be in all Judea and Jerusalem for all those from the captivity to be gathered into Jerusalem.

**1 Esdras 9:4**

And as many as did not meet within two or three days, according to the judgment of the presiding elders, their cattle would be consecrated, and he himself would be alienated from the multitude of the captivity.

**1 Esdras 9:5**

And all those from the tribe of Judah and Benjamin were gathered in three days into Jerusalem; this was the ninth month, on the twentieth of the month.

**1 Esdras 9:6**

And all the multitude sat together in the broad place of the temple, trembling because of the present winter.

**1 Esdras 9:7**

And Ezra rose and said to them, You acted lawlessly and made foreign women dwell with you, to add sins to Israel.

**1 Esdras 9:8**

And now give confession and glory to the Lord God of our fathers,

**1 Esdras 9:9**

and do his will, and be separated from the nations of the land and from the foreign women.

**1 Esdras 9:10**

And all the multitude shouted and said with a great voice, Just as you have spoken, we will do.

**1 Esdras 9:11**

But the multitude is large, and it is winter weather, and we do not have strength to stand in the open air, and the work is not for us of one day or two, for we have sinned greatly in these things.

**1 Esdras 9:12**

But let the leaders of the multitude stand, and let all those from our dwellings who have foreign wives come at an appointed time,

**1 Esdras 9:13**

and the elders and judges of each place, until the wrath of the Lord is loosed from us concerning this matter.

**1 Esdras 9:14**

Jonathan son of Azael and Ezekias son of Theokanos accepted these things, and Mosollamos and Leuis and Sabbataios judged together with them.

**1 Esdras 9:15**

And those from the captivity did according to all these things.

**1 Esdras 9:16**

And Ezra the priest chose for himself men, leaders of their paternal houses, all by name, and they were shut in at the new moon of the tenth month to examine the matter.

**1 Esdras 9:17**

And the matters concerning the men holding foreign women were brought to an end by the new moon of the first month.

**1 Esdras 9:18**

And from the priests, those who had gathered and were found having foreign wives were:

**1 Esdras 9:19**

from the sons of Jesus son of Josedek and his brothers, Mathelas and Eleazar and Joribos and Joadanos.

**1 Esdras 9:20**

And they laid on their hands to cast out their wives, and for atonement, rams for their ignorance.

**1 Esdras 9:21**

And from the sons of Emmer: Ananias and Zabadaios and Manes and Samaios and Jereel and Azarias.

**1 Esdras 9:22**

And from the sons of Phaisour: Elionais, Massias, Ishmael, Nathanael, Okodelos, and Saloas.

**1 Esdras 9:23**

And from the Levites: Jozabados and Semeis and Koios, that is Kalitas, and Pathaios and Judas and Jonas.

**1 Esdras 9:24**

From the temple singers: Eliasebos, Bacchouros.

**1 Esdras 9:25**

From the gatekeepers: Saloumos and Tolbanes.

**1 Esdras 9:26**

From Israel, from the sons of Phoros: Jermas and Jezias and Melchias and Maelos and Eleazar and Asebias and Banaias.

**1 Esdras 9:27**

From the sons of Ela: Matthanias, Zacharias, and Jezrielos, and Joabdios, and Jeremoth, and Aidias.

**1 Esdras 9:28**

And from the sons of Zamoth: Eliadas, Eliasimos, Othonias, Jarimoth, and Sabathos and Zeralias.

**1 Esdras 9:29**

And from the sons of Bebai: John and Ananias and Jozabdos and Amathias.

**1 Esdras 9:30**

From the sons of Mani: Olamus, Mamouchos, Jedaios, Jasoubos, and Jasaelos, and Jeremoth.

**1 Esdras 9:31**

And from the sons of Addi: Naathos and Moosias, Lakkounos and Naidos, Matthanias and Sesthel; and Balnouos and Manassias.

**1 Esdras 9:32**

And from the sons of Anan: Elionas and Asaias and Melchias and Sabbaios and Simon Chosamaios.

**1 Esdras 9:33**

And from the sons of Asom: Altanaios and Mattathias and Sabannaios and Eliphalat and Manasses and Semei.

**1 Esdras 9:34**

And from the sons of Baani: Jeremias, Momdios, Ismaeros, Jouel, Mabdai, and Pedias and Anos, Rabasion and Enasibos and Mamnitanaios, Eliasib, Bannous, Eliali, Someis, Selemias, Nathanias; and from the sons of Ezora: Sesis, Esrel, Azael, Samatos, Zambri, Joseph.

**1 Esdras 9:35**

And from the sons of Ethma: Mazitias, Zabadaias, Edais, Jouel, Banaias.

**1 Esdras 9:36**

All these made foreign women dwell with them, and they released them with their children.

**1 Esdras 9:37**

And the priests and the Levites and those from Israel lived in Jerusalem and in the country at the new moon of the seventh month, and the sons of Israel were in their dwellings.

**1 Esdras 9:38**

And all the multitude was gathered with one accord into the broad place by the temple gate toward the east,

**1 Esdras 9:39**

and they told Ezra the priest and reader, Bring the law of Moses, which was handed down by the Lord God of Israel.

**1 Esdras 9:40**

And Ezra the high priest brought the law to the whole multitude, from man to woman, and to all the priests, to hear the law at the new moon of the seventh month.

**1 Esdras 9:41**

And he read in the broad place before the temple gate, from daybreak until midday, before both men and women, and all the multitude gave their mind to the law.

**1 Esdras 9:42**

And Ezra the priest and reader of the law stood upon the wooden platform that had been prepared,

**1 Esdras 9:43**

and beside him stood Mattathias, Sammous, Ananias, Azarias, Ourias, Ezekias, Baalsamos, on the right,

**1 Esdras 9:44**

and on the left, Phaldaios and Misael, Melchias, Lothasoubos, Nabarias, Zacharias.

**1 Esdras 9:45**

And Ezra took up the book before the multitude and sat before all in glory,

**1 Esdras 9:46**

and when he opened the law, all stood upright. And Ezra blessed the Lord God Most High, God Sabaoth Almighty,

**1 Esdras 9:47**

and all the multitude answered, Amen. And lifting up their hands above, falling upon the ground, they worshiped the Lord.

**1 Esdras 9:48**

Jesus and Anniouth and Sarabias and Jadinos and Jakoubos, Sabbataios, Autaias, Maiannas and Kalitas, Azarias and Jozabdos and Ananias, Phalias, the Levites, were teaching the law of the Lord and reading the law of the Lord to the multitude, making the reading clear at the same time.

**1 Esdras 9:49**

And Attharates said to Ezra the high priest and reader and to the Levites who were teaching the multitude, to all of them,

**1 Esdras 9:50**

This day is holy to the Lord. And all were weeping as they heard the law.

**1 Esdras 9:51**

Therefore go, eat fat things and drink sweet things, and send portions to those who do not have,

**1 Esdras 9:52**

for the day is holy to the Lord. And do not be grieved, for the Lord will glorify you.

**1 Esdras 9:53**

And the Levites were commanding all the people, saying, This day is holy; do not be grieved.

**1 Esdras 9:54**

And all went away to eat and drink and rejoice and give portions to those who did not have, and to rejoice greatly,

**1 Esdras 9:55**

because they were made to understand the sayings in which they had been taught, and they were gathered together.

## 2 Esdras

### Chapter 1

**2 Esdras 1:1**

And in the first year of Cyrus king of the Persians, for fulfillment of the word of the Lord from the mouth of Jeremiah, the Lord stirred the spirit of Cyrus king of the Persians, and he proclaimed with a voice through all his kingdom, also in writing, saying,

**2 Esdras 1:2**

Thus said Cyrus king of the Persians: The Lord God of heaven gave me all kingdoms of the earth, and he charged me to build for him a house in Jerusalem, the one in Judah.

**2 Esdras 1:3**

Who among you is from all his people? Let his God be with him, and let him go up to Jerusalem in Judah and build the house of God of Israel. He is the God in Jerusalem.

**2 Esdras 1:4**

And everyone left behind from all the places where he sojourns there, let men of his place support him with silver and gold and goods and cattle, besides the voluntary gift for the house of God in Jerusalem.

**2 Esdras 1:5**

Then the heads of father-houses of Judah and Benjamin rose up, and priests and Levites, all whose spirit God stirred to go up to build the house of the Lord in Jerusalem.

**2 Esdras 1:6**

And all around them strengthened their hands with silver vessels, with gold, with goods and cattle and gifts, besides all the voluntary offerings.

**2 Esdras 1:7**

And King Cyrus brought out vessels of the house of the Lord, which Nebuchadnezzar took from Jerusalem and placed in the house of his god.

**2 Esdras 1:8**

And Cyrus king of the Persians brought them out by the hand of Mithredath the treasurer and counted them out to Sheshbazzar, governor of Judah.

**2 Esdras 1:9**

And this their number: thirty golden basins, one thousand silver basins, twenty-nine substitute vessels,

**2 Esdras 1:10**

thirty golden bowls, two hundred silver bowls, and one thousand other vessels.

**2 Esdras 1:11**

All the vessels, in gold and silver, were five thousand four hundred. Sheshbazzar brought them all up with those coming up from captivity, from Babylon to Jerusalem.

### Chapter 2

**2 Esdras 2:1**

And these are sons of the province, those coming up from the captivity of the exile whom Nebuchadnezzar king of Babylon exiled to Babylon, and they returned to Jerusalem and Judah, each to his city.

**2 Esdras 2:2**

Those who came with Zerubbabel were Jeshua, Nehemiah, Seraiah, Reelaiah, Mordecai, Bilshan, Mizpar, Bigvai, Rehum, and Baana. Number of men of the people of Israel:

**2 Esdras 2:3**

Sons of Parosh: 2,172.

**2 Esdras 2:4**

Sons of Shephatiah: 372.

**2 Esdras 2:5**

Sons of Hara: 775.

**2 Esdras 2:6**

Sons of Pahathmoab, to the sons of Iesoue-Joab: 2,812.

**2 Esdras 2:7**

Sons of Elam: 1,254.

**2 Esdras 2:8**

Sons of Zattu: 945.

**2 Esdras 2:9**

Sons of Zaccai: 760.

**2 Esdras 2:10**

Sons of Bani: 642.

**2 Esdras 2:11**

Sons of Bebai: 623.

**2 Esdras 2:12**

Sons of Azgad: 3,222.

**2 Esdras 2:13**

Sons of Adonikam: 666.

**2 Esdras 2:14**

Sons of Bigvai: 2,056.

**2 Esdras 2:15**

Sons of Adin: 454.

**2 Esdras 2:16**

Sons of Ater, of Hezekiah: 98.

**2 Esdras 2:17**

Sons of Basou: 323.

**2 Esdras 2:18**

Sons of Jorah: 112.

**2 Esdras 2:19**

Sons of Hashum: 223.

**2 Esdras 2:20**

Sons of Gibbar: 95.

**2 Esdras 2:21**

Sons of Bethlehem: 123.

**2 Esdras 2:22**

Sons of Netophah: 56.

**2 Esdras 2:23**

Sons of Anathoth: 128.

**2 Esdras 2:24**

Sons of Azmaveth: 42.

**2 Esdras 2:25**

Sons of Kirjathjearim, Chephirah, and Beeroth: 743.

**2 Esdras 2:26**

Sons of Ramah and Gibeah: 621.

**2 Esdras 2:27**

Men of Michmash: 122.

**2 Esdras 2:28**

Men of Bethel and Ai: 423.

**2 Esdras 2:29**

Sons of Nebo: 52.

**2 Esdras 2:30**

Sons of Magbish: 156.

**2 Esdras 2:31**

Sons of Elam: 1,254.

**2 Esdras 2:32**

Sons of Harim: 320.

**2 Esdras 2:33**

Sons of Lod, Aroth, and Ono: 725.

**2 Esdras 2:34**

Sons of Jericho: 345.

**2 Esdras 2:35**

Sons of Senaah: 3,630.

**2 Esdras 2:36**

And the priests: sons of Iedoua, of the house of Jeshua, 973.

**2 Esdras 2:37**

Sons of Immer: 1,052.

**2 Esdras 2:38**

Sons of Pashur: 1,247.

**2 Esdras 2:39**

Sons of Harim: 1,007.

**2 Esdras 2:40**

And the Levites: sons of Jeshua and Kadmiel, to the sons of Hodaiah, 74.

**2 Esdras 2:41**

The singers: sons of Asaph, 128.

**2 Esdras 2:42**

Sons of the gatekeepers: sons of Shallum, sons of Ater, sons of Talmon, sons of Akkub, sons of Hatita, sons of Shobai, all together 139.

**2 Esdras 2:43**

The nethinim: sons of Sia, sons of Asouph, sons of Tabbaoth,

**2 Esdras 2:44**

sons of Keros, sons of Soeia, sons of Padon,

**2 Esdras 2:45**

sons of Labano, sons of Hagabah, sons of Akkub,

**2 Esdras 2:46**

sons of Hagab, sons of Shalmai, sons of Anan,

**2 Esdras 2:47**

sons of Giddel, sons of Gaer, sons of Reaiah,

**2 Esdras 2:48**

sons of Rezin, sons of Nekoda, sons of Gazzam,

**2 Esdras 2:49**

sons of Ousa, sons of Paseah, sons of Besai,

**2 Esdras 2:50**

sons of Asena, sons of Maonim, sons of Nephusim,

**2 Esdras 2:51**

sons of Bakbuk, sons of Hakupha, sons of Harhur,

**2 Esdras 2:52**

sons of Bazluth, sons of Mehida, sons of Harsha,

**2 Esdras 2:53**

sons of Barkos, sons of Sisera, sons of Thamah,

**2 Esdras 2:54**

sons of Nasoue, sons of Atoupha.

**2 Esdras 2:55**

Sons of Solomon's servants: sons of Sotai, sons of Sophereth, sons of Phadoura,

**2 Esdras 2:56**

sons of Iela, sons of Darkon, sons of Giddel,

**2 Esdras 2:57**

sons of Shephatiah, sons of Hattil, sons of Phacherath-aseboin, sons of Emi.

**2 Esdras 2:58**

All the nethinim and sons of Solomon's servants: 392.

**2 Esdras 2:59**

And these were the ones coming up from Telmelah, Telharsa, Cherub, Edan, and Immer, and they were not able to declare their father-house and their seed, whether they were from Israel:

**2 Esdras 2:60**

sons of Delaiah, sons of Boua, sons of Tobiah, sons of Nekoda: 652.

**2 Esdras 2:61**

And from the sons of the priests: sons of Habaiah, sons of Koz, sons of Barzillai, who took a wife from the daughters of Barzillai the Gileadite and was called by their name.

**2 Esdras 2:62**

These sought their enrolled writing, and it was not found, and they were excluded from the priesthood.

**2 Esdras 2:63**

And the Tirshatha said to them not to eat from the holy of holies until a priest might arise with the lights and the perfections.

**2 Esdras 2:64**

And all the assembly together was forty-two thousand three hundred sixty,

**2 Esdras 2:65**

besides their male slaves and female slaves; these were seven thousand three hundred thirty-seven, and they had two hundred singers and singing women.

**2 Esdras 2:66**

Their horses were 736, their mules 245,

**2 Esdras 2:67**

their camels 435, their donkeys 6,720.

**2 Esdras 2:68**

And some from the heads of father-houses, when they came to the house of the Lord in Jerusalem, made freewill offerings for the house of God, to set it on its foundation.

**2 Esdras 2:69**

According to their strength they gave into the treasury of the work 61,000 minas of pure gold, 5,000 minas of silver, and 100 priestly bowls.

**2 Esdras 2:70**

And the priests and Levites and some from the people and the singers and the gatekeepers and the nethinim settled in their cities, and all Israel in their cities.

### Chapter 3

**2 Esdras 3:1**

And the seventh month arrived, and sons of Israel were in their cities, and the people gathered as one man in Jerusalem.

**2 Esdras 3:2**

And Jeshua son of Jozadak rose up, and his brothers the priests, and Zerubbabel son of Shealtiel, and his brothers, and they built altar of God of Israel to offer whole burnt offerings on it, according to things written in the law of Moses man of God.

**2 Esdras 3:3**

And they prepared the altar on its place, because terror from the peoples of the lands was upon them, and they offered on it whole burnt offerings to the Lord, morning and evening.

**2 Esdras 3:4**

And they kept feast of booths according to what is written, and whole burnt offerings day by day in number, according to ordinance, matter of each day on its day.

**2 Esdras 3:5**

And after this came the continual whole burnt offerings, and those for the new moons, and for all the sanctified feasts, and for everyone freely offering a freewill gift to the Lord.

**2 Esdras 3:6**

On the first day of seventh month they began offering whole burnt offerings to the Lord, and house of the Lord had not yet been founded.

**2 Esdras 3:7**

And they gave silver to stonecutters and craftsmen, and food and drink and oil to Sidonians and Tyrians to bring cedar timbers from Lebanon to sea of Joppa, according to grant of Cyrus king of the Persians upon them.

**2 Esdras 3:8**

And in the second year of their coming to the house of God in Jerusalem, in the second month, Zerubbabel son of Shealtiel and Jeshua son of Jozadak began, along with the rest of their brothers, the priests and the Levites and all who came from captivity to Jerusalem, and they appointed Levites from twenty years old and upward over those doing the works in the house of the Lord.

**2 Esdras 3:9**

And Jeshua stood, and his sons and his brothers, Kadmiel and his sons, sons of Judah, over those doing the works in the house of God, the sons of Henadad, their sons and their brothers the Levites.

**2 Esdras 3:10**

And they laid foundations to build the house of the Lord, and the priests stood arrayed with trumpets and the Levites, sons of Asaph, with cymbals, to praise the Lord according to the ordinance of David king of Israel.

**2 Esdras 3:11**

And they answered with praise and thanksgiving to the Lord, For he is good, for his mercy upon Israel is forever. And all the people shouted with a loud voice in praising the Lord over foundation of the house of the Lord.

**2 Esdras 3:12**

And many from the priests and the Levites and leaders of father-houses, the elders who had seen the first house on its foundation, were weeping with a loud voice when this house was before their eyes, while the crowd with shouting in joy was raising song.

**2 Esdras 3:13**

And the people could not distinguish the sound of a shout of joy from the sound of weeping of the people, for the people cried out with a loud voice, and the sound was heard from far away.

### Chapter 4

**2 Esdras 4:1**

And those afflicting Judah and Benjamin heard that sons of the captivity are building a house to God of Israel.

**2 Esdras 4:2**

And they came near to Zerubbabel and to the heads of father-houses and said to them, We will build with you, because like you we seek your God, and to him we sacrifice from the days of Esarhaddon king of Asshur, who brought us up here.

**2 Esdras 4:3**

And Zerubbabel and Jeshua and the rest of the heads of father-houses of Israel said to them, It is not for us and you to build a house to our God, for we ourselves together will build to the Lord our God, as Cyrus king of the Persians commanded us.

**2 Esdras 4:4**

And the people of the land were weakening hands of the people of Judah and hindering them from building,

**2 Esdras 4:5**

and hiring counselors against them to scatter their counsel all the days of Cyrus king of the Persians and until reign of Darius king of the Persians.

**2 Esdras 4:6**

And in the reign of Ahasuerus, at the beginning of his reign, they wrote a letter against the inhabitants of Judah and Jerusalem.

**2 Esdras 4:7**

And in the days of Artaxerxes, Mithredath, Tabeel, together with the rest of his fellow-servants, wrote to Artaxerxes king of the Persians. The tax-writer wrote the document in Syrian, and it was translated.

**2 Esdras 4:8**

Rehum the chancellor and Shimshai the scribe wrote one letter against Jerusalem to King Artaxerxes,

**2 Esdras 4:9**

thus judged Rehum the chancellor and Shimshai the scribe and our other fellow-servants, the Dinaeans, the Apharsathchites, the Tarphelaeans, the Apharsaeans, the Archyaeans, the Babylonians, the Sousanchaeans, who are Elamites,

**2 Esdras 4:10**

and the rest of the nations whom Asnapper the great and honored deported and settled in the cities of Samaria and the rest beyond the River.

**2 Esdras 4:11**

This is the copy of the letter which they sent to him, to King Artaxerxes: Your servants, men beyond the River.

**2 Esdras 4:12**

Let it be known to the king that the Jews who came up from you to us have come to Jerusalem, the rebellious and wicked city. They are building it, and its walls are being made ready, and they raised up its foundations.

**2 Esdras 4:13**

Now then let it be known to the king that if that city is rebuilt and its walls completed, taxes will not belong to you, nor will they give tribute, and this harms kings.

**2 Esdras 4:14**

And it is not lawful for us to see shame of the king; because of this we sent and made it known to the king,

**2 Esdras 4:15**

so that he may inspect book of records of your fathers, and you will find and know that that city is a rebellious city, harming kings and lands, and that runaways of slaves were in its midst from ancient times. Because of these things that city was laid waste.

**2 Esdras 4:16**

Therefore we make known to the king that if that city is rebuilt and its walls completed, there will not be peace for you.

**2 Esdras 4:17**

And the king sent to Rehum the chancellor and Shimshai the scribe and the rest of their fellow-servants dwelling in Samaria and the rest beyond the River, Peace, and he says,

**2 Esdras 4:18**

The tax-document which you sent to us was read before me.

**2 Esdras 4:19**

And a decree from me was set down, and they searched and found that that city from ancient days rises up against kings, and rebellions and runaways happen in it.

**2 Esdras 4:20**

And strong kings were over Jerusalem, ruling all the west beyond the River, and full taxes and tribute were given to them.

**2 Esdras 4:21**

And now set decision to stop those men, and that city shall not be built again except by decree.

**2 Esdras 4:22**

And take care not to be negligent concerning this, lest destruction multiply to harm kings.

**2 Esdras 4:23**

Then tax-document of King Artaxerxes was read before Rehum and Shimshai the scribe and their fellow-servants, and they went in haste to Jerusalem and into Judah and stopped them with horses and force.

**2 Esdras 4:24**

Then work of the house of God in Jerusalem ceased, and it lay idle until the second year of the reign of Darius king of the Persians.

### Chapter 5

**2 Esdras 5:1**

And Haggai the prophet and Zechariah son of Iddo prophesied over the Jews in Judah and Jerusalem, in the name of God of Israel upon them.

**2 Esdras 5:2**

Then Zerubbabel son of Shealtiel and Jeshua son of Jozadak rose up and began to build the house of God in Jerusalem, and with them were prophets of God helping them.

**2 Esdras 5:3**

At that time Tatnai, governor beyond the River, and Shetharboznai and their fellow-servants came upon them and said these things to them: Who set a decree for you to build this house and complete this structure?

**2 Esdras 5:4**

Then they said these things to them: What are names of the men building this city?

**2 Esdras 5:5**

And the eyes of God were upon captivity of Judah, and they did not stop them until a report was carried to Darius and then an answer was sent to the tax-officer concerning this.

**2 Esdras 5:6**

Copy of letter which Tatnai governor beyond the River and Shetharboznai and their fellow-servants, the Apharsachites beyond the River, sent to King Darius:

**2 Esdras 5:7**

They sent a report to him, and these things were written in it: To King Darius, all peace.

**2 Esdras 5:8**

Let it be known to the king that we went into the land of Judah, to the house of the great God, and it is being built with choice stones, and timbers are laid in the walls, and that work is done skillfully and prospers in their hands.

**2 Esdras 5:9**

Then we asked those elders and said this to them: Who set a decree for you to build this house and complete this structure?

**2 Esdras 5:10**

And we also asked them their names, to make them known to you, so that we might write to you names of the men leading them.

**2 Esdras 5:11**

And this word they answered us, saying, We are servants of the God of heaven and earth, and we are building the house that was built many years before this, and a great king of Israel built it and completed it.

**2 Esdras 5:12**

But from time when our fathers provoked the God of heaven, he gave them into the hand of Nebuchadnezzar king of Babylon the Chaldean, and he destroyed this house and exiled the people to Babylon.

**2 Esdras 5:13**

But in the first year of King Cyrus, Cyrus the king set a decree for this house of God to be built.

**2 Esdras 5:14**

And vessels of the house of God, the gold and silver ones which Nebuchadnezzar took from the house in Jerusalem and carried into the temple of the king, King Cyrus took them out from the temple of the king and gave them to Sheshbazzar, the treasurer over the treasury.

**2 Esdras 5:15**

And he said to him, Take all the vessels and go, put them in the house in Jerusalem, each in its own place.

**2 Esdras 5:16**

Then that Sheshbazzar came and laid foundations of the house of God in Jerusalem, and from then until now it has been built and not completed.

**2 Esdras 5:17**

And now, if it seems good to the king, let search be made in the house of royal treasury in Babylon, that you may know whether decree was set from King Cyrus to build that house of God in Jerusalem, and after knowing concerning this let the king send to us.

### Chapter 6

**2 Esdras 6:1**

Then King Darius set a decree, and search was made in the libraries where the treasure is laid in Babylon.

**2 Esdras 6:2**

And in the city of Bareis, city of the Medes, one scroll was found, and this memorandum was written in it:

**2 Esdras 6:3**

In the first year of King Cyrus, Cyrus the king set a decree concerning the house of God in Jerusalem: Let the house be built, and the place where they sacrifice the offerings. And he set its height at sixty cubits and its breadth at sixty cubits,

**2 Esdras 6:4**

with three courses of massive stone and one course of timber, and expense shall be given from the house of the king.

**2 Esdras 6:5**

And silver and gold vessels of the house of God, which Nebuchadnezzar brought out from the house in Jerusalem and carried to Babylon, let them be given back and go to the temple in Jerusalem, to place where they were put, in the house of God.

**2 Esdras 6:6**

Now stay away from there, governors beyond the River, Shetharboznai and your fellow-servants, the Apharsachites beyond the River.

**2 Esdras 6:7**

Leave work of that house of God alone. Let leaders of the Jews and elders of the Jews build that house of God on its place.

**2 Esdras 6:8**

And from me decree has been set that you do nothing against elders of the Jews in building that house of God, and from possessions of the king, from taxes beyond the River, let expense be carefully given to those men so that it not stop.

**2 Esdras 6:9**

And whatever is lacking, young bulls and rams and lambs for whole burnt offerings to the God of heaven, wheat, salt, wine, and oil, according to the word of priests in Jerusalem, let it be given to them day by day, whatever they ask,

**2 Esdras 6:10**

so that they may be offering fragrant offerings to the God of heaven and praying for the life of the king and his sons.

**2 Esdras 6:11**

And from me decree has been set that every man who changes this word, a timber shall be pulled down from his house, and after being raised up he shall be impaled on it, and his house shall be made a ruin for this.

**2 Esdras 6:12**

And God, who causes name to dwell there, will overthrow every king and people who stretches out his hand to alter or destroy that house of God in Jerusalem. I, Darius, set a decree; it shall be done carefully.

**2 Esdras 6:13**

Then Tatnai, governor beyond the River, Shetharboznai, and their fellow-servants did carefully according to what Darius the king sent.

**2 Esdras 6:14**

And elders of the Jews were building and prospering through prophecy of Haggai the prophet and Zechariah son of Iddo. And they built and completed it by the decree of God of Israel and by the decree of Cyrus and Darius and Artaxerxes, kings of Persia.

**2 Esdras 6:15**

And they completed this house by third day of the month Adar, which was sixth year in the reign of Darius the king.

**2 Esdras 6:16**

And sons of Israel, the priests and the Levites and rest of sons of exile, kept dedication of the house of God with gladness.

**2 Esdras 6:17**

And they offered at the dedication of the house of God one hundred calves, two hundred rams, four hundred lambs, and twelve goat kids for sin concerning all Israel, according to the number of tribes of Israel.

**2 Esdras 6:18**

And they set priests in their divisions and Levites in their allotments for the service of God in Jerusalem, according to the writing of book of Moses.

**2 Esdras 6:19**

And the sons of the exile kept the Passover on fourteenth day of the first month.

**2 Esdras 6:20**

For the priests and the Levites had purified themselves until all of them together were pure, and they slaughtered the Passover for all sons of exile and for their brothers the priests and for themselves.

**2 Esdras 6:21**

And sons of Israel from the exile ate the Passover, and everyone separating himself from uncleanness of nations of the land to them, to seek the Lord God of Israel.

**2 Esdras 6:22**

And they kept feast of unleavened for seven days with gladness, because the Lord gladdened them and turned heart of the king of Asshur toward them, to strengthen their hands in the works of the house of God of Israel.

### Chapter 7

**2 Esdras 7:1**

And after these words, in the reign of Artaxerxes king of the Persians, Ezra son of Seraiah, son of Azariah, son of Hilkiah,

**2 Esdras 7:2**

son of Shallum, son of Zadok, son of Ahitub,

**2 Esdras 7:3**

son of Amariah, son of Azariah, son of Meraioth,

**2 Esdras 7:4**

son of Zerahiah, son of Saouia, son of Bukki,

**2 Esdras 7:5**

son of Abishua, son of Phinehas, son of Eleazar, son of Aaron the first priest.

**2 Esdras 7:6**

This Ezra came up from Babylon, and he was a quick scribe in the law of Moses, which the Lord God of Israel gave, and the king gave to him because the hand of the Lord his God was upon him in all he sought.

**2 Esdras 7:7**

And some from the sons of Israel and from the priests and from the Levites and the singers and the gatekeepers and the nethinim went up to Jerusalem in the seventh year of King Artaxerxes.

**2 Esdras 7:8**

And they came to Jerusalem in the fifth month; this was seventh year of the king.

**2 Esdras 7:9**

For on the first day of the first month he founded the going-up from Babylon, and on the first day of the fifth month they came to Jerusalem, because good hand of his God was upon him.

**2 Esdras 7:10**

For Ezra gave his heart to seek the law and to do it and to teach ordinances and judgments in Israel.

**2 Esdras 7:11**

And this is a copy of the decree which Artaxerxes gave to Ezra the priest, the scribe of book of words of commandments of the Lord and his ordinances for Israel:

**2 Esdras 7:12**

Artaxerxes, king of kings, to Ezra, scribe of the law of the God of heaven: the matter is settled, and this the answer.

**2 Esdras 7:13**

From me decree has been set that everyone in my kingdom who freely chooses from the people of Israel and priests and Levites to go to Jerusalem with you may go.

**2 Esdras 7:14**

Because you are sent from the presence of the king and the seven counselors to inspect concerning Judah and Jerusalem by law of your God in your hand,

**2 Esdras 7:15**

and to bring silver and gold which the king and his counselors freely gave to God of Israel who dwells in Jerusalem,

**2 Esdras 7:16**

and all the silver and gold whatever you find in the whole land of Babylon, together with the freewill offering of the people and priests who freely give for the house of God in Jerusalem,

**2 Esdras 7:17**

and whatever comes with this, promptly arrange in this writing: calves, rams, lambs, and their grain offerings and their drink offerings, and you shall offer them on the altar of the house of your God in Jerusalem.

**2 Esdras 7:18**

And whatever seems good to you and your brothers to do with rest of the silver and the gold, do as pleasing to your God.

**2 Esdras 7:19**

And vessels given to you for the service of the house of God, deliver before God in Jerusalem.

**2 Esdras 7:20**

And whatever remains of need for the house of your God, whatever seems to you to give, you shall give from houses of treasury of the king.

**2 Esdras 7:21**

And from me, I Artaxerxes the king, set a decree to all the treasuries beyond the River, that whatever Ezra the priest and scribe of the law of the God of heaven asks from you, let it be promptly done,

**2 Esdras 7:22**

up to one hundred talents of silver and up to one hundred kors of wheat and up to one hundred baths of wine and up to one hundred baths of oil, and salt without measure.

**2 Esdras 7:23**

Everything in decree of the God of heaven, let it be done carefully for the house of the God of heaven. Take care lest anyone attack it, lest wrath come upon the kingdom of the king and his sons.

**2 Esdras 7:24**

And it is made known to you that upon all the priests and Levites, singers, gatekeepers, nethinim, and servants of this house of God, tribute shall not be for you; you will have no authority to enslave them.

**2 Esdras 7:25**

And you, Ezra, according to wisdom of God in your hand, appoint scribes and judges, that they may be judging all the people beyond the River, all knowing law of your God, and you shall make it known to those not knowing.

**2 Esdras 7:26**

And everyone who is not doing the law of your God and the law of the king, judgment shall promptly be carried out from him, whether to death or to discipline or to confiscation of substance or to bonds.

**2 Esdras 7:27**

Blessed is the Lord God of our fathers, who gave thus into the heart of the king to glorify house of the Lord in Jerusalem,

**2 Esdras 7:28**

and bent mercy toward me before the king and his counselors and all the exalted rulers of the king. And I was strengthened, as the good hand of God was upon me, and I gathered leaders from Israel to go up with me.

### Chapter 8

**2 Esdras 8:1**

And these were chiefs of their father-houses, leaders going up with me in the reign of Artaxerxes, king of Babylon:

**2 Esdras 8:2**

from the sons of Phinehas, Gershom; from the sons of Ithamar, Daniel; from the sons of David, Hattush;

**2 Esdras 8:3**

from the sons of Sechanias; from the sons of Parosh, Zechariah, and with him a band of one hundred fifty males;

**2 Esdras 8:4**

from the sons of Pahathmoab, Elianas son of Zerahiah, and with him two hundred males;

**2 Esdras 8:5**

from the sons of Zathoes, Shechaniah son of Aziel, and with him three hundred males;

**2 Esdras 8:6**

and from the sons of Adin, Obeth son of Jonathan, and with him fifty males;

**2 Esdras 8:7**

and from the sons of Elam, Jeshaiah son of Athaliah, and with him seventy males;

**2 Esdras 8:8**

and from the sons of Shephatiah, Zebadiah son of Michael, and with him eighty males;

**2 Esdras 8:9**

and from the sons of Joab, Obadiah son of Jehiel, and with him two hundred eighteen males;

**2 Esdras 8:10**

and from the sons of Baani, Salimoth son of Iosephia, and with him one hundred sixty males;

**2 Esdras 8:11**

and from the sons of Bebai, Zechariah son of Bebai, and with him seventy-eight males;

**2 Esdras 8:12**

and from the sons of Azgad, Johanan son of Hakkatan, and with him one hundred ten males;

**2 Esdras 8:13**

and from the sons of Adonikam, the last, and these their names: Eliphalat, Jehiel, and Shemaiah, and with them sixty males;

**2 Esdras 8:14**

and from the sons of Bigvai, Uthai, and with him seventy males.

**2 Esdras 8:15**

And I gathered them to the river coming to Ahava, and we camped there three days, and I discerned among the people and the priests, but from the sons of Levi I found no one there.

**2 Esdras 8:16**

And I sent for Eleazar, Ariel, Shemaiah, Alonam, Jarib, Elnathan, Nathan, Zechariah, and Meshullam, men, and for Ioarib and Elnathan, those with understanding.

**2 Esdras 8:17**

And I sent them out to the leader at Casiphia the place, and I put words in their mouth to speak to their brothers the nethinim at Casiphia the place, to bring us singers for the house of our God.

**2 Esdras 8:18**

And they came to us, because good hand of our God was upon us, a man, Mahli, from the sons of Mahali son of Levi son of Israel, and his sons and his brothers, eighteen.

**2 Esdras 8:19**

And Hashabiah and Jeshaiah from the sons of Merari, his brothers and their sons, twenty.

**2 Esdras 8:20**

And from the nethinim, whom David and the rulers gave for the service of the Levites, two hundred twenty nethinim. All were enrolled by names.

**2 Esdras 8:21**

And I proclaimed a fast there at river Ahava, to humble ourselves before our God, to seek from him a straight way for us and for our children and for all our possessions.

**2 Esdras 8:22**

For I was ashamed to ask from the king force and horsemen to save us from enemy on the road, because we had spoken to the king, saying, The hand of our God is upon all seeking him for good, but his might and his wrath are upon all forsaking him.

**2 Esdras 8:23**

And we fasted and sought from our God concerning this, and he listened to us.

**2 Esdras 8:24**

And I separated twelve from the chiefs of the priests: Seraiah, Hashabiah, and with them ten from their brothers.

**2 Esdras 8:25**

And I weighed out to them the silver and the gold and the vessels, the firstfruits of the house of our God, which the king and his counselors and his rulers and all Israel found there had lifted up.

**2 Esdras 8:26**

And I weighed into their hands six hundred fifty talents of silver and one hundred silver vessels and one hundred talents of gold,

**2 Esdras 8:27**

and twenty golden bowls worth one thousand chamanim, and vessels of shining good bronze, choice as gold.

**2 Esdras 8:28**

And I said to them, You are holy to the Lord, and the vessels are holy, and the silver and gold are a freewill offering to the Lord God of your fathers.

**2 Esdras 8:29**

Stay awake and guard them until you stand before chiefs of the priests and the Levites and chiefs of father-houses in Jerusalem, in the chambers of the house of the Lord.

**2 Esdras 8:30**

And the priests and the Levites received the weight of the silver and the gold and the vessels, to bring them to Jerusalem into the house of our God.

**2 Esdras 8:31**

And we set out from the river Ahava on twelfth day of the first month to come to Jerusalem, and hand of our God was upon us, and he rescued us from the hand of enemy and foe on the road.

**2 Esdras 8:32**

And we came to Jerusalem and sat there three days.

**2 Esdras 8:33**

And it happened on fourth day that we weighed the silver and the gold and the vessels in the house of our God by the hand of Meremoth son of Uriah the priest, and with him Eleazar son of Phinehas, and with them Jozabad son of Jeshua and Noadiah son of Benaiah, the Levites,

**2 Esdras 8:34**

by number and by weight, everything, and all the weight was written at that time.

**2 Esdras 8:35**

Those coming from captivity, sons of exile, offered whole burnt offerings to God of Israel: twelve calves for all Israel, ninety-six rams, seventy-seven lambs, twelve goat kids for sin, all as whole burnt offerings to the Lord.

**2 Esdras 8:36**

And they gave king's decree to the king's administrators and governors beyond the River, and they honored the people and the house of God.

### Chapter 9

**2 Esdras 9:1**

And when these things were finished, the rulers came near to me saying, People of Israel and the priests and the Levites have not separated themselves from the peoples of the lands in their abominations, from the Canaanite, Hittite, Perizzite, Jebusite, Ammonite, Moabite, Moserite, and Amorite.

**2 Esdras 9:2**

For they took from their daughters for themselves and for their sons, and the holy seed was mixed with the peoples of the lands, and hand of the rulers was first in this faithlessness.

**2 Esdras 9:3**

And when I heard this word, I tore my garments and mantle and plucked hair from my head and from my beard and sat devastated.

**2 Esdras 9:4**

And everyone pursuing word of God of Israel was gathered to me over the faithlessness of the exile, and I sat devastated until evening sacrifice.

**2 Esdras 9:5**

And at evening sacrifice I rose from my humbling, and as I tore my garments and mantle I bent upon my knees and spread out my hands to the Lord God

**2 Esdras 9:6**

and said, Lord, I was ashamed and embarrassed to lift my face to you, because our lawlessnesses multiplied above our head and our trespasses grew great unto heaven.

**2 Esdras 9:7**

From the days of our fathers we have been in great trespass until this day, and in our lawlessnesses we and our kings and our sons were given into the hand of kings of the nations, to sword and captivity and plundering and shame of face, as this day.

**2 Esdras 9:8**

And now the Lord our God dealt gently with us, to leave us a remnant for salvation and give us a foothold in place of his sanctuary, to enlighten our eyes and give us a little reviving in our slavery.

**2 Esdras 9:9**

For slaves we are, yet in our slavery the Lord our God did not forsake us, and he bent mercy upon us before kings of Persia, to give us reviving, to raise up house of our God and restore its desolate places and give us a wall in Judah and in Jerusalem.

**2 Esdras 9:10**

And what shall we say, our God, after this? For we forsook your commandments,

**2 Esdras 9:11**

which you gave by the hand of your servants the prophets, saying, The land into which you enter to inherit it is a land made unclean in uncleanness of peoples of the nations, in their abominations with which they filled it from one end to the other with their uncleannesses.

**2 Esdras 9:12**

And now do not give your daughters to their sons, and do not take from their daughters for your sons, and do not seek their peace and their good forever, so that you may grow strong and eat good things of the land and leave it as inheritance to your sons forever.

**2 Esdras 9:13**

And after all that came upon us for our evil deeds and our great trespass, since you, our God, lightened from our lawlessnesses and gave us such deliverance,

**2 Esdras 9:14**

shall we turn again to scatter your commandments and intermarry with the peoples of the lands? Do not be angry with us until completion, so that there be no remnant and no one escaping.

**2 Esdras 9:15**

Lord God of Israel, righteous are you, because we were left escaping as this day. Behold, we are before you in our trespasses, because there is no standing before you on account of this.

### Chapter 10

**2 Esdras 10:1**

And as Ezra prayed and confessed, weeping and praying before the house of God, a very great assembly from Israel was gathered to him, men and women and young people, because the people wept and lifted up weeping.

**2 Esdras 10:2**

And Shechaniah son of Jehiel, from the sons of Elam, answered and said to Ezra, We acted faithlessly against our God and seated foreign women from the peoples of the land, and now there is patient hope for Israel about this.

**2 Esdras 10:3**

And now let us make a covenant with our God to put out all the women and those born from them, according to your counsel. Rise and warn them in the commandments of our God, and let it be according to law.

**2 Esdras 10:4**

Rise, because the matter is on you, and we are with you. Be strong and do it.

**2 Esdras 10:5**

And Ezra rose and made the rulers, the priests, and the Levites and all Israel swear to do according to this word, and they swore.

**2 Esdras 10:6**

And Ezra rose from before the house of God and went into the treasury-room of Johanan son of Eliashib, and he went there. He ate no bread and drank no water, because he mourned over the faithlessness of the exile.

**2 Esdras 10:7**

And they proclaimed through Judah and Jerusalem to all the sons of the exile to gather into Jerusalem.

**2 Esdras 10:8**

And everyone who would not come within three days, according to the counsel of the rulers and the elders, all his substance would be devoted, and he himself would be separated from assembly of the exile.

**2 Esdras 10:9**

And all the men of Judah and Benjamin gathered into Jerusalem within three days. This was the ninth month, on twentieth day of the month. And all the people sat in the open square of the house of God, trembling because of the matter and because of the winter.

**2 Esdras 10:10**

And Ezra the priest stood and said to them, You have acted faithlessly and seated foreign women, to add to trespass of Israel.

**2 Esdras 10:11**

And now give praise to the Lord God of your fathers and do what is pleasing before him, and separate yourselves from the peoples of the land and from the foreign women.

**2 Esdras 10:12**

And all the assembly answered and said, Great is this word of yours upon us to do.

**2 Esdras 10:13**

But the people are many, and the season is rainy, and there is no strength to stand outside, and the work is not for one day and not for two, because we multiplied wrongdoing in this matter.

**2 Esdras 10:14**

Let our rulers stand for the whole assembly, and all in our cities who seated foreign women, let them come at appointed times, and with them elders of the city by city and judges, to turn away anger of wrath of our God from us concerning this matter.

**2 Esdras 10:15**

Only Jonathan son of Asahel and Jahaziah son of Tikvah stood for this, and Meshullam and Shabbethai the Levite were helping them.

**2 Esdras 10:16**

And the sons of the exile did thus, and Ezra the priest and men, leaders of father-houses by house, all by names, were set apart, and they sat on the first day of the tenth month to investigate the matter.

**2 Esdras 10:17**

And they finished with all the men who had seated foreign women by first day of the first month.

**2 Esdras 10:18**

And from the sons of the priests who had seated foreign women were found these: from the sons of Jeshua son of Jozadak and his brothers, Maaseiah and Eliezer and Jarib and Gedaliah.

**2 Esdras 10:19**

And they gave their hands to put out their wives, and for their trespass a ram from the sheep for their trespass.

**2 Esdras 10:20**

And from the sons of Immer: Anani and Zebadiah.

**2 Esdras 10:21**

And from the sons of Harim: Maaseiah and Eliah and Shemaiah and Jehiel and Uzziah.

**2 Esdras 10:22**

And from the sons of Pashur: Elioenai, Maaseiah, Ishmael, Nathanael, Jozabad, and Elasah.

**2 Esdras 10:23**

And from the Levites: Jozabad and Samou and Kelaiah, he is Kolitas, and Pethahiah and Iodom and Eliezer.

**2 Esdras 10:24**

And from the singers: Eliasaph. And from the gatekeepers: Shillem and Telem and Odoue.

**2 Esdras 10:25**

And from Israel, from the sons of Parosh: Ramiah and Iazia and Malchijah and Mijamin and Eleazar and Hashabiah and Benaiah.

**2 Esdras 10:26**

And from the sons of Elam: Mattaniah and Zechariah and Jehiel and Obadiah and Jeremoth and Eliah.

**2 Esdras 10:27**

And from the sons of Zattu: Elioenai, Eliashib, Mattaniah, Jeremoth, Zabad, and Oziza.

**2 Esdras 10:28**

And from the sons of Bebai: Johanan, Hananiah, Zabbai, Athlai.

**2 Esdras 10:29**

And from the sons of Bani: Meshullam, Malluch, Adaiah, Jashub, Salouia, and Ramoth.

**2 Esdras 10:30**

And from the sons of Pahathmoab: Edna, Chelal, Benaiah, Maaseiah, Mattaniah, Bezaleel, Bani, and Manasseh.

**2 Esdras 10:31**

And from the sons of Harim: Eliezer, Iessia, Malchijah, Shemaiah, Shimeon,

**2 Esdras 10:32**

Benjamin, Malluch, Samaria.

**2 Esdras 10:33**

And from the sons of Hashum: Mattenai, Mathatha, Zabad, Eliphaleph, Jeremai, Manasseh, Shimei.

**2 Esdras 10:34**

From the sons of Bani: Maadai, Amram, Uel,

**2 Esdras 10:35**

Benaiah, Bedeiah, Chelluh,

**2 Esdras 10:36**

Ouechoa, Meremoth, Eliashib,

**2 Esdras 10:37**

Mattaniah, Mathanai, and they made.

**2 Esdras 10:38**

The sons of Bani and the sons of Shimei,

**2 Esdras 10:39**

and Shelemiah and Nathan and Adaiah,

**2 Esdras 10:40**

Machnadebai, Sese, Sharai,

**2 Esdras 10:41**

Azareel and Shelemiah and Samaria,

**2 Esdras 10:42**

and Shallum, Amariah, Joseph.

**2 Esdras 10:43**

From the sons of Nebo: Jehiel, Mattithiah, Zedem, Zebina, Jahdai, Joel, and Benaiah.

**2 Esdras 10:44**

All these took foreign women and begot sons from them.

## Prayer of Manasseh

### Chapter 1

**Prayer of Manasseh 1:1**

Lord Almighty, the God of our fathers, of Abraham and Isaac and Jacob and of their righteous seed.

**Prayer of Manasseh 1:2**

who made heaven and earth with all their ordered array.

**Prayer of Manasseh 1:3**

who bound the sea by the word of your command, who shut the abyss and sealed it with your fearful and glorious name;

**Prayer of Manasseh 1:4**

before whom all things shudder and tremble from the face of your power,

**Prayer of Manasseh 1:5**

because the magnificence of your glory is unendurable, and the wrath of your threat upon sinners is irresistible;

**Prayer of Manasseh 1:6**

and the mercy of your promise is unmeasured and unsearchable;

**Prayer of Manasseh 1:7**

for you are Lord Most High, compassionate, longsuffering, and very merciful, relenting over human evils. You, Lord, according to the multitude of your kindness, promised repentance and forgiveness to those who have sinned against you, and by the multitude of your compassions appointed repentance for sinners unto salvation.

**Prayer of Manasseh 1:8**

Therefore you, Lord, God of the righteous, did not appoint repentance for the righteous, for Abraham and Isaac and Jacob, those who did not sin against you,

**Prayer of Manasseh 1:9**

My lawless deeds multiplied, Lord, they multiplied, and I am not worthy to gaze and see the height of heaven because of the multitude of my unrighteous deeds,

**Prayer of Manasseh 1:10**

being bent down by a heavy iron bond, so as not to lift up my head, and there is no relief for me, because I provoked your wrath and did evil before you, not doing your will and not keeping your commandments, setting up abominations and multiplying provocations.

**Prayer of Manasseh 1:11**

And now I bend the knee of my heart, asking for the kindness from you: I have sinned,

**Prayer of Manasseh 1:12**

Lord, I have sinned, and I know my lawless deeds. But I ask, begging you: forgive me,

**Prayer of Manasseh 1:13**

Lord, forgive me, and do not destroy me together with my lawless deeds, nor, being angry forever, keep my evils, nor condemn me in the lowest parts of the earth, because you are God, the God of those who repent.

**Prayer of Manasseh 1:14**

And in me you will show all your goodness, because, though I am unworthy, you will save me according to your abundant mercy.

**Prayer of Manasseh 1:15**

And I will praise you continually in the days of my life, because all the power of the heavens hymns you, and yours is the glory into the ages. Amen.

## 3 Maccabees

### Chapter 1

**3 Maccabees 1:1**

But Philopator, having learned from those brought back of the seizure by Antiochus of the places held under him, commanded all his forces, both foot and horse, and taking along his sister Arsinoe, set out as far as the places around Raphia, where those around Antiochus had camped.

**3 Maccabees 1:2**

And a certain Theodotus, intending to complete the plot, took the best of the Ptolemaic weapons previously assigned to him and was carried by night to the tent of Ptolemy, as though to kill him alone and in this way dissolve the war.

**3 Maccabees 1:3**

But Dositheus, called son of Drimylus, a Jew by race, but afterward having changed the customs and been alienated from the ancestral decrees, led this man through and laid down a certain obscure man in the tent, who happened to receive that one's punishment.

**3 Maccabees 1:4**

And when a hard battle came to be and matters were rather strengthened for Antiochus, Arsinoe sufficiently went among the forces and exhorted them, with pity and tears, her locks loosed, to help themselves and their children and wives courageously, promising to give each who conquered two minas of gold.

**3 Maccabees 1:5**

And so it happened that the opponents were destroyed in hand-to-hand fighting, and many also were seized as spear-captives.

**3 Maccabees 1:6**

And having taken control of the plot, he judged to go upon the nearby cities and encourage them.

**3 Maccabees 1:7**

And having done this and assigned gifts to the sacred precincts, he made those subject to him bold.

**3 Maccabees 1:8**

But when the Jews sent to him from the senate and the elders those who would greet him and bring guest-gifts and rejoice with him over the things that had happened, it came about that he was all the more eager to come to them as quickly as possible.

**3 Maccabees 1:9**

And being brought into Jerusalem and sacrificing to the greatest God and giving thanks and doing something of the following for the place, and indeed coming to the place and being struck by its earnestness and good order,

**3 Maccabees 1:10**

and marveling also at the good arrangement of the temple, he considered taking counsel to enter into the shrine.

**3 Maccabees 1:11**

But when they said that this was not fitting to happen, because not even those from the nation were permitted to enter, nor all the priests, but only the high priest leading all, and he once each year, he in no way wished to be persuaded.

**3 Maccabees 1:12**

And when the law had been read beside him, he in no way left off, putting himself forward and saying that he must enter; and if they have been deprived of this honor, I must not be.

**3 Maccabees 1:13**

And he inquired for what reason, when he entered every sacred precinct, none of those present had hindered him.

**3 Maccabees 1:14**

And someone thoughtlessly said that this very thing was wrongly treated as a sign.

**3 Maccabees 1:15**

But since this has happened for some reason, he says, shall I not surely enter, whether they wish it or not?

**3 Maccabees 1:16**

And when the priests in the holy garments fell forward and begged the greatest God to help the present necessity and turn aside the rush of the one making an evil attack, and filled the temple with crying and tears,

**3 Maccabees 1:17**

those left in the city were disturbed and rushed out, making unclear what was happening.

**3 Maccabees 1:18**

And the shut-in virgins in the chambers rushed out with those who had borne them, and after sprinkling their heads with ash and dust, they filled the streets with wailings and groans.

**3 Maccabees 1:19**

And those newly adorned, leaving the bridal rooms arranged for meeting and the modesty that fitted them, made a disorderly run in the city.

**3 Maccabees 1:20**

And the mothers and nurses left the newborn children here and there, some in houses and some along the streets, and gathered without turning back to the temple above all.

**3 Maccabees 1:21**

And varied was the supplication of those gathered for this, over the impious things being attempted by him.

**3 Maccabees 1:22**

Together with these, the citizens who had grown bold could not endure him pressing on completely and intending to fulfill his purpose.

**3 Maccabees 1:23**

And crying out to make a rush to arms and to die courageously for the ancestral law, they made considerable harshness in the place; but with difficulty, being turned back by the elderly and the elders, they stood in the same posture of supplication.

**3 Maccabees 1:24**

And the multitude, as before, was moving about in these things, begging.

**3 Maccabees 1:25**

But the elders around the king tried in many ways to remove his arrogant mind from the counsel he had considered.

**3 Maccabees 1:26**

But being emboldened and dismissing everything, he was already making an approach, thinking to put an end to what had been said before.

**3 Maccabees 1:27**

Therefore those who were around him, seeing these things, turned to call upon the One having all power, together with our people, to help in the present things, not overlooking the lawless and over-proud act.

**3 Maccabees 1:28**

And from the most dense and painful cry of the crowds being gathered, there was a shout beyond comparing.

**3 Maccabees 1:29**

For it seemed that not only the humans, but also the walls and the whole ground were resounding, since all then were exchanging death instead of the profaning of the place.

### Chapter 2

**3 Maccabees 2:1**

Therefore Simon the high priest, opposite the shrine, bending his knees and stretching out his hands in good order, made such a petition.

**3 Maccabees 2:2**

Lord, Lord, king of the heavens and master of all creation, holy in holies, sole ruler, almighty, attend to us being worn down by an unholy and profane one puffed up with insolence and strength.

**3 Maccabees 2:3**

For you, the one who created all things and rules over the whole, are a righteous ruler and judge those who act in insult and arrogance.

**3 Maccabees 2:4**

You destroyed those before who did unrighteousness, among whom also were giants trusting in might and insolence, bringing upon them immeasurable water.

**3 Maccabees 2:5**

You burned up with fire and sulfur the Sodomites, who worked pride and became manifest in evils, setting them as an example to those who would come after.

**3 Maccabees 2:6**

You tested the insolent Pharaoh, who enslaved your holy people Israel, with various and many punishments and made known your dominion, by which you made known your great might.

**3 Maccabees 2:7**

And when he pursued with chariots and a multitude of crowds, you flooded him with the depth of the sea, but those trusting in you, the one ruling all creation, you brought through safe,

**3 Maccabees 2:8**

and they, seeing together the works of your hand, praised you, the Almighty.

**3 Maccabees 2:9**

You, King, having created the boundless and immeasurable earth, chose this city and sanctified this place for your name, you who need nothing of all things, and glorified it beyond measure with magnificent appearing, making its establishment for the glory of your great and honorable name.

**3 Maccabees 2:10**

And loving the house of Israel, you promised indeed that if our turning away came to be and distress overtook us, and we came into this place and made petition, you would hear our petition.

**3 Maccabees 2:11**

And indeed you are faithful and true.

**3 Maccabees 2:12**
